We now express this inequality entirely in terms of the scale-invariant norms. Using (
3.43
), we write:

\displaystyle\frac{a^{s_{2}}}{|u|^{2s_{2}+\frac{1}{2}}}\|\varphi\|_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{s_{2}}}{|u_{\infty}|^{2s_{2}+\frac{1}{2}}}\|\varphi\|_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2(s_{2}+1)}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4(s_{2}+1)}}\|\not{\nabla}_{3}\varphi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\times\left[\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2s_{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4s_{2}+2}}\|\varphi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}\right)^{1/4}+\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2(s_{2}+\frac{1}{2})}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4(s_{2}+\frac{1}{2})}}\|\not{\nabla}\varphi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}\right)^{1/4}\right].

(3.46)

Multiplying both sides by
|u|^{2s_{2}+\frac{1}{2}}a^{-s_{2}}
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\|\varphi\|_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|u|^{2s_{2}+\frac{1}{2}}}{|u_{\infty}|^{2s_{2}+\frac{1}{2}}}\|\varphi\|_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+a^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\|\not{\nabla}_{3}\varphi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\frac{|u|^{4s_{2}+2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4s_{2}+2}}\,du^{\prime}\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\times\left[a^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\|\varphi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\frac{|u|^{4s_{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4s_{2}}}\,du^{\prime}\right)^{1/4}+\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\|\not{\nabla}\varphi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\frac{|u|^{4s_{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4s_{2}}}\,du^{\prime}\right)^{1/4}\right].

(3.47)

Now, observe that for all
u^{\prime}\in[u_{\infty},u]
, we have the inequality

\frac{|u|}{|u^{\prime}|}\leq 1,

which implies

\frac{|u|^{4s_{2}+2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4s_{2}+2}}\leq 1,\quad\frac{|u|^{4s_{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4s_{2}}}\leq 1.

Thus, all the multiplicative factors involving
\frac{|u|}{|u^{\prime}|}
are bounded above by
1
, and we conclude:

\displaystyle||\varphi||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim||\varphi||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+a^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\not{\nabla}_{3}\varphi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\frac{|u|^{4s_{2}+2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4s_{2}+2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}

(3.48)

\displaystyle\left[a^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\frac{|u|^{4s_{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4s_{2}}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\not{\nabla}\varphi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\frac{|u|^{4s_{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4s_{2}}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\right].

as claimed.

\square

Proposition 3.11

Let the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
hold, and suppose the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
) are in force. Let
\varphi
be a scalar (or tensorial) function defined on the domain of dependence associated with the double null foliation, and suppose that
\varphi
is sufficiently regular so that the quantities below are well-defined. The following codimension 1 trace inequality holds

\displaystyle||\varphi||_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim||\varphi||_{L^{4}(S_{u,0})}+||\not{\nabla}_{4}\varphi||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}(H)}\left(|u|^{-\frac{1}{2}}||\varphi||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}(H)}+||\not{\nabla}\varphi||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}(H)}\right)

(3.49)

Proof.

Exactly similar only we integrate in the
e_{4}
direction.

\square

The following scale-invariant version holds

Proposition 3.12

Let the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
hold, and suppose the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
) are in force. Let
\varphi
be a scalar (or tensorial) function defined on the domain of dependence associated with the double null foliation, and suppose that
\varphi
is sufficiently regular so that the quantities below are well-defined. The following scale-invariant trace inequality holds

\displaystyle||\varphi||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim||\varphi||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}+||\not{\nabla}_{4}\varphi||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(H)}\left(|||\varphi||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(H)}+||a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\varphi||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(H)}\right)

(3.50)

3.4
Sobolev embedding

With the derived estimates for the metric
\gamma
, we can obtain a bound on the isoperimetric constant for a topological
2-
sphere
S
:

\displaystyle I(S):=\sup_{U\subset S,~\partial U\in C^{1}}\frac{\min\left\{Area(U),Area(U^{c})\right\}}{[Perimeter(\partial U)]^{2}}.

(3.51)

The following proposition yields an upper bound for
I(S)
.

Proposition 3.13

Under the assumption on the initial data and the bootstrap assumption (2.10), the isoperimetric constant obeys the following bound

\displaystyle I(S_{u,\underline{u}})\leq\frac{1}{\pi}

(3.52)

for
u\in[u_{\infty},-\frac{a}{4}]
and
\underline{u}\in[0,1]
.

Proof.
Fix a
u
. For
U_{\underline{u}}
a subset of
S_{u,\underline{u}}
, denote by
U_{0}\subset S_{u,0}
the backward image of
U_{\underline{u}}
under the diffeomorphism generated by the equivariant vector field
L.
Using Propositions
3.3
and
3.4
and their proof, we can obtain the estimates

\frac{\text{Perimeter}(\partial U_{\underline{u}})}{\text{Perimeter}(\partial U_{0})}\geq\sqrt{\inf_{S_{u,0}}\lambda(\underline{u})}

and

\frac{\text{Area}(U_{\underline{u}})}{\text{Area}(U_{0})}\leq\sup_{S_{u,0}}\frac{\det(\gamma_{\underline{u}})}{\det(\gamma_{0})},\hskip 5.69054pt\frac{\text{Area}(U_{\underline{u}}^{c})}{\text{Area}(U_{0}^{c})}\leq\sup_{S_{u,0}}\frac{\det(\gamma_{\underline{u}})}{\det(\gamma_{0})}.

The conclusion then follows from the fact that
I(S_{u,0})=\frac{1}{2\pi}
and the bounds from Propositions
3.3
,
3.4
, and
3.2
.
\square

Throughout this work, we will be using an
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
Sobolev estimate. To obtain it, utilizing the basic estimates above, we may proceed to write down the following gauge-invariant Sobolev inequalities for the topological
2-
sphere
S
.

Proposition 3.14

Let
(S,\gamma)
be a compact Riemannian
2
–manifold with isoperimetric constant
I(S)
and area
Area(S)
. For any tensor field
\mathcal{G}\in\Gamma(\,^{N}\!\otimes T^{*}S)
and any
p\in(2,\infty)
, one has

Area(S)^{-\frac{1}{p}}\|\mathcal{G}\|_{L^{p}(S)}\;\leq\;C_{p}\,\big(\max\{1,I(S)\}\big)^{\tfrac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla\mathcal{G}\|_{L^{2}(S)}+Area(S)^{-\tfrac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{G}\|_{L^{2}(S)}\right),

(3.53)

where
C_{p}
depends only on
p
.

Proposition 3.15

Let
(S,\gamma)
be a Riemannian
2-
manifold with the isoperimetric constant
I(S)
. Then the following Sobolev embedding holds for any
\mathcal{G}\in\Gamma(^{N}\otimes T^{*}S)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq C_{p}\left(\max(1,I(S))\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}[Area(S)]^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\left(||\nabla\mathcal{G}||_{L^{p}(S)}+Area(S)^{-\frac{1}{2}}||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{p}(S)}\right)

(3.54)

for any
p\in(2,\infty)
.

Proof.
A calculation similar to the previous one and the standard
L^{\infty}-L^{p}
Sobolev inequality on the Riemannian manifold
(S,\gamma)
yield the result.
\square

The two inequalities above, together with Propositions
3.3
-
3.4
, allow us to control the
L^{2}
-norm of
\mathcal{G}
in terms of its
H^{2}
-norm. Following the area estimates, we have
Area(S_{u,\underline{u}})\approx u^{2}
. Therefore, we obtain the following important inequality.

Proposition 3.16

Let the hypotheses of Theorem
1.2
hold and assume the bootstrap bounds (
3.1
).
Assume moreover that, for each
(u,\underline{u})
in the region under consideration, the
2
–surface

S_{u,\underline{u}}
is diffeomorphic to
S^{2}
and its induced metric
\not{g}
satisfies the uniform comparison from propositions
3.3
,
3.4
, and
3.2
.
Under these hypotheses, for every tensor field

\mathcal{G}\in\Gamma(^{N}\otimes T^{*}S_{u,\underline{u}})
the following holds:

\|\mathcal{G}\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{I=0}^{2}\big\||u|^{\,I-1}\,\nabla^{\,I}\mathcal{G}\big\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(3.55)

Equivalently, in the scale–invariant norms used in the paper,

\|\mathcal{G}\|_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{I=0}^{2}\big\|(a^{1/2}\nabla)^{I}\mathcal{G}\big\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(3.56)

Proof.
Substitute
p=4
in the previous proposition (
3.54
) and estimate the right hand side by means of (
3.53
), the estimate on the area given by Proposition
3.4
and on the isoperimetric constant of
S_{u,\underline{u}}
given by Proposition
3.52
.
\square

3.5
The schematic form of the Equations and Commutation

Let us restate that, in order to obtain the desired Theorem
1.2
it is vital that higher derivatives of both Ricci coefficients as well as curvature components be estimated in
L^{2}
. In order to deal with the nonlinearities manifesting in the equations, certain error terms are estimated in
L^{\infty}
on the spheres. These
L^{\infty}
bounds themselves are obtained not through a direct bootstrap argument, but rather through
L^{2}
or
L^{4}
estimates for higher derivatives, using Sobolev inequalities. These
higher order estimates are obtained through commuting the null structure and Bianchi equations respectively with suitable differential operators. Let us mention here the important fact that the Vlasov matter system necessitates that these differential operators are more than one. For example, in
[
4
]
or the more recent
[
8
]
, only the angular derivative
\not{\nabla}
was required. However, in our case, control of the Vlasov matter necessitates control on
Vf
and its derivatives
VVf
and so on, where the
V^{\prime}
s are appropriately chosen vector fields (chosen so as to remain essentially constant under the evolution of the geodesic flow). The very definition of these vector fields implies that estimates on
VVf,VVVf
necessitate control on
\mathcal{D}(\psi,\Psi)
and
\mathcal{D}^{2}(\psi,\Psi)
, where
\mathcal{D}
now denotes not only an angular derivative, but rather any of a triple of differential operators in the set

S_{\mathcal{D}}:=\{\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4},a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\}.

Notice, of course, that each of the operators
\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4},\not{\nabla}
could be separately multiplied by a different function, giving rise to another set
S^{\prime}_{\mathcal{D}}
. However, the set
S_{\mathcal{D}}
is so defined such that an arbitrary
\mathcal{D}\in S_{\mathcal{D}}
should preserve the rate of decay of any tensorfield that it is applied to. This is summarized in the informal:

Commutation Principle: Applying an arbitrary operator
\mathcal{D}
to any of the
\Gamma,\Psi,\mathcal{T}
should not alter their respective rate of decay.

See also
[
17
]
and
[
27
]
. For an
S_{u,\underline{u}}
-tensor field
\varphi
, let
\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi
denote any fixed
k-
tuple
\mathcal{D}_{k}\mathcal{D}_{k-1}\dots\mathcal{D}_{1}
applied to
\varphi
, where each
\mathcal{D}_{i}\in S_{\mathcal{D}}
. The following commutation lemmata will be used to derive expressions for the commuted structure/Bianchi equations in the schematic notation.

Lemma 3.1

If
\xi
is a
(0,k)

S_{u,\underline{u}}-
tensor, then

\displaystyle(\not{\nabla}_{3}\xi)_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}=e_{3}(\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}})-\sum_{i=1}^{k}\big({\underline{\chi}_{A_{i}}}^{B}-\not{\nabla}_{A}b^{B}+b^{C}\not{\Gamma}^{B}_{AC}\big)\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{i-1}BA_{i+1}\dots A_{k}},

\displaystyle(\not{\nabla}_{4}\xi)_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}=e_{4}(\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}})-\sum_{i=1}^{k}{\chi_{A_{i}}}^{B}\hskip 1.42262pt\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{i-1}BA_{i+1}\dots A_{k}},

\displaystyle(\not{\nabla}_{B}\xi)_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}=e_{B}(\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}})-\sum_{i=1}^{k}\not{\Gamma}^{C}_{BA_{i}}\hskip 1.42262pt\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{i-1}BA_{i+1}\dots A_{k}}.

Proof.

This follows from the definition of how a covariant derivative acts on a tensor field, after taking into account the table (
2.12
)-(
2.16
) of connection coefficients.

\square

The above lemma leads us to commutation formulae for the projected covariant derivatives
\not{\nabla}_{4},\not{\nabla}_{3}
and
\not{\nabla}
. The proof can be found, for example, in
[
27
]
,
[
15
]
,
[
17
]
, among others.

Lemma 3.2

If
\xi
is a
(0,k)

S_{u,\underline{u}}-
tensor, then

\displaystyle[\not{\nabla}_{4},\not{\nabla}_{B}]\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}=

\displaystyle-{\chi_{B}}^{C}\not{\nabla}_{C}\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{k}\big(\chi_{A_{i}B}\underline{\eta}^{C}-\underline{\eta}_{A_{i}}{\chi_{B}}^{C}+\prescript{*}{}{\beta}_{B}{\not{\epsilon}_{A_{i}}}^{C}+\frac{1}{2}{\not{g}_{B}}^{C}\not{T}_{4A_{i}}-\frac{1}{2}\not{g}_{A_{i}B}{\not{T}_{4}}^{C}\big)\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{i-1}CA_{i+1}\dots A_{k}},

(3.57)

\displaystyle[\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{B}]\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}=

\displaystyle(\eta_{B}+\underline{\eta}_{B})\not{\nabla}_{3}\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}-{\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{C}\not{\nabla}_{C}\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{k}\big(\underline{\chi}_{A_{i}B}\eta^{C}-\eta_{A_{i}}{\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{C}-\prescript{*}{}{\underline{\beta}}_{B}{\not{\epsilon}_{A_{i}}}^{C}+\frac{1}{2}{\not{g}_{B}}^{C}\not{T}_{3A_{i}}-\frac{1}{2}\not{g}_{A_{i}B}{\not{T}_{3}}^{C}\big)\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{i-1}CA_{i+1}\dots A_{k}},

(3.58)

\displaystyle[\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4}]\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}=

\displaystyle 2\big(\eta^{C}-\underline{\eta}^{C}\big)\not{\nabla}_{C}\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}-\underline{\omega}\not{\nabla}_{4}\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\sum_{i=1}^{k}\big(\underline{\eta}_{A_{i}}\eta^{C}-\eta_{A_{i}}\underline{\eta}^{C}-\sigma{\not{\epsilon}_{A_{i}}}^{C}\big)\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{i-1}CA_{i+1}\dots A_{k}}.

(3.59)

Finally, for
B,C\in\{1,2\}
, the angular commutation relation

[\not{\nabla}_{B},\not{\nabla}_{C}]\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{k}}=K\sum_{i=1}^{k}(\not{g}_{BA_{i}}\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{i-1}CA_{i+1}\dots A_{k}}-\not{g}_{CA_{i}}\xi_{A_{1}\dots A_{i-1}BA_{i+1}\dots A_{k}})

(3.60)

holds, where we recall that
K
is the Gauss curvature of
(S_{u,\underline{u}},\not{g})
.

3.5.1
Commutation formulae

Suppose
\mathcal{D}\in\begin{Bmatrix}\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4},a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\end{Bmatrix}
. In this subsection we obtain commutation formulae.

Lemma 3.3

Suppose
\Phi
satisfies an equation of the form

\not{\nabla}_{3}\Phi+k\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\Phi=\mathcal{Y}.

(3.61)

Then, there holds

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\big(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\Phi\big)+k\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\Phi=\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{Y}+k\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\Phi\hskip 1.42262pt\bigg[\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\bigg]+k\lvert u\rvert(\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}-\underline{\omega}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\not{T}_{33})\Phi-\mathcal{Y}.

Proof.

Simple computation using the formula
[\not{\nabla}_{3},\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}]=-\not{\nabla}_{3}
(recall that
e_{3}=\partial_{u}+b^{A}\partial_{\theta^{A}}
in our frame).

\square

Lemma 3.4

Suppose
\Phi
satisfies an equation of the form
\not{\nabla}_{3}\Phi+k\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\Phi=\mathcal{Y}.
Then

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{\nabla}_{4}\Phi+k\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{\nabla}_{4}\Phi=

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{Y}-k(\not{\nabla}_{4}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\Phi+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}\Phi+(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\Phi+\sigma\hskip 1.42262pt\Phi+\underline{\omega}\not{\nabla}_{4}\Phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{Y}-k\big(2\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}+2\lvert\underline{\eta}\rvert^{2}+\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\rho-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{34}\big)\Phi+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}\Phi+(\eta,\underline{\eta})^{2}\Phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sigma\Phi+\underline{\omega}\not{\nabla}_{4}\Phi.

Proof.

Simple computation, using the commutation formula (
3.59
) for
[\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4}]
.

\square

Lemma 3.5

Suppose
\Phi
satisfies an equation of the form
\not{\nabla}_{3}\Phi+k\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\Phi=\mathcal{Y}.
Then

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{\nabla}\Phi+\big(k+\frac{1}{2}\big)\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{\nabla}\Phi=

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}\mathcal{Y}-k\big(\not{\nabla}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\big)\Phi-k(\eta,\underline{\eta})\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\Phi+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{Y}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\underline{\hat{\chi}}\not{\nabla}\Phi+\eta(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\Phi+\underline{\beta}\Phi+\not{T}_{3}\Phi.

Proof.

Simple computation, using the commutation formula (
3.58
) for
[\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{B}]
.

\square

We now give the remaining commutation lemmata that describe the pair
(\not{\nabla}_{4},\mathcal{D})
.

Lemma 3.6

Suppose
\Phi
satisfies an equation of the form
\not{\nabla}_{4}\Phi=\mathcal{X}
. Then

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\Phi)=

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{\nabla}_{3}\Phi=\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{X}+\lvert u\rvert\bigg[(\eta,\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}\Phi+(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\Phi+\sigma\Phi\bigg]+\bcancel{\omega\big(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\Phi\big)}-\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{X}.

Proof.

Simple computation, using the commutation formula (
3.59
) for
\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4}
and the fact that
\not{\nabla}_{4}\lvert u\rvert=0
.

\square

Lemma 3.7

Suppose
\Phi
satisfies an equation of the form
\not{\nabla}_{4}\Phi=\mathcal{X}
. Then

\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{\nabla}\Phi=\not{\nabla}\mathcal{X}+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}\Phi+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\Phi+(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{4})\Phi.

Proof.

Simple computation, using the commutation formula for (
3.57
)
\not{\nabla}_{4}
and
\not{\nabla}
.

\square

Combining the results of Lemmata
3.3
-
3.7
, we arrive at two important commutation formulae, along each of the
e_{4}
and
e_{3}
directions, which we give in the form of two Propositions.

Proposition 3.17

Assume a tensor-field
\phi
satisfies
\not{\nabla}_{4}\phi=F_{0}
. Then, if
\mathcal{D}\in\{\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4},a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\}
, there holds
\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{i}\phi:=F_{i},
where

\displaystyle F_{i}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}F_{0}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+\dots+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\phi.

(3.62)

We recall that here
\psi_{g}=\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}
.

Proof.

We use Lemmata
3.6
and
3.7
For the base case:
If
\mathcal{D}=a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla},
then

\not{\nabla}_{4}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\phi=a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}F_{0}+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\phi+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\eta}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\phi+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\phi,

(3.63)

which, as we see, agrees with our claim. If
\mathcal{D}=\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}
, we have

\not{\nabla}_{4}(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\phi)=\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}F_{0}+\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}\phi+\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\phi+\lvert u\rvert\sigma\phi+\bcancel{\omega(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})\phi}-\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega}\not{\nabla}_{4}\phi,

(3.64)

which also agrees with (
10.33
), since
\not{\nabla}_{4}\phi=F_{0}
. Assume it holds for
i-1
. Then

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{i}\phi=[\not{\nabla}_{4},\mathcal{D}]\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+\mathcal{D}F_{i-1}.

(3.65)

We again distinguish between two cases. If the top-order
\mathcal{D}
operator is
a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}
, then

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi=

\displaystyle(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\eta}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})F_{i-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\eta}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}F_{0}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+\dots+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\phi,

(3.66)

which agrees exactly with (
10.33
).
Similarly, for
\mathcal{D}=\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi=

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\bigg[(\eta,\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi\bigg]-\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega}\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})F_{i-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\bigg[(\eta,\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi+\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi\bigg]-\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega}\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}F_{0}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+\dots+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\phi,

which again agrees with (
10.33
). Finally, for
\mathcal{D}=\not{\nabla}_{4},
we have

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{i-1}\phi=\not{\nabla}_{4}F_{i-1},

(3.67)

which, again, yields (
10.33
). The result follows.

\square

We finally provide the commutation formula for
\not{\nabla}_{3}
.

Proposition 3.18

Assume a tensorfield
\phi
satisfies an equation of the form

\not{\nabla}_{3}\phi+a\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\phi=G_{0}.

Then if
\ell
is the number of times
(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})
appears in
\mathcal{D}^{k+\ell}
and
i:=k+\ell
, there holds:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}^{k+\ell}\phi+\big(\frac{\ell}{2}+a\big)\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{k+\ell}\phi=G_{i},

where

\displaystyle G_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\big(\eta,\underline{\eta},1\big)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}G_{0}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}+1}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{T}_{33}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi.

(3.68)

Here,
\psi_{g}\in\{\eta,\underline{\eta},1\}.

Proof.

This is an inductive argument similar to the above.

\square

4
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}_{0,4}}
estimates for the Ricci coefficients

In this section we begin the estimates on the Ricci coefficients. We commence with an estimate for
\underline{\omega}
:

Proposition 4.1

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\lVert{\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\rho]\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\rho]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\rho]+1.

Proof.

Recall that
\underline{\omega}
satisfies the equation

\not{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\omega}=2\eta\cdot\underline{\eta}-\lvert\eta\rvert^{2}-\rho-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{34}.

As such, we have

\displaystyle\lVert{\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\eta\cdot(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\lVert{\rho,\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\eta,\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\rho,\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(4.1)

At this point we use the following formula from Section 4.3 of
[
AnPastNullInfinity
]
:

\displaystyle\lVert\psi\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lVert\psi\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\not{\nabla}\psi\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert\psi\rVert_{L^{2}(S)}

(4.2)

Translating to scale-invariant norms, we have

\displaystyle a^{-s_{2}(\psi)}\lvert u\rvert^{2s_{2}(\psi)+\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\psi\rVert_{L^{4}(S)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(a^{-s_{2}(\psi)}\lvert u\rvert^{2s_{2}(\psi)}\lVert\psi\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(a^{-s_{2}(\psi)-\frac{1}{2}}\lvert u\rvert^{2s_{2}(\psi)+1}\lVert\not{\nabla}\psi\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot a^{\frac{1}{4}}+a^{-s_{2}(\psi)}\lvert u\rvert^{2s_{2}(\psi)}\lVert\psi\rVert_{L^{2}(S)},

or, equivalently,

\displaystyle\lVert{\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lVert{\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(4.3)

As such, we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert{\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\rho}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\rho}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\rho}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\lVert{\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}+\big(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\rho}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\rho}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\rho}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\big(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\rho}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\rho}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\big(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\rho}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(4.4)

For
\not{T}_{34}
, since
s_{2}(\not{T}_{34})=1
, we have

\lVert{\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a^{-1}\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{5}{2}}\big(\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\lvert\not{T}_{34}\rvert^{4}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}.

However,

\lvert\not{T}_{34}(u,\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\rvert=4\int_{\mathcal{P}_{(u,\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})}}f\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}\hskip 1.42262pt\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}p^{1}\text{d}p^{2}\text{d}p^{3}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{4}},

given the bounds on
f,\sqrt{\det\not{g}}
and the momentum components. As such, there holds

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

The result follows.

\square

We continue with an estimate for
\hat{\chi}
.

Proposition 4.2

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds:

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{1}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]+1.

Proof.

There holds

\not{\nabla}_{4}\hat{\chi}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}=-\alpha.

(4.5)

As such, there holds:

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert\hat{\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,0})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert\alpha\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

(4.6)

We therefore have, since
\hat{\chi}
vanishes on the incoming cone,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}O^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}){\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\bigg(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\bigg(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{1}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]+1.

(4.7)

The claim follows.

\square

Proposition 4.3

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds:

\lVert{\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\beta]\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\beta]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\beta]+1.

Proof.

We recall the equation for
\eta
:

\not{\nabla}_{4}\eta=\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi(\underline{\eta}-\eta)+\hat{\chi}\cdot(\underline{\eta}-\eta)-\beta-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{4}.

Taking into account that
\eta
vanishes on the incoming cone, there holds

\displaystyle\lVert{\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(4.8)

For
\beta
we bound the integral as follows:

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\lVert{\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}.

(4.9)

By using the bootstrap assumptions, the result holds.
We now control
\lVert{\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}
, keeping in mind that the structure equation for
\eta
implies that
s_{2}(\not{T}_{4})=\frac{1}{2}
. There holds

\lVert{\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}=a^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{3}{2}}\bigg(\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\lvert\not{T}_{4}\rvert^{4}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}.

For a fixed
A\in\{1,2\}
, there holds
\lvert T_{4A}\rvert=\lvert 2\not{g}_{AA^{\prime}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}fp^{A^{\prime}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}p^{1}\text{d}p^{2}\text{d}p^{3}\rvert\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}
. Here we have used the fact that

\sup_{(x,p)\in\mathcal{P}}\lvert f(x,p)\rvert\leq a.

Consequently,
\not{g}^{AC}\not{T}_{4A}\not{T}_{4C}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{6}}
. This means that, given the fact that
\sqrt{\det{\not{g}}}\lesssim\lvert u\rvert^{2}
, we have the estimate

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

(4.10)

\square

We move on with estimates for
\operatorname{tr}\chi
.

Proposition 4.4

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds:

\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\mathcal{R}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]+1\big)\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\hat{\chi}]+1.

Proof.

There holds

\not{\nabla}_{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\chi)^{2}=-\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert^{2}-\not{T}_{44}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

(4.11)

For the first term, we have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(\operatorname{tr}\chi)^{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

For the term involving
\hat{\chi}
, we have

\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\hat{\chi}]\mathcal{O}_{0,4}[\hat{\chi}]\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\mathcal{R}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]+1\big)\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\hat{\chi}].

(4.12)

We proceed to control
\not{T}_{44}
. There holds

\lVert{\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}=\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\lvert\not{T}_{44}\rvert^{4}\sqrt{\det{\not{g}}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\big)^{\frac{1}{4}}.

(4.13)

However,
\lvert\not{T}_{44}\rvert=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}fp^{3}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}p^{1}\text{d}p^{2}\text{d}p^{3}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},
where we have used the bounds on
f
and
\sqrt{\det\not{g}}
. As a consequence, we have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

\square

Remark 3

The estimate for
\operatorname{tr}\chi
will be improved after closing the bootstrap for the supremum norm of
\hat{\chi}
, in Section
6
.

We proceed with components which satisfy equations in the
e_{3}-
direction.

Proposition 4.5

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the boottstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

There holds

\not{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}=\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}-\underline{\alpha}.

(4.14)

Using Proposition
3.8
, passing to scale-invariant norms and multiplying by
a^{-1/2}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert{\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\big(\lVert{\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert{\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\big(\lVert{\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert{\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{3}{2}}}\big(\lVert{\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\big)\lesssim 1,

(4.15)

where in the last line we have used Hölder’s inequality. The result follows.

\square

Proposition 4.6

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1,\hskip 8.53581pt\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]+1.

Proof.

There holds

\not{\nabla}_{3}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{1}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})^{2}=-\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}-\not{T}_{33}

Using the evolution lemma from Proposition
3.8
and passing to scale-invariant norms, we have

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}}\lVert\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2},\hskip 1.42262pt\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}.

(4.16)

For
\not{T}_{33},
given that the signature is
s_{2}(\not{T}_{33})=2,
we have

\lVert{\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a^{-2}\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{9}{2}}\lVert{\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a^{-2}\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{9}{2}}\bigg(\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\lvert\not{T}_{33}\rvert^{4}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}.

However,

\lvert\not{T}_{33}\rvert=4\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}fp^{4}p^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\text{d}p^{1}\text{d}p^{2}\text{d}p^{3}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{6}},

(4.17)

whence

\lVert{\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}.

(4.18)

Putting this back to (
4.16
), we get the desired result.

For
\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}
, keeping in mind that
\not{\nabla}_{3}=\partial_{u}
in our setting, the following equation holds:

\not{\nabla}_{3}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}=\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}-\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}-\not{T}_{33}.

Using the Evolution Lemma
3.8
and passing to scale-invariant norms, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert{\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\big(\lVert{\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}^{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+\lVert{\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(4.19)

We have

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a^{2}}\cdot\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}=\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1,

(4.20)

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\cdot\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}=\frac{a\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]\mathcal{O}_{0,4}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}],

(4.21)

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{1}{a\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\lesssim 1.

(4.22)

\square

Proposition 4.7

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\lVert{\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{0,\infty}[\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}]\big(\mathcal{R}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\beta]\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\beta]+\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{0}[\beta]+1\big).

(4.23)

Proof.

There holds

\not{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\eta}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}=\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\eta-\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot(\underline{\eta}-\eta)+\underline{\beta}-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{3}:=F_{\underline{\eta}}.

We recall that
s_{2}(\underline{\eta})=\frac{1}{2}
. Using Proposition
3.8
with
\lambda_{0}=\frac{1}{2},
we obtain

\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert F_{\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime},

which rewrites in scale-invariant norms as

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert{\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\underline{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\not{T}_{3}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(4.24)

From the first integral, the most dangerous term is

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\cdot\frac{1}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\bigg(\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\bigg)\lVert{\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

Using the fact that
\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}=\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{0,\infty}[\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}]
and
\lVert{\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\beta]\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\beta]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\beta]+1
, it follows that

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{0}[\beta]\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{1}[\beta]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\beta]+1\big).

(4.25)

The rest of the terms in the first integral in (
4
) are bounded by
\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}
. For the other two terms, we see that

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\lVert{\underline{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\underline{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\underline{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+1\big).

(4.26)

We note that

\lVert{\not{T}_{3}}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=\bigg(\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big\lvert\not{g}^{AC}\not{T}_{3A}\not{T}_{3C}\big\rvert^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}.

(4.27)

Using the fact that

\lvert\not{T}_{3A}\rvert\lesssim\bigg\lvert\not{g}_{AA^{\prime}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}fp^{4}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{A}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}p^{1}\text{d}p^{2}\text{d}p^{3}\bigg\rvert\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}},

we arrive as before at

\lVert{\not{T}_{3}}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{9}{2}}},

(4.28)

which means that

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\not{T}_{3}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(4.29)

Dividing the above by
\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}
, we obtain the desired result.

\square

Remark 4

As with other preceding terms, the above estimates for
\operatorname{tr}\chi,\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}},\underline{\eta}
will be improved in the section with
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{0,\infty}
estimates. At this stage, we do not have control on
\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})},\lVert{\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})},\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, as this requires control of their first angular derivatives in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
as well, which is carried out in the next section.

We now obtain estimates in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
for the non-tensorial quantity
\mathcal{G}
(recalling its definition from (
1.45
)):

Proposition 4.8

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lVert{\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{1,4}[\eta]+1.

(4.30)

Proof.

First of all, by definition,
\mathcal{G}
inherits the imposed bootstrap assumption on the Ricci coefficients

\lVert{\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\mathcal{O}.

Let us recall the transport equation

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{G}\sim\not{\nabla}\eta+\mathcal{G}\cdot\chi+\underline{\omega}\cdot\chi+\not{\Gamma}\cdot(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\eta\cdot\underline{\eta}+(\rho,\sigma)+\not{T},

(4.31)

from equation (
1.45
). Using the transport inequality along the
e_{4}
direction, together with the bootstrap assumptions, we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lVert{\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+1.

(4.32)

Here, we have used
\mathcal{L}^{4}\infty_{(sc)}-\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
estimates for the nonlinear terms
\mathcal{G}\cdot\chi,\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}\cdot\chi,\not{\Gamma}\cdot(\eta,\underline{\eta})
and
\eta\cdot\underline{\eta}
. The result follows.

\square

Proposition 4.9

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\big\lVert\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}\big\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{1,4}[\hat{\chi}]+1.

Proof.

We recall the equation

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\big(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}\big)^{C}_{AB}=

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{A}{\chi_{B}}^{C}+\not{\nabla}_{B}{\chi_{A}}^{C}-\not{\nabla}^{C}{\chi_{BA}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\big({\chi_{A}}^{D}-\not{\nabla}_{A}b^{D}+b^{E}\not{\Gamma}^{D}_{AE}\big)\big(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}\big)^{C}_{DB}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\big({\chi_{B}}^{D}-\not{\nabla}_{B}b^{D}+b^{E}\not{\Gamma}^{D}_{BE}\big)\big(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}\big)^{C}_{AD}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\big({\chi_{D}}^{C}-\not{\nabla}_{D}b^{C}+b^{E}\not{\Gamma}^{C}_{DE}\big)\big(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}\big)^{D}_{AB}.

(4.33)

Notice that

{\chi_{A}}^{D}-\not{\nabla}_{A}b^{D}+b^{E}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\Gamma}^{D}_{AE}={\chi_{A}}^{D}-e_{A}(b^{D})=\Gamma^{D}_{3A}.

Hence the above equation rewrites as

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\big(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}\big)^{C}_{AB}=

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{A}{\chi_{B}}^{C}+\not{\nabla}_{B}{\chi_{A}}^{C}-\not{\nabla}^{C}{\chi_{BA}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\Gamma^{D}_{3A}\big(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}\big)^{C}_{DB}-\Gamma^{D}_{3B}\big(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}\big)^{C}_{AD}+\Gamma^{C}_{3D}\big(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}\big)^{D}_{AB}.

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{G}
from (
1.45
), we thus have the schematic identity

\not{\nabla}_{4}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})=\not{\nabla}\chi+\mathcal{G}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}).

(4.34)

As such, there holds

\displaystyle\lVert{\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\sum_{i=0}^{1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\lVert{\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+1.

(4.35)

In the last equation we have used the
\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}-\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
Sobolev embedding on
\mathcal{G}
.

\square

This concludes the
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{0,4}
-estimates for the Ricci coefficients.

5
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}_{1,4}}
estimates for the Ricci coefficients

We now move to the
L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
estimates for the first derivatives of the Ricci coefficients. We recall at this point that
\mathcal{D}
denotes an arbitrary differential operator in the set
\{\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4},a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\}
.

We note the following:

Proposition 5.1

Consider a Ricci coefficient
\psi^{(s)}
with
s_{2}-
signature
s
that satisfies an equation of the form

\not{\nabla}_{4}\psi^{(s)}=\sum_{s_{1}+s_{2}=s}\psi^{(s_{1})}\psi^{(s_{2})}+\Psi^{(s)}+\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s)}.

Then, if
\mathcal{D}
is as above, there holds

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}=

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{1}+s_{2}=s}\psi^{(s_{1})}\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s_{2})}+\mathcal{D}\Psi^{(s)}+\mathcal{D}\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s)}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}+\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\psi^{(s)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\sigma\psi^{(s)}+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\psi^{(s)}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\psi^{(s)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\big(\sum_{s_{1}+s_{2}=s}\psi^{(s_{1})}\psi^{(s_{2})}+\Psi^{(s)}+\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s)}\big).

(5.1)

As such, the following estimate holds:

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\sum_{s_{1}+s_{2}=s}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq 1}\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\psi^{(s_{1})}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s_{2})}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\Psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\mathcal{D}\Psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\Psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}

(5.2)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\mathcal{D}\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq 1}\lVert{(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq 1}\lVert{(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\lVert{\psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq 1}\lVert{\psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big(\lVert{\sigma}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\sigma}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\sigma}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq 1}\lVert{(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\hat{\chi},\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\operatorname{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq 1}\lVert{(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\hat{\chi},\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\operatorname{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq 1}\lVert{\psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big(\lVert{(\beta,\not{T}_{4})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})(\beta,\not{T}_{4})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{(\beta,\not{T}_{4})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{1}+s_{2}=s}\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq 1}\lVert{\psi^{(s_{1})}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\psi^{(s_{2})}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\lVert{\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq 1}\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big(\lVert{\Psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\Psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\Psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq 1}\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s)}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\big).

(5.3)

Proof.

This is a repeated use of the scale-invariant Hölder inequalities together with the co-dimension 1 trace inequality for the
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S)-
norms of
\sigma,\Psi^{(s)},\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s)},\beta
and
\not{T}_{4}
.

\square

Similarly, for tensor-fields satisfying equations in the
\not{\nabla}_{3}
-direction, the following holds:

Proposition 5.2

Assume a Ricci coefficient
\psi^{(s)}
with
s_{2}
-signature
s
satisfies an equation of the form

\not{\nabla}_{3}\psi^{(s)}+\lambda(\psi^{(s)})\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi^{(s)}=\sum_{s_{1}+s_{2}=s+1}\psi^{(s_{1})}\psi^{(s_{2})}+\Psi^{(s+1)}+\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s+1)}.

Then, if
\mathcal{D}
is as above and
\ell
is as in Proposition
3.18
, the following holds:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}+\big(\lambda(\psi^{(s)})+\frac{\ell}{2}\big)\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{1}+s_{2}=s+1}\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s_{1})}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi^{(s_{2})}+\mathcal{D}\Psi^{(s+1)}+\mathcal{D}\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s+1)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\eta,\underline{\eta},1)\big(\sum_{s_{1}+s_{2}=s+1}\psi^{(s_{1})}\psi^{(s_{2})}+\Psi^{(s+1)}+\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s+1)}\big)+\lvert u\rvert\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\psi^{(s)}+\lvert u\rvert(\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}+\underline{\omega}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\not{T}_{33})\psi^{(s)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\omega},\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}+\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi^{(s)}+(\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\psi^{(s)}+(\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta})\psi^{(s)}+(\not{T}_{3},\not{T}_{34})\psi^{(s)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi^{(s)}+(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\psi^{(s)}:=F_{\psi^{(s)}}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert^{2\lambda(\psi^{(s)})+\ell-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2\lambda(\psi^{(s)})+\ell-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\mathcal{D}\psi^{(s)}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u_{\infty}},\underline{u})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2\lambda(\psi^{(s)})+\ell-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{F_{\psi^{(s)}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

After translating this to scale-invariant norms, we obtain the desired inequalities by using the co-dimension 1 trace inequality on
\Psi^{(s+1)},\mathcal{V}\ell^{(s+1)},\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3}
and standard scale-invariant Hölder inequalities.

We begin with the estimates for
\underline{\omega}
.

Proposition 5.3

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{1}[\rho]\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{2}[\rho]+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\rho]+1.

Proof.

We recall that

\not{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\omega}=2\eta\cdot\underline{\eta}-\lvert\eta\rvert^{2}-\rho-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{34}.

As such, there holds:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\underline{\omega}=

\displaystyle(\eta,\underline{\eta})\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\eta+\eta\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\mathcal{D}\rho+\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}+\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt(\eta\hskip 1.42262pt(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\rho+\not{T}_{34})+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\underline{\omega}+\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})^{2}\underline{\omega}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\sigma\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\underline{\omega}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\underline{\omega}.

(5.4)

Consequently, since
\mathcal{D}\underline{\omega}
vanishes on
\underline{H}_{0}
, there holds

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\rho}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+1,

(5.5)

where we have used the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
) to bound the majority of the terms above by 1.
For the integral on the right-hand side, there holds

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}.

(5.6)

Using the bounds assumed in (
2.80
) for
\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})},\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})},
the result follows.

\square

Proposition 5.4

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\alpha]+1.

Proof.

We recall that

\not{\nabla}_{4}\hat{\chi}=\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}+\alpha.

As such, there holds

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}+\mathcal{D}\alpha+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}+\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega}\big(\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}+\alpha\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\hat{\chi}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\hat{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\hat{\chi}+\lvert u\rvert\sigma\hat{\chi}.

(5.7)

Let us restate that in the above schematic, for a given (fixed)
\mathcal{D}
, not all terms appear in the actual equation, but rather what is true in any case is that there is
equality of total signature
between the left and right hand sides. For example, when we consider
\not{\nabla}_{4}(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})\hat{\chi},
the term
\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}
does appear and this second time
\mathcal{D}
is equal to
(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})
. If, however,
\mathcal{D}
on the left-hand side equals
(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})
, then on the right-hand side
\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}
does not appear, but instead the term

a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\hat{\chi}
does and so on. It is a convenient fact that the schematic commutation formulae allow us to perform estimates on an arbitrary
\mathcal{D}
-derivative without the need to distinguish cases. This is possible precisely because, in the end, every term on the right-hand side is estimated in its own scale-invariant norm.

Using the
L^{\infty}(S),L^{4}(S)
bootstrap bounds on the Ricci coefficients, together with the bootstrap bounds on the curvature norms and the obtained
L^{4}
bounds on
\not{T}_{4}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+1\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\alpha]+1.

(5.8)

\square

Remark 5

We can now obtain the estimate

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\big(\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\big)\lesssim\big(\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{0}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{2}[\alpha]\big)\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{1}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\alpha]+1.

(5.9)

We will need it in the next Proposition.

Proposition 5.5

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there exist a constant
C
, quadratic in
\mathcal{R}_{i}[\alpha]
for
i=0,1,2
, such that there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}C(\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha],\mathcal{R}_{1}[\alpha],\mathcal{R}_{2}[\alpha])+\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}(\mathcal{V}_{1}[\not{T}_{44}]+\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{44}])+1.

Proof.

The term
\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi
satisfies a schematic equation of the form

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi=

\displaystyle\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}+\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi+\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega}(\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert^{2}+\not{T}_{44})+\lvert u\rvert\sigma\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\operatorname{tr}\chi+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\eta}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\operatorname{tr}\chi+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\operatorname{tr}\chi.

(5.10)

Integrating along
