H_{u}
, we have

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

(5.11)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

(5.12)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\sigma}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{(\beta,\not{T}_{4})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(5.13)

Most of the terms are immediately bounded above by 1 using the bootstrap assumptions. We will only give an explanation for the most difficult terms. For the first term, there holds

\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1}\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\big)\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\big).

(5.14)

Here a simple bootstrap will not suffice and we need the improvements on
\hat{\chi}
, found in Proposition
5.4
and Remark
5
. Indeed, we have

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\hat{\chi}]\mathcal{O}_{1,4}[\hat{\chi}]\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\big((\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{0}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{2}[\alpha])\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{1}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\alpha]+1\big)\big(\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{1}[\alpha]\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{2}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\alpha]+1\big).

For the trilinear terms involving
\lvert u\rvert
, notice that
\lVert{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a,
whence for example

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\sigma}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big(\mathcal{R}_{0}[\sigma]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1}[\sigma]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\sigma]\big)\lesssim 1,

(5.15)

where we have used the bootstrap assumptions on the curvature and so on. Finally, we have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\mathcal{V}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[T_{44}]\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{V}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[T_{44}]+\mathcal{V}_{1}[T_{44}]\big)\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}(\mathcal{V}_{1}[\not{T}_{44}]+\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{44}]).

(5.16)

\square

Proposition 5.6

There holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\beta]\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\beta]+\mathcal{V}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\not{T}_{4}]\mathcal{V}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\not{T}_{4}]+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\beta]+\mathcal{V}_{1}[\not{T}_{4}]+1.

Proof.

We obtain the schematic equation for
\mathcal{D}\eta
:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\eta=

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})(\eta,\underline{\eta})+(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\mathcal{D}\beta+\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{4}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\eta+\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\eta+\lvert u\rvert\sigma\hskip 1.42262pt\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega}\big((\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\beta+\not{T}_{4}\big)+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\eta+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\eta+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\eta.

(5.17)

By integrating along a given
H_{u}
, we have

\lVert{\mathcal{D}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lVert\mathcal{D}\eta\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(5.18)

Taking into account that
\mathcal{D}\eta
vanishes on
S_{u,0}
and using the bootstrap bounds, all of the terms emerging are bounded by 1 except those involving
\mathcal{D}\beta
and
\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{4}
. For this term, we have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\beta]\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\beta]+\mathcal{V}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\not{T}_{4}]\mathcal{V}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\not{T}_{4}]+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\beta]+\mathcal{V}_{1}[\not{T}_{4}].

(5.19)

The result follows.

\square

We now turn to quantities that satisfy equations in the ingoing direction.

Proposition 5.7

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\underline{\alpha}]\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\underline{\alpha}]+\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{1}[\underline{\alpha}]+1.

Proof.

There holds

\not{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}=\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}-\underline{\alpha}.

As such, we have

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\big(1+\frac{\ell}{2}\big)\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}=

\displaystyle\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\underline{\omega}+\mathcal{D}\underline{\alpha}+\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt(\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\underline{\alpha})+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\underline{\hat{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\sigma\underline{\hat{\chi}}+(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\underline{\hat{\chi}}:=T_{1}+\dots+T_{11}.

(5.20)

Using Proposition
3.8
and passing to scale-invariant norms, we have

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{11}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(5.21)

The most borderline term is

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\underline{\alpha}]\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\underline{\alpha}]+\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{1}[\underline{\alpha}].

The rest of the terms are bounded by 1 in a straightforward way.

\square

Remark 6

The bound

\lVert{\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{1}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim C(\underline{\mathcal{R}})

is now established and we shall need it in the next Proposition.

Proposition 5.8

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

There holds

\not{\nabla}_{3}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{1}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})^{2}=-\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}-\not{T}_{33}.

We have, with
\ell
as before, the schematic

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{1+\ell}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}=

\displaystyle\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{33}+(\eta,\underline{\eta},1)(\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}+\not{T}_{33})+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\omega},\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}^{2}+(\rho,\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3},\not{T}_{34})\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\sigma\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\lvert u\rvert\big(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt(\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega})+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}\big)\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\lvert u\rvert\not{T}_{33}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}.

(5.22)

Passing to scale-invariant norms while using Proposition
3.8
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}}\lVert\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\sum_{i=1}^{16}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(5.23)

Again, we discuss the most difficult terms. The terms

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\big(\lVert{\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\big(\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}{a}+\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}{a^{2}}\big)\frac{O^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(5.24)

The terms involving
(\sigma,\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3})\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}
are also bounded by 1 using the bootstrap assumptions, by bounding
\sigma,a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\beta},a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{T}_{3}
in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
and eventually by their
\lVert{\cdot}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}-
norms. The first term involving
\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}
is handled by a simple
\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}-\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
Hölder inequality using the bootstrap assumptions, whereas the term
involving
\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{33}
is bounded by 1 using the bootstrap assumptions on

\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})},\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}

and the codimension 1 trace inequality.

For
\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}},
we similarly have the equation

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+(1+\frac{\ell}{2})\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{33}+(\eta,\underline{\eta},1)(\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}+\not{T}_{33})+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\omega},\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\chi\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+(\rho,\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3},\not{T}_{34})\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\sigma\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\big(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}(\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}},\underline{\omega})+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}})\big)\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\lvert u\rvert\not{T}_{33}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}:=T_{1}+\dots T_{13}.

(5.25)

Passing to scale-invariant norms, we have

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{13}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(5.26)

We again examine the top-order terms, as the zero-order ones are bounded by 1 in a straightforward way using the bootstrap assumptions. We have

(1,\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a^{2}}\cdot\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1,

(5.27)

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\omega},\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a}\cdot\frac{\hskip 1.42262ptO^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1,

(5.28)

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]\mathcal{O}_{1,4}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]\lesssim\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\underline{\alpha}]\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\underline{\alpha}]+\underline{\mathcal{R}}[\underline{\alpha}]+1,

(5.29)

where we have crucially used the improvements from Proposition
5.7
and Remark
6
.

\square

We move on with an
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{1,4}
estimate for
\underline{\eta}
.

Proposition 5.9

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{D}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{3}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{3}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{3}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\big)+1.

(5.30)

Proof.

For
\underline{\eta}
we recall that the following equation holds:

\not{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\eta}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\underline{\eta}=\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\eta-\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot(\underline{\eta}-\eta)+\underline{\beta}-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{3}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}+\frac{1+\ell}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}=

\displaystyle\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\eta+\eta\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}(\mathcal{D}\eta,\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta})+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\mathcal{D}\underline{\beta}+\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{3}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\big(\frac{1}{\Omega^{2}},\omega,a^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta\big)(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\eta+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot(\underline{\eta},\eta)+\underline{\beta}+\not{T}_{3})+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\big(\frac{1}{\Omega^{2}}-1\big)\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\underline{\eta}+\lvert u\rvert(\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}+\not{T}_{33})\underline{\eta}+\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}+(\eta,\underline{\eta})^{2}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sigma\underline{\eta}+\omega\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3})\underline{\eta}:=T_{1}+\dots+T_{21}.

(5.31)

Passing to scale-invariant norms, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\sum_{j=1}^{21}\lVert{T_{j}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(5.32)

For the first term, we have

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\sup_{u_{\infty}\leq u^{\prime}\leq u}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\big).

However, at this point, the bound
\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}
implies control on
\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}
, using the improvements. Similarly, the improved control on
\lVert{\mathcal{D}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
gives a bound dependent on
\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V}\ell
. For the terms involving
\mathcal{D}\underline{\beta},\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\beta},\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{3}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{3}{2}}}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\beta,\not{T}_{3})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\beta,\not{T}_{3})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\beta,\not{T}_{3})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\big).

(5.33)

The rest of the terms are bounded above by
\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}
using the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
).

\square

We continue with the estimate for the non-tensorial quantity
\mathcal{G}
.

Proposition 5.10

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
),there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{3,2}[\eta]+\mathcal{R}_{2}[\rho,\sigma]+\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}]+1.

(5.34)

Proof.

Recall the equation

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{G}\sim\not{\nabla}\eta+\mathcal{G}\cdot\chi+\underline{\omega}\cdot\chi+\not{\Gamma}\cdot(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\eta\cdot\underline{\eta}+(\rho,\sigma)+\not{T}.

Differentiating, we get

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\mathcal{G}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\not{\nabla}\eta+(\mathcal{G},\underline{\omega})\hskip 1.42262pt(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}(\mathcal{G},\underline{\omega})+\mathcal{D}(\eta,\not{\Gamma})\hskip 1.42262pt(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\mathcal{D}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\hskip 1.42262pt(\not{\Gamma},\eta)+\mathcal{D}(\rho,\sigma)+\mathcal{D}\not{T}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt(\eta,\underline{\eta})\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\mathcal{G}+\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt(\eta,\underline{\eta})^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{G}+\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\sigma\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{G}+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\mathcal{G}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{G}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega}\big(\not{\nabla}\eta+\mathcal{G}\cdot\chi+\underline{\omega}\cdot\chi+\not{\Gamma}\cdot(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\eta\cdot\underline{\eta}+(\rho,\sigma)+\not{T}\big).

(5.35)

As before, integrating along the
e_{4}-
direction, we see that the only top-derivative term is
\mathcal{D}\not{\nabla}\eta
, which can be bounded by the
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{2,2}
estimate for
\eta
as well as the elliptic estimate for
\eta
(here we use
L^{4}-L^{2}
Sobolev embedding):

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lVert{\mathcal{D}\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{\nabla}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+l.o.t.\lesssim\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{3,2}[\eta]+l.o.t.

(5.36)

In particular, the terms
\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\rho,\sigma,\not{T})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
are bounded as before in the current section. The claim follows.

\square

Proposition 5.11

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{3,2}[\hat{\chi}]+1.

Proof.

Recall the schematic equation

\not{\nabla}_{4}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})=\not{\nabla}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)+\mathcal{G}\cdot(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}).

As such, we have

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})=

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\not{\nabla}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)+\mathcal{D}\mathcal{G}\hskip 1.42262pt(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})+\mathcal{G}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})+\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})+\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt(\eta,\underline{\eta})^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\sigma\hskip 1.42262pt(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\hskip 1.42262pt\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\hskip 1.42262pt(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\big(\not{\nabla}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)+\mathcal{G}\cdot(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})\big).

(5.37)

Integrating along the
e_{4}
-direction and using the bootstrap assumptions, we see that the top-order term is

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{\nabla}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime},

which is bounded as before by
\mathcal{O}_{3,2}[\hat{\chi}]+1
. The rest of the terms are bounded by
1
as before. The result follows.

\square

6
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}}
estimates for the Ricci coefficients

In this section, we improve the bootstrap assumptions on the
\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S)-
norm of the Ricci coefficients.

Proposition 6.1

There exists a constant
C
depending on the total norms
\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V}\ell
such that

\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{0,\infty}\lesssim C.

Proof.

The scale-invariant inequality

\lVert{\phi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lVert{\phi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\phi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\phi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})},

(6.1)

holds for an arbitrary horizontal tensor-field. At first instance, Propositions

4.2
,
5.4
,

4.5
and
5.7
imply control for
\hat{\chi}
and
\underline{\hat{\chi}}
in the supremum norm, given in Remarks
5
-
6
. In turn, these estimates on the
\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}
-norm of
\hat{\chi},\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}
improve Propositions
4.4
and

4.6
for
\operatorname{tr}\chi
and
\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}
respectively.
For
\underline{\eta}
, Propositions
4.23
and
5.9
imply the improvement of the bootstrap assumption on
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{0,\infty}[\underline{\eta}]
, through equation (
6.1
). Consequently, we get

\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{0,\infty}[\underline{\omega},\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi,\eta,\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi},\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}},\underline{\eta}]\lesssim C(\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}},\mathcal{V},\underline{\mathcal{V}}),

for some constant
C
depending only on the curvature and Vlasov norms. This concludes the proof of the proposition.

\square

7
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{2,2}
estimates for the Ricci coefficients

In this section we improve the bootstrap bounds on two derivatives of Ricci coefficients in
L^{2}
. For efficiency of presentation, from now on and for the rest of this paper, we opt to carry computations out in a schematic way, rather than term by term as in
4
-
6
. We begin this section with a series of preliminary estimates that will be important in handling the equations schematically.

7.1
Preliminary estimates

Proposition 7.1

Let
\psi
denote an arbitrary element of the set
\big\{\frac{1}{a}\lvert u\rvert,\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi,\underline{\omega},\eta,\underline{\eta},\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}},\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\underline{\hat{\chi}},\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\operatorname{tr}\chi\big\}
. Let
\psi_{g}\in\{\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega},\eta,\underline{\eta},1\}
and let
\Psi_{\underline{u}}\in\big\{\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\alpha,\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta}\big\}
,
\Psi_{u}\in\big\{\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta},\underline{\alpha}\big\}
. Finally, denote an arbitrary element of the set
\big\{\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{a}\not{T}_{44},\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{a}\not{T}_{4},\not{T}_{34},\not{T}_{33},\not{T}_{3},\not{T}\big\}
by
\mathcal{V}\ell
. Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), the following hold:

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}\leq 1}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lvert u\rvert,

(7.1)

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}\leq 1}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\psi,\mathcal{V}\ell)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim O+V,

(7.2)

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}\leq 1}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\psi,\mathcal{V}\ell)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{O(O+V)}{\lvert u\rvert}

(7.3)

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}\leq 1}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\Psi_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{O^{2}\hskip 1.42262ptR}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(7.4)

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}\leq 1}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{O^{2}\hskip 1.42262ptR}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(7.5)

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}\leq 1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Psi_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim R,

(7.6)

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}\leq 1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}R,

(7.7)

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}\leq 1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{R\hskip 1.42262ptO}{\lvert u\rvert},

(7.8)

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}\leq 1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{R\hskip 1.42262ptO}{\lvert u\rvert},

(7.9)

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}\leq 1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{R\hskip 1.42262ptO^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(7.10)

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}\leq 1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{R\hskip 1.42262ptO^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(7.11)

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}\leq 1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\psi,\mathcal{V}\ell)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{O^{2}(O+V)}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(7.12)

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\psi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert},

(7.13)

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{V}\ell}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim V.

(7.14)

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Psi_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lesssim R

(7.15)

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}R.

(7.16)

Proof.

Let us note that (
7.13
)-(
7.16
) correspond to bounds for the highest-order terms encountered in the structure equations for
\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{2}\psi,\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}^{2}\psi
, as indicated in the commutation formulae (
10.33
), (
3.68
), since for example

\not{\nabla}_{4}\psi=\psi\psi+\Psi_{\underline{u}}+\mathcal{V}\ell\hskip 8.53581pt\text{or}\hskip 8.53581pt\not{\nabla}_{3}\psi+k\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\psi=\psi\psi+\Psi_{u}+\mathcal{V}\ell,

whereas the rest of the inequalities correspond to the lower-order terms in the structure equations. For any given inequality above, if there are three terms, they are bounded either in
\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}-\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}-\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
or
\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}-\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}-\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}
. If there are two terms, they are bounded either in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}-\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
or in
\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}-\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}
.

1.

For (
7.13
), the case
i_{2}=2
is straightforward. For
i_{2}=1,

the operator
\mathcal{D}
falls on exactly one of the three terms
\psi
,
\psi_{g}
. We bound that term in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
together with one of the remaining terms and the last term in
\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}.
This gives

a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt(\lVert{\psi\psi\mathcal{D}\psi_{g}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\psi_{g}\psi\mathcal{D}\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})})\lesssim\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

For
i_{2}=0
, we have

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\psi\mathcal{D}\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\psi\mathcal{D}^{2}\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{D}\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\big)\lesssim\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

2.

For (
7.14
), we can condition with respect to
i_{2}
similarly. For
i=2
, there holds

a\lVert{\psi\psi\mathcal{V}\ell}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{aO^{2}\hskip 1.42262ptV}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

where we bound
\mathcal{V}\ell
in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
. For
i_{2}=1
we have
a^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{D}\psi\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{V}\ell}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\psi_{g}\mathcal{D}\mathcal{V}\ell}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\big)\lesssim 1
, where we bound both terms in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
. Finally, for
i_{2}=0
, there holds by definition

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\mathcal{V}\ell}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim V.

3.

For (
7.15
)-(
7.16
) we follow the same strategy, keeping in mind that a given
\Psi_{u}
or
\Psi_{\underline{u}}
can be estimated in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
at zeroth and first orders, whereas for second order we control directly the energy norms.

\square

7.2
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{2,2}
estimates.

Proposition 7.2

Given the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{2}[\rho]+\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{34}]+1.

Proof.

Recall that

\not{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\omega}=2(\eta,\underline{\eta})\eta+\rho+\not{T}_{34}.

Using Proposition
10.33
, we have

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\omega}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\eta\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\underline{\omega}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\underline{\omega}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\omega}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\omega}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\underline{\omega}:=T_{1}+\dots+T_{8}.

(7.17)

Thus, since
\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\omega}
vanishes on
\underline{H}_{0}
, we have

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{8}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(7.18)

The most dangerous term is

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{2}[\rho]+\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{34}]+1.

(7.19)

The rest of the terms are bounded by
1
, using the preliminary estimates from Proposition
7.1
and using the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
). The result follows.

\square

Proposition 7.3

Given the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{2}[\alpha]+1.

Proof.

Using the commutation formula (
10.33
), we obtain

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{2}\hat{\chi}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\hat{\chi}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\hat{\chi}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\hat{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\hat{\chi}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\hat{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\hat{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\hat{\chi}:=T_{1}+\dots+T_{8}.

(7.20)

As such, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i=1}^{8}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(7.21)

Using the preliminary estimates from Proposition
7.1
, we obtain the result. We only further explain the most borderline terms, namely

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{2}[\alpha]+1,

(7.22)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\big(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{2}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a\hskip 1.42262ptO^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}+1\lesssim 1,

(7.23)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\big(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{2}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=1}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262ptO^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}+1\lesssim 1.

(7.24)

\square

Proposition 7.4

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{2}[\beta]+1.

Proof.

Recall the schematic equation

\not{\nabla}_{4}\eta=(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\beta+\not{T}_{4}.

Using Proposition
10.33
, the equation

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{2}\eta=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})

(7.25)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\eta+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\eta+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\eta:=T_{1}+\dots+T_{8}.

(7.26)

Again, we have

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{8}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(7.27)

The most borderline term is

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2}[\beta]+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2}[\beta]+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{34}]+1\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{2}[\beta]+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}V+1\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{2}[\beta]+1.

(7.28)

The rest are bounded by 1 using the preliminary estimates from Proposition
7.1
and the bootstrap assumptions.

\square

Proposition 7.5

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there exists a constant
C
depending on the curvature and Vlasov norms, such that there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim C(\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}},\mathcal{V},\underline{\mathcal{V}}).

Proof.

There holds

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{T}_{44}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\eta+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\eta+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\eta:=T_{1}+\dots+T_{8}.

(7.29)

As such, there holds

\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\lVert\operatorname{tr}\chi\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,0})}+\sum_{i=1}^{8}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

The most dangerous terms are as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}(\lVert{\hat{\chi}\cdot\mathcal{D}^{2}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}\cdot\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}(\mathcal{R}_{2}[\alpha]+1)\big[\big(\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{0}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{2}[\alpha]\big)\mathcal{R}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{1}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\alpha]+1\big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\alpha]+1\big)^{2}+1,

(7.30)

where we have used the improved estimates on
\hat{\chi}
given in Proposition
5.4
, Remark
5
and Proposition
7.3
.
For the matter term, we have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{44}]+1,

(7.31)

since the slowest-decaying term is the one when two derivatives fall on
\not{T}_{44}
. The estimates from Proposition
7.1
and bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
) bound the rest of the terms, which are non-borderline. The result follows.

\square

We now move on to estimates for the components satisfying
\not{\nabla}_{3}
-equations.

Proposition 7.6

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

Given the structure equation for
\underline{\hat{\chi}}
and the commutation formula (
3.68
), we have

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\big(1+\frac{\ell}{2}\big)\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\underline{\hat{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}+1}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{T}_{33}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}:=T_{1}+\dots+T_{16}.

(7.32)

Passing to scale-invariant norms and using the Evolution Lemma
3.8
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert\mathcal{D}^{2}\hat{\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{16}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(7.33)

The top-order terms are handled as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\hskip 1.42262ptO^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1,

(7.34)

For the curvature term, we have

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+1\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{3}{2}}}\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{2}[\underline{\alpha}]+1\lesssim 1.

(7.35)

For the remaining top-order terms, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\big(\underline{\omega},\eta,\underline{\eta},\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\big)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262ptO^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1,

(7.36)

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}+1}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\lesssim 1.

(7.37)

The lower-order terms are treated as in Proposition
7.1
, using the bootstrap assumptions. This concludes the proof.

\square

Proposition 7.7

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1,\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

We have the following structure equation for
\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}
:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{1+\ell}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{T}_{33}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}+1}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{T}_{33}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}:=T_{1}+\dots+T_{16}.

(7.38)

Making use of Proposition
3.8
and passing to scale-invariant norms, we have

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{16}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(7.39)

For the top-order terms, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1,

(7.40)

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}{T_{33}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{5}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+1\lesssim 1,

(7.41)

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\omega},\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1,

(7.42)

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(7.43)

The lower order terms are bounded above by 1 in the usual way, by using the estimates from Proposition
7.1
together with the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
). For
\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}
, we have

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}^{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\big(\frac{\ell}{2}+1\big)\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}},\underline{\hat{\chi}})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{T}_{33}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}+1}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{T}_{33}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}:=T_{1}+\dots+T_{16}.

(7.44)

Passing to scale-invariant norms and using Proposition
3.8
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert\mathcal{D}^{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{16}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(7.45)

We once again only give details for the top-order terms, which are the most borderline. For the first term,

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}},\underline{\hat{\chi}})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

(7.46)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}(\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]\mathcal{O}_{2,2}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]+\mathcal{O}_{1,4}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]^{2})+1\lesssim 1,

(7.47)

where we have used the improvements on
\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}],\mathcal{O}_{1,4}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}],\mathcal{O}_{2,2}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]
. For the next terms, making use of the bootstrap assumptions, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{T}_{33}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{3}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}{\not{T}_{33}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+1\lesssim 1,

(7.48)

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\omega},\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1,

(7.49)

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1,

(7.50)

where we have used the preliminary estimates from Proposition
7.1
and the bootstrap assumptions. These also bound all the lower-order terms in (
7.45
) by 1. The result follows.

\square

Proposition 7.8

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there exists a constant
C
, depending on the curvature and Vlasov norms, such that

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim C(\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}},\mathcal{V}).

Proof.

We have the following schematic identity:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}+\frac{\ell+1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle=
