\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\eta+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\underline{\eta}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\eta}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}+1}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{T}_{33}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\underline{\eta}:=T_{1}+\dots+T_{17}.

(7.51)

Using Proposition
3.8
and passing to scale-invariant norms, we have

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{17}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{T_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(7.52)

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

(7.53)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\big(\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{2}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}+\lVert{\eta\mathcal{D}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\sup_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\sup_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\lVert\eta\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\sup_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\lVert\mathcal{D}\eta\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(7.54)

Here we have made use of the improved estimates on
\mathcal{D}^{2}\eta,\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}
as well as the
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)},\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}
estimates from Sections
5
and
6
. The second term is bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}O^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(7.55)

For the third term, we have

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}(\underline{\mathcal{R}}_{2}[\underline{\beta}]+\underline{\mathcal{V}}_{2}[\not{T}_{3}])+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(7.56)

The fourth, fifth and sixth terms, which also can be considered top-order, are bounded by
\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}
, together with the rest of the terms using the results of Proposition
7.1
. Ultimately, we obtain

\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}C(\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}},\mathcal{V}).

(7.57)

Dividing by
\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}
, we get the desired result.

\square

Proposition 7.9

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there exists a constant
C
depending on the total norms
\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}},\mathcal{V}
such that there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{3,2}[\eta]+C(\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}},\mathcal{V}).

Proof.

There holds

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{2}\mathcal{G}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}\eta+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\omega},\mathcal{G})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\not{\Gamma})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,\sigma,\not{T})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\mathcal{G}

(7.58)

The first term is the one of highest order and is bounded as before by

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\mathcal{O}_{3,2}[\eta].

(7.59)

The rest of the terms are bounded in total by a constant
C(\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}},\mathcal{V})
as before. The claim follows.

\square

Finally, we conclude with
\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{2,2}
estimates for
\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ}
.

Proposition 7.10

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there exists a constant
C
depending on the total norms
\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}},\mathcal{V}
such that there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{3,2}[\hat{\chi}]+C(\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}},\mathcal{V}).

Proof.

There holds

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{2}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{G}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}(\not{\Gamma}-\not{\Gamma}^{\circ})

(7.60)

The highest-order term is the first one. We bound this term by

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim\prescript{(S)}{}{\mathcal{O}}_{3,2}[\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi].

(7.61)

The rest of the error terms are bounded by
C(\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}},\mathcal{V})
as in previous propositions. The result follows.

\square

8
Elliptic estimates for
\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi,\eta

We begin by proving two propositions about curvature components.

8.1
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
estimates for Curvature Components

For the curvature components
\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta}
we have the following equations:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\beta+2\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\beta=\not{\mbox{div }}\alpha+\omega\hskip 1.42262pt\beta+(\eta^{\#}-2\underline{\eta}^{\#})\cdot\alpha-\frac{1}{2}(\not{\nabla}\not{T}_{44}-\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{4}-2\chi\cdot\not{T}_{4}+\omega\hskip 1.42262pt\not{T}_{4}-\not{T}_{44}\eta),

(8.1)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\rho+\frac{3}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\rho=\not{\mbox{div }}\beta-\frac{1}{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}^{\#}\cdot\alpha+(\underline{\eta},\beta)-\frac{1}{4}(\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{T}_{44}-\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{34}-2\eta\cdot\not{T}_{4}+\omega\hskip 1.42262pt\not{T}_{34}),

(8.2)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\sigma+\frac{3}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\sigma=-\not{\mbox{curl }}\beta-\underline{\eta}\wedge\beta+\frac{1}{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}\wedge\alpha-\frac{1}{2}(\not{\epsilon}\cdot\not{\nabla}\not{T}_{4}-\not{\epsilon}\cdot(\chi\times\not{T})-\not{\epsilon}\cdot(\underline{\eta}\otimes\not{T}_{4})),

(8.3)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\beta}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\underline{\beta}=

\displaystyle-\not{\nabla}\rho+\prescript{*}{}{\not{\nabla}}\sigma-\omega\underline{\beta}+2\underline{\hat{\chi}}^{\#}\cdot\beta-3(\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\rho-\prescript{*}{}{\underline{\eta}}\hskip 1.42262pt\sigma)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\not{\nabla}\not{T}_{34}-\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{T}_{4}-\underline{\chi}\cdot\not{T}_{4}-\chi\cdot\not{T}_{3}+\not{T}_{34}\eta+2\eta\cdot\not{T}).

(8.4)

For
\beta,
we have

\displaystyle\lVert{\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\omega,\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lVert{\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1}\lVert{(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big(\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1}[\alpha]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\big)+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1}\lVert{(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\hat{\chi},\omega,\operatorname{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1}\lVert{\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}.

(8.5)

Proposition 8.1

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), there holds:

\displaystyle\lVert{K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+1.

Proof.
We begin with the bootstrap assumption

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq B_{0},

(8.6)

where
B_{0}
is a large constant. For
K
there holds:

There holds

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}K+\operatorname{tr}\chi K=

\displaystyle-\not{\mbox{div }}\beta-(\zeta+2\underline{\eta})\cdot\beta+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{\nabla}\hat{\otimes}\underline{\eta}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot(\underline{\eta}\hat{\otimes}\underline{\eta})-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\not{\mbox{div }}\underline{\eta}-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\lvert\underline{\eta}\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{T}+\frac{1}{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi\not{T}_{34}+\frac{1}{4}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{T}_{44}-\frac{1}{4}\big(\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{T}_{44}-2\eta\cdot\not{T}_{4}+\omega\not{T}_{34}\big)+\frac{3}{4}\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{34}.

(8.7)

Hence, making use of the results of Sections
4
-
6
,

\displaystyle\lVert{K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert{K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert{K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,0})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{D}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\not{T}_{34},\not{T}_{44})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}(\not{T}_{34},\not{T}_{44})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\not{T}_{34},\not{T}_{44})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+1.

(8.8)

Taking into account that
K=\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}
on
S_{u,0}
, we have
\lVert K\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,0})}=\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{a}
, hence

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

(8.9)

This implies an improved estimate for
K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}
via the equation

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)=

\displaystyle-\not{\mbox{div }}\beta-(\zeta+2\underline{\eta})\cdot\beta+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{\nabla}\hat{\otimes}\underline{\eta}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot(\underline{\eta}\hat{\otimes}\underline{\eta})-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\not{\mbox{div }}\underline{\eta}-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\lvert\underline{\eta}\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{T}+\frac{1}{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi\not{T}_{34}+\frac{1}{4}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{T}_{44}-\frac{1}{4}\big(\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{T}_{44}-2\eta\cdot\not{T}_{4}+\omega\not{T}_{34}\big)+\frac{3}{4}\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{34}-\operatorname{tr}\chi K.

(8.10)

As such,

\displaystyle\lVert{K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+1.

(8.11)

\square

8.2
\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
estimates for First Derivatives of Curvature

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions
2.80
, there exists a constant
C
depending only on
\mathcal{R}_{1},\mathcal{R}_{2},\mathcal{V}_{1}
and
\mathcal{V}_{2}
such that, for first derivatives of
\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta}
, there holds:

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathcal{D}\in\{\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4},a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim C(\mathcal{R}_{1},\mathcal{R}_{2},\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathcal{V}_{2}).

(8.12)

Proof.

We begin with the bootstrap assumptions

\sum_{\mathcal{D}\in\{\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4},a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq C_{2},

(8.13)

where
C_{2}
is a large constant. The following equations hold,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\beta=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{D}^{2}\alpha+\mathcal{D}(\omega,\operatorname{tr}\chi)\beta+(\omega,\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\beta+\mathcal{D}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\alpha+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\alpha+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{44}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi,\omega)\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{4}+\mathcal{D}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{T}_{4}+\eta\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}+\mathcal{D}\eta\not{T}_{44}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\beta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\beta+\lvert u\rvert\sigma\beta+\omega\mathcal{D}\beta+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\beta+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\beta+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\beta,

(8.14)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\rho=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta+\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi\rho+\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}\rho+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\alpha+\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\alpha+\mathcal{D}\big(\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}\big)+\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\beta+\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\beta+\mathcal{D}\eta\hskip 1.42262pt\not{T}_{4}+\eta\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{4}+\mathcal{D}\omega\hskip 1.42262pt\not{T}_{34}+\omega\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\rho

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\rho+\lvert u\rvert\sigma\rho+\omega\mathcal{D}\rho+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\rho+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\rho+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\rho,

(8.15)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\sigma=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta+\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi\sigma+\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}\sigma+\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}\beta+\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\beta+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}\alpha+\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\alpha+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\hskip 1.42262pt\not{T}+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\not{T}+\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}\not{T}_{4}+\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{4}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\sigma+\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\sigma

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\sigma^{2}+\omega\mathcal{D}\sigma+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\sigma+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\sigma+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\sigma,

(8.16)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\underline{\beta}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathcal{D}^{2}\rho+\mathcal{D}^{2}\sigma)+(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\omega)\mathcal{D}\underline{\beta}+\underline{\beta}\mathcal{D}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\omega)+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}\beta+\beta\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+(\rho,\sigma)\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}+\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}(\rho,\sigma)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}+\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}+\mathcal{D}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{T}_{4}+(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{4}+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{3}+\not{T}_{3}\mathcal{D}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\eta\mathcal{D}(\not{T}_{34},\not{T})+(\not{T}_{34},\not{T})\mathcal{D}\eta+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\underline{\beta}+\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\eta,\underline{\eta})\underline{\beta}+\lvert u\rvert\sigma\underline{\beta}+\omega\mathcal{D}\underline{\beta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\underline{\beta}+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\beta}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\underline{\beta}.

(8.17)

For
\Psi\in\{\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta}\}
, we bound

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})^{2}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\lVert{\lvert u\rvert\sigma\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{\omega\mathcal{D}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1,

(8.18)

using the bootstrap assumptions on
\lVert{\mathcal{D}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, together with the results of Sections
4
and
6
and Subsection
8.1
. In particular, we bound

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1,

where we have used the bootstrap
\lVert{\mathcal{D}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq C_{2}
and the results on
(\eta,\underline{\eta})
from section
6
(even though just using the bootstrap assumption for
(\eta,\underline{\eta})
would also be enough). Similarly,

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})^{2}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\eta,\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1,

where we also use the results of Section
8.1
on curvature in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
. Moreover,

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert\sigma\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\sigma}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lesssim 1.

For the terms involving
\lVert{\psi\mathcal{D}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}
, we bound
\psi
in
\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}
and
\mathcal{D}\Psi
in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
, while for the term
\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}
, we bound
\underline{\eta}
in
\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}
and
(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)
together with
\Psi
in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}
. Finally, for the term involving
\lVert{(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}
, we bound both terms in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}.
For each
\Psi\in\{\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta}\}
, we give an explanation for the most difficult terms. For
\beta
, we have

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}_{2}[\alpha].

(8.19)

Similarly,

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{44}]\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}V}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

(8.20)

For
\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}
, we have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}=\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{4}].

(8.21)

For
\rho
, we have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{R}_{2}[\beta]\lesssim\frac{R}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1,

(8.22)

while for the borderline term

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}\big(\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}\big)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\frac{1}{\Omega^{2}}-1\big)\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{a}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\big)+1\lesssim\mathcal{V}_{1}[\not{T}_{44}]+\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{44}]+1.

(8.23)

Moreover,

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{34}].

(8.24)

For
\sigma
, we have

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\big(\mathcal{R}_{2}[\beta]+\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{4}]\big)\lesssim 1.

(8.25)

For both
\rho
and
\sigma
, we have the last borderline term

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\alpha\mathcal{D}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]\mathcal{R}_{1}[\alpha]+\mathcal{O}_{1,4}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]\big(\mathcal{R}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\alpha]+\mathcal{R}_{0}[\alpha]\big),

(8.26)

which is bounded by a constant depending on
\mathcal{R}_{0},\mathcal{R}_{1},\mathcal{R}_{2}
because of the improved control on
\mathcal{O}_{0,\infty}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]
and
\mathcal{O}_{1,4}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]
obtained in Sections
5
,
6
. For
\underline{\beta}
, we have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}(\rho,\sigma)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{R}_{2}[\rho,\sigma]\lesssim\frac{R}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

(8.27)

The last difficult term is

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{a}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}=\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{4}].

(8.28)

It is easy to see that all other remaining terms are
\lesssim 1
.
\square

We continue with an estimate for
\mathcal{D}K
.

Proposition 8.2

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions
2.80
, there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{V}_{1}[\not{T}_{44}]+\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{44}]+\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{34}]+1.

Proof.
Similarly to Proposition
8.1
, we begin by making the bootstrap assumption

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim B_{1},

(8.29)

where
B_{1}
is a large constant.
There holds

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}K=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\beta+\beta\mathcal{D}(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}+\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta})+\underline{\eta}^{2}\mathcal{D}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)+2(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}+\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta})+\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}\not{T}+\not{T}\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}+\not{T}_{34}\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}+\not{T}_{44}\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\big(\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}\big)+\eta\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{4}+\not{T}_{4}\mathcal{D}\eta+\omega\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}+\not{T}_{34}\mathcal{D}\omega+\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}K+K\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}K+\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})^{2}K+\lvert u\rvert\sigma K+\omega\mathcal{D}K+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}K+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}K

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})K:=F.

(8.30)

Consequently,

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert\mathcal{D}K\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,0})}+\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{F}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(8.31)

It is easy to check that
\mathcal{D}K=\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}}
when
\mathcal{D}=\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}
and
\mathcal{D}K=0
otherwise, whence

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert\mathcal{D}K\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,0})}\lesssim 1.

Using the results of Sections
5
,
6
and
8.1
together with the bootstrap assumption (
8.29
) it follows that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}

\displaystyle\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})^{2}K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\lvert u\rvert\sigma K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\omega\mathcal{D}K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(\hat{\chi},tr\chi)\mathcal{D}K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{(\beta,\not{T}_{4})K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(8.32)

For the rest of the terms, we have:

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{R}_{2}[\beta]\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}R}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

(8.33)

The most dangerous terms are:

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{44}],

(8.34)

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert\Omega^{2}}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\frac{1}{\Omega^{2}}-1\big)\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{V}_{1}[\not{T}_{44}]\lesssim 1.

(8.35)

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{V}_{2}[\not{T}_{34}],

(8.36)

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{V}_{1}[\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}],

(8.37)

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{T}_{44}\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lesssim\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{a}\lVert{\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

(8.38)

The rest of the terms are bounded above by
1
using the bootstrap assumptions. For
\mathcal{D}\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{D}^{2}\beta+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\beta+\beta\mathcal{D}(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}+\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta})+\underline{\eta}^{2}\mathcal{D}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)+2(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\eta}+\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}\underline{\eta})+\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}\not{T}+\not{T}\mathcal{D}\hat{\chi}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}+\not{T}_{34}\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}+\not{T}_{44}\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\big(\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{44}\big)+\eta\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{4}+\not{T}_{4}\mathcal{D}\eta+\omega\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{34}+\not{T}_{34}\mathcal{D}\omega+\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{34}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}K+K\mathcal{D}\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)+\lvert u\rvert(\eta,\underline{\eta})^{2}\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)+\lvert u\rvert\sigma\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)+\omega\mathcal{D}\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)+(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)+a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big).

(8.39)

The process for bounding
\lVert{\mathcal{D}\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
is exactly the same as for
\lVert{\mathcal{D}K}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
. The only difference is that, since
K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\equiv 0
on the entire
\underline{H}_{0}
, we have
\lVert K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,0})}=0
and hence there is no longer a need for an
\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}
-term in front of our bootstrap assumption. Instead, we begin with the bootstrap assumption

\lVert{\mathcal{D}\big(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\big)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\tilde{B}_{1}.

(8.40)

This concludes the proof.

\square

Remark 7

Essentially, however, we are only going to need control only on the
(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})-
derivatives of
K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}
.

Let us recall, at this point, the definitions of divergence and curl for a symmetric, covariant tensor
\phi
of arbitrary rank:

(\not{\mbox{div }}\phi)_{A_{1}\dots A_{r}}=\not{\nabla}^{B}\phi_{BA_{1}\dots A_{r}},

(\not{\mbox{curl }}\phi)_{A_{1}\dots A_{r}}=\not{\epsilon}^{BC}\not{\nabla}_{B}\phi_{CA_{1}\dots A_{r}}.

Let us, finally, recall the definition for the trace:

(\operatorname{tr}\hskip 1.42262pt\phi)_{A_{1}\dots A_{r-1}}=\not{g}^{BC}\phi_{BCA_{1}\dots A_{r-1}}.

We are ready to state the main elliptic estimate for Hodge systems.

Lemma 8.1
(Main Elliptic Estimate for Hodge Systems)

We continue to work under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
). Let
\phi
be a totally symmetric,
(r+1)-
covariant tensor field on a metric
2-
sphere
(\mathbb{S}^{2},\gamma)
satisfying

\not{\mbox{div }}\phi=f,\hskip 8.53581pt\not{\mbox{curl }}\phi=g,\hskip 8.53581pt\operatorname{tr}\hskip 1.42262pt\phi=h.

Then, there holds

\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{3}\phi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}(f,g)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{j=0}^{2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}(\phi,h)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

Proof.

Recall the following identity from Chapter 7 in
[
13
]
, that for
\phi,f,g
and
h
as above, there holds

\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\big(\lvert\not{\nabla}\phi\rvert^{2}+(r+1)K\lvert\phi\rvert^{2}\big)\text{d}\mu_{\not{g}}=\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}})}\big(\lvert f\rvert^{2}+\lvert g\rvert^{2}+r\hskip 1.42262ptK\hskip 1.42262pt\lvert h\rvert^{2}\big)\text{d}\mu_{\not{g}}.

(8.41)

To obtain the estimate, we proceed iteratively. Recall, from
[
13
]
, that the symmetrized angular derivative of
\phi
, defined by

(\not{\nabla}\phi)^{s}_{BA_{1}\dots A_{r+1}}:=\frac{1}{r+2}\big(\not{\nabla}_{B}\phi_{A_{1}\dots A_{r}}+\sum_{i=1}^{r+1}\not{\nabla}_{A_{i}}\phi_{A_{1}\dots<A_{i}>B\dots A_{r+1}}\big),

satisfies the div-curl system

\not{\mbox{div }}(\not{\nabla}\phi)^{s}=(\not{\nabla}f)^{s}-\frac{1}{r+2}(\prescript{*}{}{\not{\nabla}}g)^{s}+(r+1)K\phi-\frac{2K}{r+1}(\not{g}\otimes^{s}h),

\not{\mbox{curl }}(\not{\nabla}\phi)^{s}=\frac{r+1}{r+2}(\not{\nabla}g)^{s}+(r+1)\hskip 1.42262ptK(\prescript{*}{}{\phi})^{s},

\operatorname{tr}(\not{\nabla}\phi)^{s}=\frac{2}{r+2}f+\frac{r}{r+2}(\not{\nabla}h)^{s},

where

(\not{g}\otimes^{s}h)_{A_{1}\dots A_{r+1}}=\not{g}_{A_{i}A_{j}}\sum_{i<j=1,\dots,r+1}h_{A_{1}\dots<A_{i}>\dots<A_{j}>\dots A_{r+1}}

and

(\prescript{*}{}{\phi})^{s}_{A_{1}\dots A_{r+1}}:=\frac{1}{r+1}\sum_{i=1}^{r+1}{\not{\epsilon}_{A_{i}}}^{B}\phi_{A_{1}\dots<A_{i}>B\dots A_{r+1}}.

Using the main elliptic identity (
8.41
), we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{2}\phi}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert{\not{\nabla}f}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\lVert{\not{\nabla}g}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\lVert{K(\lvert\not{\nabla}\phi\rvert^{2}+\lvert f\rvert^{2}+\lvert\not{\nabla}h\rvert^{2}}\rVert_{L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{K\phi}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\lVert{Kh}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}.

(8.42)

Iterating this procedure and using (
8.41
), we hence have

\displaystyle\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{3}\phi}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{2}f}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{2}g}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\lVert{(\phi,h)\not{\nabla}K}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\lVert{K(\not{\nabla}\phi,\not{\nabla}h)}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{Kf}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\lVert{K(\not{\nabla}f)^{2}}\rVert_{L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\lVert{K(\not{\nabla}g)^{2}}\rVert_{L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\lVert{K(\not{\nabla}^{2}\phi)^{2}}\rVert_{L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{K(\not{\nabla}^{2}h)^{2}}\rVert_{L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\lVert{K^{3}(\phi^{2},h^{2})}\rVert_{L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(8.43)

By decomposing
K=K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}+\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}
and using Hölder’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{3}\phi}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{2}f}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{2}g}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{4}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}\phi,\not{\nabla}h}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{2}\phi,\not{\nabla}f,\not{\nabla}g,\not{\nabla}^{2}h}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{6}}\lVert{\phi,h}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{4}}\lVert{f}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lVert{\not{\nabla}(K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}})}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}\lVert{(\phi,h)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\lVert{K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}\lVert{\not{\nabla}\phi,\not{\nabla}h,f}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{2}\phi,\not{\nabla}f,\not{\nabla}g,\not{\nabla}^{2}h}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert{K-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}}\rVert_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{3}\lVert{\phi,h}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2},

(8.44)

which gives the desired result after translation to scale-invariant norms.

\square

Proposition 8.3

There exists a constant
C(\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V})
, depending only on the curvature and energy norms, such that there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim C(\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V}),\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim C(\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V}).

Proof.
We begin by making the auxiliary bootstrap assumptions

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\leq O_{3},\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\leq O_{3},

(8.45)

where
O_{3}\leq a^{\frac{1}{320}}
.
Recall the transport equation for
\operatorname{tr}\chi
:

\not{\nabla}_{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi=-\frac{1}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\chi)^{2}-\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert^{2}-\not{T}_{44}

Commuting this equation with
\not{\nabla}
and then with
\mathcal{D}^{i}
,
i=0,1,2
yields

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{i}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}\not{T}_{44}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle T_{1}+\dots+T_{10}.

(8.46)

We therefore have, for
i=0,1,2
:

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{10}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{T_{j}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(8.47)

Of the terms on the right-hand side, the most borderline ones are essentially the ones with top-order derivatives. Beginning with the first one, we have:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\psi_{g},\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{\mathcal{D}\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{(\psi_{g},\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{(\psi_{g},\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})\mathcal{D}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\psi_{g},\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{(\psi_{g},\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{\mathcal{D}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(8.48)

Here we have simply conditioned on whether
i=0,1,2
. For the first term herein, there holds:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime\prime}\leq 1}\lVert\mathcal{D}^{2}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime\prime}})}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime\prime}\leq 1}\lVert\mathcal{D}^{2}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime\prime}})}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}(\mathcal{R}_{2}[\alpha]+1)(\mathcal{R}_{2}[\alpha]+1+O_{3})+\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}(\mathcal{R}_{2}[\alpha]+1)\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a\not{\nabla}^{3}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big).

(8.49)

Given the
\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}
bounds on up to two derivatives of Ricci coefficients obtained in the previous Sections, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}(R+V)}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a\not{\nabla}^{3}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{aC(\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V})}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a\not{\nabla}^{3}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{aC(\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V})}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a\not{\nabla}^{3}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)+1.

(8.50)

For the second term, there holds:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{aC(\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V})}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}C(\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V})}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{aC(\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V})}{\lvert u\rvert}\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)+1,

(8.51)

where we have used the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), (
8.45
) and the results of the previous sections. The rest of the terms in (
8.48
) are controlled above by
1
, using the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
) and the results of Sections
4
,
5
,
6
and
7.2
. We finally arrive at

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{2}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}(\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{\nabla}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}(\mathcal{R}_{2}[\alpha]+1)\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a\not{\nabla}^{3}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)+1.

(8.52)

For the second term in (
8.2
), we have

\sum_{i=0}^{2}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}\not{T}_{44}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lesssim 1,

(8.53)

where we have used the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
) involving the energy norms for Vlasov. The rest of the terms in (
8.2
) are similarly bounded above by 1, using the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
) and the results of Sections
4
-
6
and
7.2
. We therefore arrive at the important inequality

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}(\mathcal{R}_{2}[\alpha]+1)\big(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a\not{\nabla}^{3}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\big)+1.

(8.54)

We are, thus, inclined to obtain control on
\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.
Recall the elliptic equation for
\hat{\chi}
:

\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\hat{\chi}=\frac{1}{2}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi-\hat{\chi}\cdot(\eta-\underline{\eta})-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\not{g}\cdot(\eta-\underline{\eta})-\beta-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{4}.

(8.55)

Using the elliptic Proposition
8.1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert{a\not{\nabla}^{3}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{0\leq i\leq 3}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{3}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\sum_{0\leq i\leq 2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\beta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq i\leq 2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\not{T}_{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq i\leq 2\\
i_{1}+i_{2}=i\end{subarray}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}^{i_{2}}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq i\leq 2}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}.

(8.56)

Integrating along
H_{u}
and dividing by
a^{\frac{1}{2}}
, we have

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a\not{\nabla}^{3}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a\not{\nabla}^{3}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+1.

(8.57)

Plugging this back to
(
8.52
) and then again back to (
8.57
), we obtain the result in the case
\mathcal{D}^{2}=(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}
. If
\not{\nabla}_{4}
appears in
\mathcal{D}^{2}
, then we can use the structure equation for
\hat{\chi}
to reduce the number of derivatives by
1
. This only produces terms that have been estimated at previous steps. Similarly, if
\mathcal{D}^{2}=(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}
, then we use the commutation formula to estimate

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\big(\lVert{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{2}\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\lVert{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\lvert u\rvert[\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}]\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{2}(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\text{terms that have been estimated at previous steps}.

(8.58)

To estimate
\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{2}(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}
, we use the commutation formula for
[\not{\nabla}_{3},\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,]
along with Proposition
8.1
:

\displaystyle\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})\hat{\chi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\hat{\chi}+\lvert u\rvert[\not{\nabla}_{3},\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,]\hat{\chi}=\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}\big(\frac{1}{2}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi-\hat{\chi}\cdot(\eta-\underline{\eta})-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\not{g}\cdot(\eta-\underline{\eta})-\beta-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{4}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\big(-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\hat{\chi}-\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}-(\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{3})\hat{\chi}+\frac{1}{2}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}_{3}\hat{\chi}-(\hat{\chi}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi)\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}+(\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\hskip 1.42262pt\eta\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}\big).

(8.59)

Hence

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{2}(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{1}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}(\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3})\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}+\text{lower order terms}.

Putting everything together, for
\mathcal{D}^{2}=(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3}
, we arrive at

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+1.

(8.60)

Proceeding in the same way, we can show that the above inequality holds for any choice of
\mathcal{D}^{2}=\mathcal{D}_{1}\mathcal{D}_{2}
. We therefore obtain that there exists a constant
C(\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V})
such that

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim C(\mathcal{R},\mathcal{V}).

(8.61)

Putting this back to (
8.54
), we arrive at the result.

\square

Elliptic control on
\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi
, in turn, allows to obtain control on the elliptic norm of
\eta
. This is the content of the next Proposition.

Proposition 8.4

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap bounds (
2.80
), there exists a constant
C
depending only the curvature norms
\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}}
, such that there holds

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{\nabla}\eta}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim C(\mathcal{R},\underline{\mathcal{R}}).

Proof.

Let us introduce the quantity

\mu:=-\not{\mbox{div }}\eta-\rho+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\frac{1}{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}.

We wish to obtain a
\not{\nabla}_{4}
evolution equation for
\mu
. For a
1-
form U, recall the commutation formula (see for example
[
4
]
):

[\not{\nabla}_{4},\not{\mbox{div }}]U=-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\mbox{div }}U-\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{\nabla}U-(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{4})\cdot U+\frac{1}{2}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}_{4}U-\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptU+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262ptU.

(8.62)

We explicitly compute:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\eta=

\displaystyle\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,(\not{\nabla}_{4}\eta)+[\not{\nabla}_{4},\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,]\eta=\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\big(\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi(\underline{\eta}-\eta)+\hat{\chi}\cdot(\underline{\eta}-\eta)-\beta-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{4}\big)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\eta-\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{\nabla}\eta-(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{4})\eta+\frac{1}{2}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}_{4}\eta-\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\eta+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\eta.

(8.63)

Moreover,

\not{\nabla}_{4}\rho=-\frac{3}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\rho+\not{\mbox{div }}\beta-\frac{1}{2}\underline{\hat{\chi}}^{\sharp}\cdot\alpha+(\underline{\eta},\beta)-\frac{1}{4}\big(\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{T}_{44}-\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{34}+2\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{T}_{44}+\bcancel{\omega\not{T}_{34}}+2(\underline{\eta}-\eta)\cdot\not{T}_{4}\big).

(8.64)

For the rest of the terms, we have

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}^{AB}\big(-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{AB}+(\not{\nabla}_{A}\underline{\eta}_{B}+\not{\nabla}_{B}\underline{\eta}_{A}-\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}_{AB})-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hat{\chi}_{AB}+2\underline{\eta}_{A}\underline{\eta}_{B}-\lvert\underline{\eta}\rvert^{2}\not{g}_{AB}+\not{T}_{AB}\big)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}^{AB}\big(-\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}_{AB}-\alpha_{AB},\big).

(8.65)

Finally,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\big(\frac{1}{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\big)=\frac{1}{4}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\big(-\frac{1}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\chi)^{2}-\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert^{2}-\not{T}_{44}\big)+\frac{1}{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi\big(2\mbox{div }\underline{\eta}+2\lvert\underline{\eta}\rvert^{2}+\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\rho-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{34}\big)

(8.66)

After a lengthy but straightforward calculation, adding
-
(
8.63
)
-
(
8.64
)+(
8.65
)+(
8.66
), the terms

\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\beta,-\frac{1}{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}^{\sharp}\cdot\alpha,

as well as

\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta},\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{\nabla}\underline{\eta}

crucially cancel out. We finally obtain

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mu+\operatorname{tr}\chi\mu=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\underline{\eta}-\eta)\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi-2\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{\nabla}\eta+(\eta-\underline{\eta})\hskip 1.42262pt\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\hat{\chi}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\not{T}_{4}-(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{4})\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\big(-\chi\cdot(\eta-\underline{\eta})-\beta-\frac{1}{2}\not{T}_{4}\big)-\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\eta+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt\eta+\frac{1}{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi\rho

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\underline{\eta},\beta)-\frac{1}{4}\big(\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{T}_{44}-\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{34}+2\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{T}_{44}+2(\underline{\eta}-\eta)\cdot\not{T}_{4}\big)-\frac{1}{4}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\eta}\cdot\underline{\eta}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{T}+\frac{1}{8}\operatorname{tr}\chi\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\operatorname{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{4}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}(-\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert^{2}-\not{T}_{44})+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\lvert\underline{\eta}\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\operatorname{tr}\chi^{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}-\frac{1}{8}\operatorname{tr}\chi\not{T}_{34}:=M_{0}.

(8.67)

By bounding

\lVert{\mu}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{M_{0}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime},

(8.68)

using the bootstrap assumptions, we get

\lVert{\mu}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

(8.69)

Commuting with
\mathcal{D}
, we get the equation (for
i=1,2
)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{i}\mu=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}M_{0}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\mu

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\mu

\displaystyle+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\mu

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\mu

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\mu

\displaystyle+
