in a manner that ensures integrability. More precisely, since the natural quantity appearing in the
transport operator is
|u|V_{4+A}f
, it is necessary that the rescaled coefficient
|u|^{-1}C^{4+B}_{A}

is locally integrable. A detailed inspection of the structure of
C^{4+B}_{A}
, combined with the
pointwise estimates for the Ricci coefficients and curvature components yield the bound

\displaystyle|u|^{-1}\,\big|C^{4+B}_{A}\big|\;\lesssim\;\frac{a^{1/2}(\Gamma,\mathcal{R})}{|u|^{2}}.

(9.98)

This estimate is precisely at the threshold required to guarantee integrability along the null
hypersurfaces and thus suffices for the closure of the weighted energy hierarchy. The last error coefficient
C^{3}_{A}
reads

\displaystyle C^{3}_{A}=\bigg[2(\eta_{A}+\underline{\eta}_{A})p^{4}+\bigg(\big[\not{\Gamma}^{C}_{AB}p^{B}+\big({\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{A}}^{C}+\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}{\delta_{A}}^{C}\big)p^{3}+{\chi_{A}}^{C}p^{4}\big]\frac{\not{g}_{CD}p^{D}}{2p^{3}}\bigg)-\big(\frac{1}{2}\chi_{AB}p^{B}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}\big)\frac{p^{4}}{p^{3}}\bigg].

This term exhibits superior integrable decay without the necessity of any cancellation and is easy to estimate. It reads

\displaystyle|C^{3}_{A}|\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}.

(9.99)

Let the hypotheses and notation of the preceding sections hold and assume the bootstrap bound displayed as (
9.75
). We will prove the following bound

\frac{|u|^{2}}{a^{2}}\,\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(u)\lesssim\frac{|u_{\infty}|^{2}}{a^{2}}\,\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(u_{\infty})+(\Gamma,\mathcal{R})\,\mathcal{V}\,\frac{a^{1/2}}{|u|}.

(9.100)

Consequently, under the bootstrap assumption (
9.75
) there exists
C^{\prime}>0
such that

\frac{|u|^{2}}{a^{2}}\,\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(u)\lesssim\frac{|u_{\infty}|^{2}}{a^{2}}\,\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(u_{\infty})+1

(9.101)

We begin by collecting the error estimates obtained previously. There exists an absolute constant
C>0
for which the following differential inequality holds (for
s
in the corresponding parameter interval):

\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(s)\lesssim\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(s_{\infty})+\int_{s_{\infty}}^{s}|\mathcal{E}(s^{\prime})|\,ds^{\prime},

(9.102)

with

|\mathcal{E}(s^{\prime})|\lesssim\Bigg(\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\int_{P}|\mathbf{V}_{A}f|^{2}+\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{3}}\int_{P}\bigl|\,|u|\,\mathbf{V}_{0}f\bigr|^{2}\\
+\Bigl[\frac{a^{3/2}\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{3}}+\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\Bigr]\int_{P}|\mathbf{V}_{4}f|^{2}+\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\int_{P}|\mathbf{V}_{3}f|^{2}+\frac{(\Gamma,\mathcal{R})\,a^{1/2}}{|u|^{2}}\int_{P}\bigl|\,|u|\,\mathbf{V}_{4+B}f\bigr|^{2}\Bigg).

(9.103)

By the bootstrap control of
\mathfrak{V}_{1}
we have the moment bounds (uniform in
s
)

\int_{P}|\mathbf{V}_{A}f|^{2}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}},\quad\int_{P}\bigl|\,|u|\,\mathbf{V}_{4+A}f\bigr|^{2}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}},\quad\int_{P}\bigl|\,|u|\,\mathbf{V}_{0}f\bigr|^{2}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}},

(9.104)

and

\int_{P}|\mathbf{V}_{4}f|^{2}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}},\qquad\int_{P}|\mathbf{V}_{3}f|^{2}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}.

Inserting these bounds into (
9.103
) and collecting like terms yields (after absorbing absolute constants)

|\mathcal{E}(s^{\prime})|\leq(\Gamma,\mathcal{R})\,\mathcal{V}\left(\frac{a^{5/2}}{|u|^{4}}+\frac{a^{7/2}}{|u|^{5}}\right).

(9.105)

Next we change variables from
s
to
u
using Proposition
2.1
, which gives
\dfrac{d}{ds}=p^{3}\dfrac{d}{du}
. we deduce from (
9.102
) and (
9.105
) that

\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(u)\lesssim\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(u_{\infty})+(\Gamma,\mathcal{R})\,\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\!\left(\frac{a^{5/2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}+\frac{a^{7/2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{5}}\right)\,du^{\prime}.

Carrying out the elementary integrals in
u^{\prime}
yields

\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(u)\leq\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(u_{\infty})+(\Gamma,\mathcal{R})\,\mathcal{V}\left(\frac{a^{5/2}}{|u|^{3}}+\frac{a^{7/2}}{|u|^{4}}\right).

Multiplying both sides by
\dfrac{|u|^{2}}{a^{2}}
we obtain

\frac{|u|^{2}}{a^{2}}\,\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(u)\leq\frac{|u_{\infty}|^{2}}{a^{2}}\,\mathfrak{V}^{1,2}_{1}(u_{\infty})+(\Gamma,\mathcal{R})\,\mathcal{V}\left(\frac{a^{1/2}}{|u|}+\frac{a^{3/2}}{|u|^{2}}\right).

Since
a
is fixed in our regime and
|u|^{-1}\geq|u|^{-2}
for the relevant
u
, the right-most bracket is bounded by
\lesssim\,a^{1/2}/|u|
, which proves (
9.100
). Finally, invoking the bootstrap assumption (
9.75
) to control the left-hand side uniformly allows us to absorb the remaining term and conclude (
9.101
).

Now we show the estimates for a vector field in the weighted category, namely
V_{4+A}
. Consider
V_{4+A}f
i.e., we want to estimate

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}_{1}:=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{4+A}f|^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}.

(9.106)

In reality, we want to estimate
|u|^{2}\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}_{1}
, but do so indirectly.
Recall lemma
9.1
and set
A(s):=\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}_{1}
and obtain

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}_{1}(s)\lesssim\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}_{1}(s_{\infty})+\int_{s_{\infty}}^{s}\Bigg(\int_{p}V_{4+A}fX[V_{4+A}f]\sqrt{\det(\not{g})}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg)ds^{{}^{\prime}}.

(9.107)

We control the error term. Recall the commutation formula from proposition
9.1

\displaystyle[X,V_{(4+B)}]=

\displaystyle\Bigg[-\frac{p^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{\delta_{B}}^{A}\big(\frac{\chi_{AC}p^{C}}{2p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\big)-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\Gamma^{3}_{\mu\nu}\partial_{\overline{p}^{B}}(p^{\mu}p^{\nu})\Bigg]\bf{V_{(0)}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{p^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{\delta_{B}}^{A}\bf{V_{(A)}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\not{g}_{BC}p^{C}}{2\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\bf{V_{(3)}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\Bigg[-\frac{p^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{\delta_{B}}^{A}\bigg(\frac{\chi_{AC}p^{C}}{2p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\bigg)-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\Gamma^{3}_{\mu\nu}\partial_{\overline{p}^{B}}(p^{\mu}p^{\nu})\Bigg]\bf{V_{(4)}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\Bigg[-\frac{p^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{\delta_{B}}^{C}\hskip 1.42262pt\lvert u\rvert^{2}\big(\frac{\not{\Gamma}^{A}_{CD}p^{D}}{p^{3}}+{\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{C}}^{A}+{\chi_{C}}^{A}\frac{p^{4}}{p^{3}}+\frac{1}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}){\delta_{C}}^{A}\big)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\not{g}_{BC}p^{C}}{2}\bigg(\frac{{\chi_{C}}^{A}p^{C}}{p^{3}}+2\underline{\eta}^{A}\bigg)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\bigg[{\delta_{B}}^{A}\big((-\Gamma^{3}_{\mu\nu}p^{\mu}p^{\nu}+\frac{2(p^{3})^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\big)+\Gamma^{A}_{\mu\nu}p^{3}\partial_{\overline{p}^{B}}(p^{\mu}p^{\nu})\bigg]\Bigg]\bf{V_{(4+A)}}.

(9.108)

This expression is relatively simple, and estimating the error coefficients is straightforward

\displaystyle|C^{0}_{4+B}|=\big|-\frac{p^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{\delta_{B}}^{A}\big(\frac{\chi_{AC}p^{C}}{2p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\big)-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\Gamma^{3}_{\mu\nu}\partial_{\overline{p}^{B}}(p^{\mu}p^{\nu})\big|\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}},

(9.109)

\displaystyle|C^{A}_{4+B}|=|-\frac{p^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\delta^{A}_{B}|\lesssim\lvert u\rvert^{-2},\qquad|C^{3}_{4+B}|=|-\frac{\not{g}_{BC}p^{C}}{2\lvert u\rvert^{2}}|\lesssim|u|^{-2},

(9.110)

\displaystyle|C^{4}_{4+B}|=|-\frac{p^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{\delta_{B}}^{A}\bigg(\frac{\chi_{AC}p^{C}}{2p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\bigg)-\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\Gamma^{3}_{\mu\nu}\partial_{\overline{p}^{B}}(p^{\mu}p^{\nu})|\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}},

(9.111)

and lastly

\displaystyle C^{4+A}_{4+B}

\displaystyle=-\delta_{B}^{C}\left(\frac{p^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\right)\left(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{\not{\Gamma}^{A}_{CD}p^{D}}{p^{3}}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{C}^{A}+\chi_{C}^{A}\frac{p^{4}}{p^{3}}+\frac{1}{2}\left(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\right)\delta_{C}^{A}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\not{g}_{BC}p^{C}}{2}\left(\frac{(\chi_{C}^{A}-e_{C}(b^{A}))p^{C}}{p^{3}}+2\underline{\eta}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\delta_{B}^{A}\left(-\Gamma^{3}_{\mu\nu}p^{\mu}p^{\nu}+\frac{2(p^{3})^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\right)+\Gamma^{A}_{\mu\nu}p^{3}\,\partial_{\overline{p}^{B}}(p^{\mu}p^{\nu}).

(9.112)

We explicitly calculate

\displaystyle\Gamma^{3}_{\mu\nu}p^{\mu}p^{\nu}=\underline{\omega}(p^{3})^{2}+(\eta_{A}-\underline{\eta}_{A})p^{A}p^{3}+\chi_{AB}p^{A}p^{B}

(9.113)

and

\displaystyle\Gamma^{A}_{\mu\nu}p^{3}\frac{\partial}{\partial\overline{p}^{B}}(p^{\mu}p^{\nu})=\chi^{A}_{B}p^{4}p^{3}+\underbrace{\Bigg(\underline{\chi}^{A}_{B}-e_{A}(b^{B})\Bigg)p^{3}p^{3}}_{A}+\not{\Gamma}^{A}_{BC}p^{C}p^{3}+\chi^{A}_{C}P^{C}+\underline{\eta}^{A}p^{3}.

(9.114)

There are several points to be noted here. First, note that
e_{A}(b^{B})
appears with
\underline{\chi}^{A}_{B}
and therefore the form
\Gamma^{B}_{3A}
and can be controlled through the
\not{\nabla}_{4}
evolution equation. But more importantly, in the trace-tracefree decomposition of
\Gamma^{A}_{3B}=\underline{\chi}^{A}_{B}-e_{A}(b^{B})
, the non-integrable term
\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}-e_{A}(b^{A})
needs to be taken care of. But notice that this term appears with a good sign since
\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}-e_{A}(b^{A})=-\frac{2}{|u|}+(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|}-e_{A}(b^{A}))
and therefore the non-integrable part in term
A
is estimated as

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{P}}V_{4+A}f\Bigg(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}-e_{A}(b^{A})\Bigg)p^{3}p^{3}V_{4+B}f\sqrt{\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{|u|}\int_{P}|V_{4+A}f|^{2}(p^{3})^{2}\sqrt{g}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+\Bigg(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|}-e_{A}(b^{A})\Bigg)\int_{P}|V_{4+A}f|^{2}(p^{3})^{2}\sqrt{g}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}.

(9.115)

Finally, we collect every term, yielding

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}_{1}(s)\lesssim\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}_{1}(s_{\infty})+

\displaystyle\int_{s_{\infty}}^{s}\Bigg(\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\int_{P}|V_{A}f||V_{4+B}f|dP+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}\int_{P}|V_{0}f||V_{4+B}f|+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}\int_{P}|V_{4}f||V_{4+B}f|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\int_{P}|V_{3}f||V_{4+B}f|+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\int_{P}|V_{4+A}f|^{2}dP\Bigg)ds^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{s_{\infty}}^{s}\Bigg(\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\int_{P}|V_{A}f||V_{4+B}f|dP+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}\int_{P}|V_{0}f||V_{4+B}f|+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}\int_{P}|V_{4}f||V_{4+B}f|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\int_{P}|V_{3}f||V_{4+B}f|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|u|^{3}}\int_{P}|V_{3}f||uV_{4+B}f|dP+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{4}}\int_{P}|uV_{4+A}f|^{2}dP\Bigg)ds^{{}^{\prime}}.

(9.116)

First, note that we estimate
V_{4+A}f
at the very end, by which time estimates have already been obtained for the rest of the vector fields i.e.,

\displaystyle\int_{P}|V_{A}f|^{2}dp\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}^{0}}{|u|^{2}},\int_{P}||u|V_{0}f|^{2}dp\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}^{0}}{|u|^{2}},~\int_{P}|V_{4}f|^{2}dp\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}^{0}}{|u|^{2}},~\int_{P}|V_{3}f|^{2}dp\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}^{0}}{|u|^{2}}

(9.117)

and we use the boot-strap on the
|u|V_{4+A}f
,

\displaystyle\int_{P}||u|V_{4+A}f|^{2}dp\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}},

(9.118)

which yields

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}(s)\lesssim\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}(s_{\infty})+\int_{s_{\infty}}^{s}\bigg[\frac{a\mathcal{V}^{0}}{|u|^{3}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{V}^{0}}{|u|^{4}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}}+\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{6}}\bigg]ds^{{}^{\prime}}.

(9.119)

Now recall
\frac{d}{ds}=p^{3}\frac{d}{du}
(Proposition
2.1
) to obtain, under the bootstrap,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|^{4}}{a^{2}}\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}(s)\lesssim\frac{|u_{\infty}|^{4}}{a^{2}}\mathfrak{V}^{4+A}(s_{\infty})+\mathcal{V}^{0}+1.

(9.120)

Therefore, all the estimates collectively yield

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}(u)\lesssim\mathcal{V}^{0}+1

(9.121)

uniformly in
u
,
which completes the proof. For the estimates for
\mathfrak{V}^{s}(u)
, we use the second transport inequality
9.134
in the lemma
9.1
and proceed in exactly the same fashion. For brevity, we do not repeat the computations since it would be repetitive. This concludes the proof of the estimates for the first derivatives of the distribution function
f
.

\square

9.2
Second Derivative control of Vlasov

Now we move on to the second derivative estimates for the Vlasov field. To this end, we need to compute the second commutator. Let
f
be a smooth scalar function on phase space and let
X
,
V_{A}
, and
V_{B}
denote smooth vector fields satisfying the necessary regularity and geometric compatibility assumptions (e.g., adapted to a null foliation or a geometric frame bundle over spacetime). Then the following elementary identity holds:

X(V_{A}V_{B}f)=V_{A}\left(X(V_{B}f)\right)-[V_{A},X](V_{B}f).

(9.122)

We now compute each term on the right-hand side of (
9.122
) in terms of the frame
\{V_{I}\}_{I=0}^{6}
, assumed to span the relevant tangent bundle. Specifically, we expand the commutator
[X,V_{A}]
as follows:

[X,V_{A}]=\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{A}V_{I},

(9.123)

for some smooth scalar coefficient functions
C^{I}_{A}
, depending on spacetime position and momentum variables.

Applying (
9.123
), we obtain the action of the commutator on
V_{B}f
:

[X,V_{A}](V_{B}f)=\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{A}V_{I}(V_{B}f).

(9.124)

Next, we compute the full second-order derivative term
V_{A}(X(V_{B}f))
. Using the product rule and the expansion of
X(V_{B})
, we write:

V_{A}\left(X(V_{B}f)\right)=\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{B}V_{A}\left(V_{I}f\right)+\sum_{I=0}^{6}V_{A}(C^{I}_{B})V_{I}f.

(9.125)

Combining (
9.124
) and (
9.125
) into (
9.122
), we obtain the following identity:

X(V_{A}V_{B}f)\sim\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{A}V_{I}V_{B}f+\sum_{I=0}^{6}V_{A}(C^{I}_{B})V_{I}f.

(9.126)

This identity expresses the commuted second-order operator
X(V_{A}V_{B}f)
entirely in terms of the frame
\{V_{I}\}
, the coefficient functions
C^{I}_{A}
, and their derivatives. Now we already have explicit expressions for the
C^{I}_{A}
. At the level of the second derivative of the Vlasov field, we therefore need to estimate the first derivatives of
C^{I}_{B}
, which would involve first derivatives of the Weyl curvature, first derivatives of the connection coefficients, and the first derivatives of the Vlasov matter stress-energy tensor. We need a systematic way to compute the typical term
V_{A}(C^{I}_{B})
. The following lemma accomplishes it.

Lemma 9.2

Let
\psi=(\psi_{0},\dots,\psi_{k})
denote a finite collection of smooth scalar or tensor fields (on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
) depicting the Ricci coefficients of the Weyl curvature components and let
\not{p}
denote the pair
(p^{1},p^{2})
of angular momentum components,
p^{4}
the mass shell component determined by the constraint relation, and
\not{g}=\not{g}_{AB}
the induced metric on the sphere
S_{u,\underline{u}}
. Consider an entity of the form

G:=f_{1}(\psi)\cdot\,f_{2}(\not{p})\cdot\,f_{3}(p^{4})\cdot\,f_{4}(\not{g}),

where
f_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4}
are smooth functions of their respective arguments. Then the action of the derivative
V_{D}
satisfies the identity:

\displaystyle V_{D}(G)

\displaystyle=\Bigg[\left(\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{\partial f_{1}}{\partial\psi_{i}}\not{\nabla}\psi_{i}\right)f_{4}(\not{g})+\left(\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{\partial f_{1}}{\partial\psi_{i}}\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{i}\right)f_{4}(\not{g})+f_{1}(\psi)f_{4}^{\prime}(\not{g})\,\not{\Gamma}\,\not{g}\Bigg]f_{2}(\not{p})f_{3}(p^{4})

\displaystyle\quad+f_{1}(\psi)f_{4}(\not{g})\Bigg[\left(\hat{\chi}\,p^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\,p^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,p^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p}\right)\left(f_{2}^{\prime}(\not{p})f_{3}(p^{4})+f_{2}(\not{p})\,\not{g}\,\not{p}\,f_{3}^{\prime}(p^{4})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\underline{\eta}\,p^{3}+\hat{\chi}\,\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,\not{p}\right)f_{2}(\not{p})\,f_{3}^{\prime}(p^{4})\,p^{4}+\frac{p^{3}}{|u|}\left(f_{2}^{\prime}(\not{p})f_{3}(p^{4})+\not{g}\,\not{p}\,f_{2}(\not{p})\,f_{3}^{\prime}(p^{4})\right)\Bigg]_{D}.

(9.127)

Moreover, if each
\psi_{i}
is a scalar function (i.e., independent of the angular variables), then the term involving
\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{i}
vanishes identically:

\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{\partial f_{1}}{\partial\psi_{i}}\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{i}=0.

The subscript in equation
9.2
denotes the index that is not summed over i.e., for an entity
\alpha^{A}_{D}\eta_{A}
we write
[\alpha\eta]_{D}
.

Proof.

Straightforward computation using the definition of the vector fields
V
.

\square

Equipped with Lemma
9.2
, we proceed to estimate the second derivative of the Vlasov field. First, recall the definition of the norm that we want to control (together with the definition of the set
\tilde{V}
in (
9.5
)):

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}_{2}:=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\sum_{V_{(1)},V_{(2)}\in\tilde{V}}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\tilde{V}_{(1)}\tilde{V}_{(2)}f|^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262ptd^{2}\theta.

The following proposition accomplishes it:

Proposition 9.3
(Second derivative estimates for the Vlasov distribution function)

Let
f:\mathcal{M}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\to\mathbb{R}
denote a classical solution to the Vlasov equation posed on a smooth, globally hyperbolic Lorentzian manifold
(\mathcal{M},g)
, foliated by a double null coordinate system
(u,\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})
. Suppose that
f
satisfies the transport equation along the null geodesic flow and that all relevant Ricci coefficients, curvature components, and geometric quantities satisfy the bootstrap assumptions described in Section
3.1
.

Let
\mathfrak{V}_{2}
denote the second-order energy quantity associated to
f
, defined by
\mathfrak{V}
,
built from commutation vector fields adapted to the geometric foliation as follows

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}_{2}:=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\sum_{V_{(1)},V_{(2)}\in\tilde{V}}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\tilde{V}_{(1)}\tilde{V}_{(2)}f|^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262ptd^{2}\theta.

Denote, moreover, the initial data expression

\mathcal{I}^{0}_{2}:=\mathfrak{V}_{2}:=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\sum_{V_{(1)},V_{(2)}\in\tilde{V}}\sup_{\underline{u}}\int_{S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\tilde{V}_{(1)}\tilde{V}_{(2)}f|^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262ptd^{2}\theta<\infty.

Then the following estimates hold

\mathfrak{V}_{2}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{I}^{0}_{2}.

(9.128)

where the involved constants are purely numerical.

Proof.

First, we make the bootstrap assumption

\mathfrak{V}_{2}\leq\mathcal{V},

(9.129)

where
\mathcal{V}
is large enough so that

\mathcal{I}^{0}\ll\mathcal{V}

but also
\mathcal{V}\leq a^{\frac{1}{320}}
. Once again, we will only provide a detailed proof for the borderline terms. We consider
V_{A}V_{B}f
. More precisely, we want to estimate

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}:=\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{B}f|^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262ptd^{2}\theta.

(9.130)

First, recall the elementary identity

\displaystyle X(V_{A}V_{B}f)=V_{A}(X(V_{B}f))-[V_{A},X](V_{B}f).

(9.131)

Now we can explicitly compute both
V_{A}(X(V_{B}))
and
[V_{A},X](V_{B}f)
. Indeed,

\displaystyle[X,V_{A}]V_{B}f=\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{A}V_{I}V_{B}f,

(9.132)

and

\displaystyle V_{A}(X(V_{B}f))=\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{A}V_{I}V_{B}f+\sum_{I=0}^{6}V_{A}(C^{I}_{B})V_{I}f.

(9.133)

The new terms that we need to estimate now are the
V_{A}(C^{I}_{B})
.
First, recall the transport inequality from lemma
9.1
and set
B:=\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}
,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}(s)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}(s_{\infty})+\int_{s_{\infty}}^{s}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\int_{\pi^{-1}(x)}V_{A}V_{B}f\cdot X[V_{A}V_{B}f]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\,\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\right)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\,dS_{u,\underline{u}}(x)\,ds^{\prime},

(9.134)

where
s_{\infty}=s(u_{\infty})
. The first thing that we need to do is evaluate the error term
X[V_{A}V_{B}f]
. Note that the only non-trivial computations that we have to execute are
V_{D}(C^{I}_{B})
for
I=A,4,3,4+A,0
. To this end, we will use the lemma
9.2
. The idea here is to keep track of the upper and lower indices since they result in the appearance of
\not{g}^{-1}
and
\not{g}
in the component-wise point-wise norm. Then we can use the table
9.1
to control them. Schematically, we show a few terms. First, we have
V_{D}(C^{0}_{A})
which contains both the indices downstairs. Using Lemma
9.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle V_{D}(C^{0}_{A})\supset V_{D}(\psi_{g}\psi_{g}p^{4})_{A}

\displaystyle\sim\bigg[(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g})\,\psi_{g}\,p^{4}+\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{g}\,\psi_{g}\,p^{4}+(\hat{\chi}\,p^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\,p^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,p^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p})\,\psi_{g}\,\psi_{g}\,\not{g}\,\not{p}

\displaystyle\quad+(\underline{\eta}+\hat{\chi}\,\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,\not{p})\,\psi_{g}\,\psi_{g}\,p^{4}+|u|^{-1}\,\psi_{g}\,\psi_{g}\,\not{g}\,\not{p}\bigg]_{DA},

(9.135)

\displaystyle V_{D}(C^{0}_{A})\supset V_{D}(\psi_{g}\,\psi_{g}\,\not{p}^{2})_{A}

\displaystyle\sim\bigg[(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g})\,\psi_{g}\,\not{p}^{2}+\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{g}\,\psi_{g}\,\not{p}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\psi_{g}\,\psi_{g}\left[(\hat{\chi}\,p^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\,p^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,p^{3})-|u|^{-1}\not{p}\right]\bigg]_{DA},

(9.136)

\displaystyle V_{D}(C^{0}_{A})\supset V_{D}(\psi_{g}\,\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,\not{p})_{A}

\displaystyle\sim\bigg[\left[(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g})\,\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+(\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\,\psi_{g}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{g}\right]\,\not{p}

\displaystyle\quad+\psi_{g}\,\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\left(\hat{\chi}\,p^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\,p^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,p^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p}+|u|^{-1}\right)\bigg]_{DA},

(9.137)

\displaystyle V_{D}(C^{0}_{A})\supset V_{D}\left(\psi_{g}\,\not{g}\,\not{p}\,p^{4}\right)_{A}

\displaystyle\sim\bigg[\left[(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g})\,\not{g}+\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{g}+\psi_{g}\,\not{\Gamma}\,\not{g}\right]\,\not{p}\,p^{4}

\displaystyle\quad+\psi_{g}\,\not{g}\Big[(\hat{\chi}\,p^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\,p^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,p^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p})(p^{4}+\not{p}\,\not{g}\,\not{p})

\displaystyle\quad+(\underline{\eta}\,p^{3}+\hat{\chi}\,\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,\not{p})\,\not{p}\,p^{4}+p^{3}\,|u|^{-1}(p^{4}+\not{g}\,\not{p}^{2})\Big]\bigg]_{DA},

(9.138)

\displaystyle V_{D}(C^{0}_{A})\supset V_{D}\left(\psi_{g}\,\not{g}\,\not{p}^{3}\right)_{A}

\displaystyle\sim\bigg[\left[(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g})\,\not{g}+\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{g}\,\not{g}\right]\,\not{p}^{3}+\psi_{g}\,\not{g}\left(\hat{\chi}\,p^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\,p^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,p^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p}\right)\,\not{p}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\psi_{g}\,\not{g}\,p^{3}\,|u|^{-1}\,\not{p}^{2}\bigg]_{DA}.

(9.139)

Notice the decay structure of every term present.
Similarly, there are additional terms in
V_{D}(C^{0}_{A})
with exactly similar schematics, but with better or equal decay bounds. Therefore, we do not include these for the sake of clarity. Next we consider
V_{D}(C^{B}_{A})
. There holds

\displaystyle V_{D}\bigl(C^{B}_{A}\bigr)

\displaystyle\sim\Bigg[\,\not{\nabla}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\;\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}})\;+\;\not{\Gamma}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\;\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}})+\bigl(\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\not{\Gamma}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\bigr)\,p^{4}

\displaystyle\qquad+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\Bigl[\bigl(\hat{\chi}\,p^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\,p^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,p^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p}\bigr)\,\not{g}\,\not{p}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl(\underline{\eta}\,p^{3}+\hat{\chi}\,\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,\not{p}\bigr)\,p^{4}+|u|^{-1}\,\not{g}\,\not{p}\Bigr]+\bigl(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}+\not{\Gamma}\psi_{g}\bigr)\,p^{4}

\displaystyle\qquad+\psi_{g}\Bigl[\bigl(\hat{\chi}\,p^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\,p^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,p^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p}\bigr)\,\not{g}\,\not{p}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl(\underline{\eta}\,p^{3}+\hat{\chi}\,\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,\not{p}\bigr)\,p^{4}+|u|^{-1}\,\not{g}\,\not{p}\Bigr]\,\Bigg]^{B}_{\;DA}\!,

(9.140)

where, once again, we omitted the terms that reappear. In particular, this will be the recurring theme in estimating the error terms with the same decay structure. The next error term
V_{D}(C^{4}_{A})
has the following semi-schematic structure

\displaystyle V_{D}(C^{4}_{A})\sim\Bigg[\bigg[\big(\hat{\chi}\,\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}+\psi_{g}\,\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}+\hat{\chi}\,\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{g}+\psi_{g}\,\not{\Gamma}\,\hat{\chi}\big)p^{4}\,\not{p}

\displaystyle\quad+\psi_{g}\,\hat{\chi}\Big[\big(\hat{\chi}p^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\,p^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,p^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p}\big)(p^{4}+\not{g}\,\not{p}^{2})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\big(\underline{\eta}\,p^{3}+\hat{\chi}\,\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,\not{p}\big)+p^{3}\,|u|^{-1}(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}^{2})\Big]\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg[\big(2\hskip 1.42262pt\psi_{g}\,\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\,\not{g}+2\psi_{g}\,\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{g}\,\not{g}+\psi_{g}^{2}\,\not{\Gamma}\,\not{g}\big)\not{p}\,p^{4}

\displaystyle\quad+\psi_{g}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\,p^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,p^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p})(p^{4}+\not{g}\,\not{p}^{2})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+(\underline{\eta}\,p^{3}+\hat{\chi}\,\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,\not{p})+p^{3}\,|u|^{-1}(p^{4}+\not{g}\,\not{p}^{2})\Big]\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\big(\psi\,\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}+\psi_{g}\,\not{\nabla}\psi+\psi\,\not{\Gamma}\,\psi_{g}+\psi_{g}\,\not{\Gamma}\,\psi\big)\not{g}\,\not{p}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\psi_{g}\,\psi\,\not{g}\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,p^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p})\,\not{p}+p^{3}\hskip 1.42262pt|u|^{-1}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\Big]\bigg]

\displaystyle+\Big[(\not{\nabla}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}+\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\Big]\not{p}+\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})+p^{3}\hskip 1.42262pt|u|^{-1}\Big]

\displaystyle+(\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})+p^{3}\hskip 1.42262pt|u|^{-1}\Big]

\displaystyle+(\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}p^{4}

\displaystyle+\hat{\chi}\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})+(\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+p^{3}\hskip 1.42262pt|u|^{-1}(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\Big]

\displaystyle+\Big[(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi_{g})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}+\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\Big]\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}

\displaystyle+\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})+(\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+p^{3}\hskip 1.42262pt|u|^{-1}\hskip 1.42262pt(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\Big]

\displaystyle+\Big[(\not{\nabla}\not{\Gamma}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\Gamma})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\Big]\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}

\displaystyle+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})(2\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})+p^{3}\hskip 1.42262pt|u|^{-1}\hskip 1.42262pt(2\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\Big]

\displaystyle+(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi_{g})\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\psi_{g}\Big[(\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+p^{3}\hskip 1.42262pt|u|^{-1}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle+(\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}

\displaystyle+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})

\displaystyle\quad+(\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+p^{3}\hskip 1.42262pt|u|^{-1}(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\Big]

\displaystyle+\Big[(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi_{g})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}+\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\Big]\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}

\displaystyle+\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})

\displaystyle\quad+(\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+p^{3}\hskip 1.42262pt|u|^{-1}\hskip 1.42262pt(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\Big]\Bigg]_{DA}.

(9.141)

Lastly, we want to write down the error term
V_{D}(C^{4+B}_{A})
schematically. Once again, we only note the non-repeating terms. The error terms read

\displaystyle V_{D}[C^{4+B}_{A}]

\displaystyle\sim\,

\displaystyle\Bigg[|u|^{2}\Big[(\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}+\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\underline{\eta}+\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\underline{\eta})(p^{4})^{2}

\displaystyle+(\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}+\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\underline{\omega}+\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\underline{\omega})\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}

\displaystyle+(\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\cdot\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}

\displaystyle+(\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{g}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{\nabla}(\not{g}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})+\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi\cdot\not{g}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\cdot\not{\nabla}(\not{g}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}))\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}

\displaystyle+\left[\left(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\operatorname{tr}\chi+\psi_{g}\cdot\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi\right)+\left(\not{\Gamma}\psi_{g}\cdot\operatorname{tr}\chi+\not{\Gamma}\psi_{g}\cdot\operatorname{tr}\chi\right)\right]\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}

\displaystyle+\psi_{g}\operatorname{tr}\chi\cdot\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\cdot(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})

\displaystyle\qquad+(\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\cdot\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+p^{3}\hskip 1.42262pt|u|^{-1}\cdot(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\Big]

\displaystyle+\left[\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{g}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{g}+\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\right]\cdot\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}

\displaystyle+\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{g}\cdot\Big[(\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})

\displaystyle\qquad+(\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{3}+\hat{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\cdot\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+p^{3}|u|^{-1}(p^{4}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})\Big]

\displaystyle+|u|^{2}\Bigg\{\left(\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}+\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\chi+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\chi\right)\cdot\left[\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\underline{\eta}\hskip 1.42262pt(p^{4})^{2}+(\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\right]

\displaystyle\quad+(\hat{\chi}+\operatorname{tr}\chi)\cdot\Big[\left(\not{\nabla}\underline{\omega}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}\right)p^{4}+\left(\not{\nabla}\underline{\eta}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}\right)(p^{4})^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}\right)\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\left(\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\right)\hskip 1.42262pt\not{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\cdot\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\Big]\Bigg\}\Bigg]^{B}_{DA}

(9.142)

Once we have the schematics, we are in a position to estimate the energy
\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}
. The important point to note here is that we need to estimate each type of term separately and carefully. Recall the transport inequality for
\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}
and perform the change of variable
s\mapsto u(s)
together with Proposition
2.1
:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}(u)\lesssim\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}(u_{\infty})+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\hskip 1.42262ptX[V_{A}V_{B}f]\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\big)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.143)

Now recall that the error term contains two types of terms:

\displaystyle X[V_{A}V_{B}f]\sim\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{A}V_{I}V_{B}f+\sum_{I=0}^{6}V_{A}(C^{I}_{B})V_{I}f,

(9.144)

hence substituting in the error integral yields

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\hskip 1.42262ptX[V_{A}V_{B}f]\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\big)\hskip 1.42262pt\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\Bigg[\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{A}V_{I}V_{B}f+\sum_{I=0}^{6}V_{A}(C^{I}_{B})V_{I}f\Bigg]\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\big)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.145)

Now for the first term

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\Big[\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{A}V_{I}V_{B}f\Big]d\mathcal{P}_{x}\big)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}u^{\prime},

(9.146)

the estimates follow in a similar fashion as the first derivative estimates since we already have the estimates for
C^{I}_{A}
. We control this term as follows

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\Big[\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{A}V_{I}V_{B}f\Big]d\mathcal{P}_{x}\big)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\Big[\sum_{I\in\{0,1,2,3,4\}}C^{I}_{A}V_{I}V_{B}f\Big]d\mathcal{P}_{x}\big)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\Big[\sum_{D=1}^{2}C^{4+D}_{A}V_{4+D}V_{B}f\Big]d\mathcal{P}_{x}\big)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{I\in\{0,1,2,3,4\}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\left(\frac{C\Gamma a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{C\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\right)\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}fV_{I}V_{B}fd\mathcal{P}_{x}\big)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\Gamma a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|u|V_{A}V_{B}fV_{4+D}V_{B}fd\mathcal{P}_{x}\big]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.147)

Now recall that we have made the bootstrap assumption at the start, namely

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}_{2}:=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\sum_{V_{(1)},V_{(2)}\in\tilde{V}}\sup_{u,\underline{u}}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\tilde{V}_{(1)}\tilde{V}_{(2)}f|^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262ptd^{2}\theta\leq\mathcal{V},

therefore the terms of the first type belonging to the error terms yield

\displaystyle\bigg|\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\big(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\Big[\sum_{I=0}^{6}C^{I}_{A}V_{I}V_{B}f\Big]d\mathcal{P}_{x}\big)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\Gamma\mathcal{V}a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{|u|}.

(9.148)

Now consider the remaining terms

\displaystyle\sum_{I=0}^{6}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\Big[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}fV_{A}(C^{I}_{B})V_{I}fd\mathcal{P}_{x}\Big]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{I=0}^{4}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\Big[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}fV_{A}(C^{I}_{B})V_{I}fd\mathcal{P}_{x}\Big]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{D=1}^{2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\Big[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}fV_{A}(C^{4+D}_{B})V_{4+D}fd\mathcal{P}_{x}\Big]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.149)

To estimate these terms systematically, we adopt the following strategy. The error term
V_{A}(C^{I}_{B})
schematically reads as follows¨

\displaystyle V_{A}(C^{I}_{B})\sim\not{\nabla}\psi\cdot\hskip 1.42262ptG(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})+\text{terms~involving~1~derivative~of~stress tensor}+l.o.t.,

(9.150)

where
l.o.t
are the terms that do not contain derivatives of
\psi
and
G
is a polynomial function of its entries and does not contain any derivatives. Here,
\psi
belongs to the Ricci coefficients-Weyl curvature set
\{\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi,\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi},\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\omega},\alpha,\underline{\alpha},\beta,\underline{\beta},\rho,\sigma\}
. First, we consider the derivative terms

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\hskip 1.42262ptG(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})\not{\nabla}\psi V_{B}fd\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.151)

We control these in the following way,

\displaystyle\big|\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\hskip 1.42262ptG(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})\not{\nabla}\psi\hskip 1.42262ptV_{B}f\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}^{\prime},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\Big[\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}|\not{\nabla}\psi|||V_{A}V_{B}f||_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}}||V_{B}f||_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\Big]\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}^{\prime},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\Big[||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{2}({S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}})}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{B}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\Big]\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{2}({S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}})}\Big[\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{B}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\Big]\text{d}u^{\prime},

(9.152)

where we have used the fact that
||V_{B}f||_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}}
is uniformly bounded by invoking the estimates, from Proposition
9.2
. The idea is that we use the bootstrap on

\frac{1}{a^{2}}\Bigg[\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{B}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\Bigg],

uniformly over
u
, which leaves

\displaystyle\big|\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\hskip 1.42262ptG(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\psi\hskip 1.42262ptV_{B}f\hskip 1.42262ptd\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\hskip 1.42262pta^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\hskip 1.42262pt\sup_{\underline{u}^{\prime},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{2}({S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.153)

Also, recall that the lower order terms appearing here can be estimated in
L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
. Now we use the explicit expressions for the error terms
\{V_{D}(C^{I}_{A})\}_{I=0}^{7}
. We estimate:

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\psi_{g}\cdot p^{4}\quad\Big(G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})=\psi_{g}\cdot p^{4}\Big)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},\,S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\,\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}\left\|\not{\nabla}\psi\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\left(\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\cdot\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\cdot\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}+\frac{a\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\cdot\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\right)\,\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{3}}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{4}},

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\psi_{g}\cdot\not{p}\cdot\not{p}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\left(\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\cdot\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\cdot\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}+\frac{a\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\cdot\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\right)du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{3}}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{4}},

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\not{p}+\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\psi_{g}\cdot\not{p}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\left(\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}+\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\cdot\frac{\Gamma a^{1/2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\right)du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}},

(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\cdot\psi_{g})\cdot\not{p}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\left(\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}+\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\cdot\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\right)du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}},

(\not{\nabla}\psi_{g})\cdot\not{g}\cdot\not{p}\cdot p^{4}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\left(\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\cdot\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\right)du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}},

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\not{g}\cdot\not{p}\cdot\not{p}\cdot\not{p}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\left(\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\cdot\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\right)du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}},

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\psi_{g}\cdot p^{4}\cdot p^{4}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\left(\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\cdot\frac{a^{1/2}}{|u^{\prime}|}\right)du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{4}},

\psi_{g}\cdot\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\not{p}\cdot p^{4}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\left(\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\cdot\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\cdot\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\right)du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{4}},

\psi_{g}\cdot\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\cdot\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\left(\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\cdot\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\right)du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|}.

\not{\nabla}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}})

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|},

\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot p^{4}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\left\|\not{\nabla}\psi\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}},

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot p^{4}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\left\|\not{\nabla}\psi\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}

\not{\nabla}\alpha\cdot\not{p}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\left\|\not{\nabla}\psi\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\Gamma a^{1/2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma a^{1/2}}{|u|}

\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\left\|\not{\nabla}\psi\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{3/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{3/2}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{p}\cdot p^{4}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\left\|\not{\nabla}\psi\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{5}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{4}},

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\psi_{g}\cdot\not{p}\cdot\not{p}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{3}}

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}

\not{\nabla}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\cdot\psi_{g}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}

\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{p}\cdot p^{4}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim a^{1/2}\Gamma\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}

\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim a\Gamma^{2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}

\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\hat{\chi}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim a\Gamma^{2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}},

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\psi_{g}\cdot p^{4}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{3}},

\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}\cdot\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\cdot\not{p}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{3}},

\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}\cdot\hat{\chi}\cdot p^{4}\cdot\not{p}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{5}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{4}}

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{p}\cdot\not{p}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{3}}

\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}\cdot\psi_{g}\cdot\not{p}\cdot p^{4}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{5}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{4}}

\not{\nabla}\beta\cdot p^{4}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}

\not{\nabla}\beta\cdot\not{p}\cdot\not{p}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}

\not{\nabla}\alpha\cdot\not{p}

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\cdot\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{1/2}\Gamma}{|u|},

\not{\nabla}\alpha\cdot\not{p}\cdot p^{4}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},\,S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\,\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}.

\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}\cdot\not{p}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},\,S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\,\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\Gamma}{|u|^{5}}.

\not{\nabla}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\cdot\not{p}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},\,S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\,\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\Gamma\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}.

\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\not{p}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},\,S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\,\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}.

\not{\nabla}\hat{\chi}\cdot\not{p}\cdot p^{4}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},\,S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\,\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}.

\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\cdot\not{p}\cdot p^{4}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},\,S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\,\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,du^{\prime}\qquad\lesssim\Gamma\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\,du^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}.

Now we estimate the following term

\displaystyle\sum_{D=1}^{2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\hskip 1.42262ptV_{A}(C^{4+D}_{B})V_{4+D}f\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.154)

Again, we have the following form

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\hskip 1.42262ptG(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\psi\hskip 1.42262pt|u^{\prime}|^{2}\hskip 1.42262ptV_{B+4}f\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\hskip 1.42262pt\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.155)

We control this term in the following way ,

\displaystyle\big|\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\hskip 1.42262ptG(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\psi\hskip 1.42262pt|u^{\prime}|^{2}\hskip 1.42262ptV_{B+4}f\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}^{\prime},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\Bigg[\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}|\not{\nabla}\psi|||V_{A}V_{B}f||_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}}|u^{\prime}|||u^{\prime}V_{B+4}f||_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\Bigg]\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}^{\prime},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\Bigg[||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{2}({S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}})}|u^{\prime}|\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{B}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\Bigg]\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{2}({S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}})}|u^{\prime}|\Bigg[\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{B}f|^{2}\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\Bigg]\text{d}u^{\prime},

(9.156)

where we have used that fact that
||\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262ptV_{B+4}f||_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}}
is uniformly bounded, by the first order estimate from Proposition
9.2
. The idea is that we use the bootstrap on
\Bigg[\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{B}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\Bigg]
, uniformly over
u
, which leaves

\displaystyle\big|\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}f\hskip 1.42262ptG(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\psi\hskip 1.42262pt|u^{\prime}|^{2}\hskip 1.42262ptV_{B+4}f\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\hskip 1.42262pt\sup_{\underline{u},S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}},\mathcal{P}_{x}}|G(\psi,\not{p},p^{4})|\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{2}({S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}})}|u^{\prime}|\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.157)

We estimate these terms in a similar fashion, and therefore we do not present the term-by-term estimates, but rather the final one (i.e., the worst possible decay). Collecting all the terms together with Grönwall inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}(u)\lesssim\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}(u_{\infty})+\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|},

(9.158)

or equivalently

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{2}}\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}(u)\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{2}(u_{\infty})+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|}.

(9.159)

The other derivative estimates follow in a similar fashion. In fact, the estimates are exactly the same with repeated control over the Ricci coefficients and the Weyl curvature components. Therefore, we do not present the readers with repeated computations. This completes the second derivative estimates for the Vlasov field.

\square

9.3
Third Derivative Control of Vlasov

Now we move on to the third derivative estimates. First, we illustrate how the regularity level is controlled, since integrability is not the major issue at higher orders (it having been resolved at first order and with the understanding that extra
\tilde{V}-
derivatives preserve it). First, recall the definition of the norm that we wish to control
for
k_{i}\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}_{3}:=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\sum_{\tilde{V}_{(1)},\tilde{V}_{(2)},\tilde{V}_{(3)}\in\tilde{V}}\sup_{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\tilde{V}_{(1)}\tilde{V}_{(2)}\tilde{V}_{(3)}f|^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262ptd^{2}\theta\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

Proposition 9.4

Let
f:\mathcal{P}\to\mathbb{R}
denote a classical solution to the Vlasov equation posed on a smooth, globally hyperbolic Lorentzian manifold
(\mathcal{M},g)
, foliated by a double null coordinate system
(u,\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})
. Suppose that
f
satisfies the transport equation along the null geodesic flow and that all relevant Ricci coefficients, curvature components, and geometric quantities satisfy the bootstrap assumptions described in Section
2.8
. Let
\mathfrak{V}_{2}
denote the second-order energy quantity associated to
f
, defined by
\mathfrak{V}
,
built from commutation vector fields adapted to the geometric foliation as follows

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}_{3}:=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\sum_{\tilde{V}_{(1)},\tilde{V}_{(2)},\tilde{V}_{(3)}\in\tilde{V}}\sup_{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\tilde{V}_{(1)}\tilde{V}_{(2)}\tilde{V}_{(3)}f|^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262ptd^{2}\theta\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

Assume, furthermore, that the initial data for
f
obeys the bound

\mathcal{I}^{0}_{3}:=\mathfrak{V}_{3}:=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\sum_{\tilde{V}_{(1)},\tilde{V}_{(2)},\tilde{V}_{(3)}\in\tilde{V}}\sup_{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\tilde{V}_{(1)}\tilde{V}_{(2)}\tilde{V}_{(3)}f|^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262ptd^{2}\theta\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}<\infty.

Then the following estimates hold

\mathfrak{V}_{3}\lesssim\left(1+\mathcal{I}^{0}_{3}\right).

(9.160)

where the involved constants are purely numerical.

Proof.

First, we make the bootstrap assumption that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}_{3}\leq\mathcal{V},

(9.161)

where
\mathcal{V}
is large enough so that

\mathcal{I}^{0}_{3}\ll\mathcal{V}

but also
\mathcal{V}\leq a^{\frac{1}{320}}
. Under the bootstrap assumption, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\tilde{V}_{(1)},\tilde{V}_{(2)},\tilde{V}_{(3)}\in\tilde{V}}\sup_{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\tilde{V}_{(1)}\tilde{V}_{(2)}\tilde{V}_{(3)}f|^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262ptd^{2}\theta\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq a^{2}\mathcal{V}.

Now, we outline the strategy to close the regularity estimates using the generalized Hölder inequality on the mass shell. This is the most important aspect of the third derivative estimates. What then remains are tedious but straightforward computations. Therefore, we keep the tedious estimates as short as possible for clarity.
For
I,J,K\in\{0,\dots,6\}
, recall the commutation formula

X\big[V_{I}V_{J}V_{K}f\big]=V_{I}\big(X[V_{J}V_{K}f]\big)-[V_{I},X]\,V_{J}V_{K}f.

(9.162)

Using the expression for the double commutator, we have

\displaystyle X\big[V_{I}V_{J}V_{K}f\big]

\displaystyle=V_{I}\big(X[V_{J}V_{K}f]\big)-[V_{I},X]\,V_{J}V_{K}f

\displaystyle=\sum_{L=0}^{6}C^{L}_{I}\,V_{L}V_{J}V_{K}f+V_{I}\Bigg[\sum_{L=0}^{6}C^{L}_{J}\,V_{L}V_{K}f+\sum_{L=0}^{6}V_{J}(C^{L}_{K})\,V_{L}f\Bigg].

(9.163)

Since each
V_{I}
is a derivation on the mass shell, it satisfies Leibniz’s rule.
Therefore,

X\big[V_{I}V_{J}V_{K}f\big]=\underbrace{\sum_{L=0}^{6}C^{L}_{I}\,V_{L}V_{J}V_{K}f}_{\mathrm{I}}+\underbrace{\sum_{L=0}^{6}V_{I}V_{J}(C^{L}_{K})\,V_{L}f}_{\mathrm{II}}+\underbrace{\sum_{L=0}^{6}V_{J}(C^{L}_{K})\,V_{I}V_{L}f}_{\mathrm{III}}.

(9.164)

The term
\mathrm{I}
is straightforward to handle (analogous to the previous two cases) and will not be discussed in detail here.
The term
\mathrm{II}
is slightly more intricate; we will treat only the most dangerous contributions.
The term
\mathrm{III}
is the most involved and will be analyzed fully.

Consider the quantity

\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{3}:=\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\bigg(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\big|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\big|^{2}\,\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\bigg)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime},

(9.165)

which, under the bootstrap assumption, verifies
\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{3}\leq a^{2}\mathcal{V}
.
Applying the transport inequality (
9.1
) to
\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{3}
yields

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{3}(u)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{V}^{1}_{3}(u_{\infty})+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\bigg(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\,X\big[V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\big]\,\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\bigg)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.166)

Thus, it remains to control the error terms arising from
X[V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f]
.
As an example, we consider

\sum_{J=0}^{6}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\bigg(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\,V_{B}(C^{J}_{C})\,V_{A}V_{J}f\,dP\bigg)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.167)

The term
V_{B}(C^{0}_{C})
has the schematic form, for
\psi
being either a Ricci coefficient or a Weyl curvature component,

V_{B}(C^{0}_{C})\sim\not{\nabla}\psi\,f_{1}(\psi)\,f_{2}(\not{p})\,f_{3}(p^{4})+\text{l.o.t.}

(9.168)

We first control the terms containing the top-order derivative of Vlasov, the second derivative of Vlasov, and the first derivative of Ricci coefficients and Weyl curvature components.
These terms have the schematic structure

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,u^{\prime}}}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\psi\,f_{1}(\psi)\,f_{2}(\not{p})\,f_{3}(p^{4})\,V_{A}V_{0}f\,\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\right)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}|\not{\nabla}\psi|\,|f_{1}(\psi)|\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|\,\|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\,\|V_{A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|\,\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\,\|f_{1}(\psi)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\,\|V_{A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\,\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,\sup_{\underline{u}}\|f_{1}(\psi)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\int_{0}^{1}\|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\,\|V_{A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\,\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.169)

Now we claim that the integral

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

(9.170)

can be controlled by means of the boot-strap assumptions on the top order norm of
f
and the lower order norms estimated earlier. Note that
||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}
is a function on spacetime and particular on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
. Therefore, we can apply Sobolev embedding on the sphere. Recall the Sobolev embedding for a function
\mathcal{G}
on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
,

\displaystyle||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}||\not{\nabla}\mathcal{G}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+|u|^{-\frac{1}{2}}||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(9.171)

Applying this to the function
\mathcal{G}=||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}
, we obtain

\displaystyle||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\not{\nabla}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{0}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg[\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{0}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\right)\Bigg]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\not{\nabla}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{0}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+a|u|^{-1}\mathcal{I}^{0}_{2},

(9.172)

by using the second derivative estimates from Proposition
9.3
. Now, note the following computations

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{0}f|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{\int_{P}V_{A}V_{0}f\not{\nabla}V_{A}V_{0}f}{\sqrt{\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{0}f|^{2}\right)}}+|\not{\Gamma}|||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\lesssim||\not{\nabla}V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|\not{\Gamma}|||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})},

and recall the following relation between the derivatives

\displaystyle e_{A}=

\displaystyle V_{(A)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{\not{\Gamma}^{C}_{AB}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}}{p^{3}}+{\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{A}}^{C}+\frac{{\chi_{A}}^{C}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+C)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert})V_{(4+A)}+\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}\chi_{AB}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}}{p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\right)(V_{(4)}+V_{(0)})

(9.173)

and the fact that, schematically,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}V_{A}V_{0}f\sim

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle V_{D}V_{A}V_{0}f+|u|^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt(\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\chi\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4})\hskip 1.42262ptV_{4+D}V_{A}V_{0}f+|u|^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptV_{4+D}V_{A}V_{0}f+(\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\underline{\eta})(V_{4}V_{A}V_{0}f+V_{0}V_{A}V_{0}f).

(9.174)

Finally, this yields

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\not{\nabla}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{0}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg[\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Bigg(||V_{D}V_{A}V_{0}f+|u|^{2}(\not{\Gamma}\not{p}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\chi p^{4})V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{0}f+|u|^{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{0}f

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\chi\not{p}+\underline{\eta})(V_{4}V_{A}V_{0}f+V_{0}V_{A}V_{0}f)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg)^{2}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\Bigg]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg[\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Bigg(||V_{D}V_{A}V_{0}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+a^{2}\Gamma^{2}||V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{0}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\Gamma^{2}||V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{0}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{4}}||V_{4}V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\Bigg]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg[||V_{D}V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma\hskip 1.42262pt||V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\Gamma\hskip 1.42262pt||V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{4}V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg].

(9.175)

Therefore, the integral in (
9.170
) is estimated as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\|V_{A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\,\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{1}\|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big[\|V_{D}V_{A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+a\Gamma\|V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\Gamma\|V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\|V_{4}V_{A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big]\,d\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\,d\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{1}\Big[\|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\|V_{D}V_{A}|u|V_{0}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{a\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\||u|V_{4+D}V_{A}|u|V_{0}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\||u|V_{4+D}V_{A}|u|V_{0}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|u|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big]\,d\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big[a^{2}\mathcal{V}+\frac{a^{3}\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{\Gamma a^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}+a^{2}|u|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{V}\Big]\lesssim a^{2}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{V}.

(9.176)

Therefore, the ultimate estimate for (
9.3
) reads
