\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\bigg(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\psi\hskip 1.42262ptf_{1}(\psi)\hskip 1.42262ptf_{2}(\not{p})\hskip 1.42262ptf_{3}(p^{4})\hskip 1.42262ptV_{A}V_{0}f\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\bigg)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\,\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,\sup_{\underline{u}}\|f_{1}(\psi)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{0}^{1}\|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\,\|V_{A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\,\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,\sup_{\underline{u}}\|f_{1}(\psi)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|\,|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{2}\mathcal{V}\,\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad=

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}\|\not{\nabla}\psi\|_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,\sup_{\underline{u}}\|f_{1}(\psi)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\,\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|\,|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.177)

Now we systematically control these terms, by recalling the commutation formula derived in the previous Section. Using the explicit expressions for the error terms
V_{I}(C^{J}_{K})
as in
9.2
-
9.2
, we estimate the following:

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})||u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.178)

First, consider the worst term. For
\psi=\alpha,~f_{1}=1,f_{2}=\not{p},f_{3}=1
, we have

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\alpha||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}||u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

(9.179)

To estimate
||\not{\nabla}\alpha||_{L^{4}}
, we first use the scale-invariant codimension-1 trace inequality (
3.12
):

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}\alpha||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim||\not{\nabla}\alpha||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}+||\nabla_{4}\not{\nabla}\alpha||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(H)}\Bigg(||\not{\nabla}\alpha||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{4}_{sc}(H)}+||a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{2}\alpha||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(H)}\Bigg).

(9.180)

Note that the right-hand side is under control in terms of
\Gamma
by bootstrap. Therefore, noting the scaling

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}\alpha||_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Gamma,

(9.181)

hence

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\alpha||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}|\hskip 1.42262pt|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{\frac{5}{2}}\mathcal{V}\Gamma\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{\prime}|^{-\frac{3}{2}}|u^{\prime}|^{-1}|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{|u|}\lesssim a^{2},

(9.182)

Now consider the curvature
\underline{\beta}
. We observe that we need to estimate the following

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

(9.183)

We control
||\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}
by the codimension-1 trace inequality
3.12

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim||\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}+||\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(H)}\Bigg(||\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{4}_{sc}(H)}+||a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\beta}||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(H)}\Bigg)

(9.184)

and by the bootstrap assumptions (
2.80
), we have

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{4}}\Gamma\implies||\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{2+\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{4+\frac{1}{2}}}\Gamma,

(9.185)

since
s_{2}(\underline{\beta})=\frac{3}{2}
,
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta})=2
, and
||\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4+\frac{1}{2}}}||\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
. Therefore

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\underline{\beta}||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{2}\mathcal{V}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2+\frac{1}{4}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4+\frac{1}{2}}}|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{4+\frac{1}{4}}\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}.

(9.186)

Similarly,

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\beta||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}||u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{2}\mathcal{V}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{1+\frac{1}{4}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2+\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{3+\frac{1}{4}}\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}.

(9.187)

Finally, we need to control the following involving
\underline{\alpha}
that has the highest decay

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}|\hskip 1.42262pt|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

(9.188)

We invoke the following codimension-1 trace inequality
3.10
for
\underline{\alpha}
,

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim||\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+||a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H})}\left(a^{-\frac{1}{4}}||\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H})}+||\not{\nabla}\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H})}\right).

(9.189)

Now, from the bootstrap, we have control over the following
\underline{\alpha}
norms

\displaystyle\lVert{\mathcal{D}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\lesssim\Gamma,\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{2}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\lesssim\Gamma

(9.190)

for
\mathcal{D}\in\{\not{\nabla}_{4},|u|\not{\nabla}_{3},a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\}
. Therefore, this translates to

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim||\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty}})}+\frac{\Gamma}{a}

(9.191)

or in the ordinary norm

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}||_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{|u|^{5+\frac{1}{2}}}\Gamma

(9.192)

and therefore

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}|\hskip 1.42262pt|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{2}\mathcal{V}\Gamma\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{|u|^{5+\frac{1}{2}}}|u^{\prime}|^{-1}|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{2+\frac{5}{2}}\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{|u|^{4}}.

(9.193)

The derivatives of the Ricci coefficients are estimated as follows (one again, we do not repeat estimates that appear twice or more):

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\psi_{g}||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

(9.194)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2+\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\mathcal{V}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}.

(9.195)

This completes the estimates for the term

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})||u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.196)

Now consider the next term

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\Bigg(\int_{p}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}fV_{B}(C^{D}_{C})V_{A}V_{D}fdP\Bigg)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.197)

Similarly, one has the following schematic structure for a
\psi
belonging to the set of connection of Weyl curvature components:

\displaystyle V_{B}(C^{D}_{C})\sim\not{\nabla}\psi\cdot f_{1}(\psi)\cdot f_{2}(\not{p})\cdot f_{3}(p^{4})+l.o.t.

(9.198)

We estimate the integral in a similar fashion,

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},u^{\prime}}}\Bigg(\int_{p}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\psi\hskip 1.42262ptf_{1}(\psi)\hskip 1.42262ptf_{2}(\not{p})\hskip 1.42262ptf_{3}(p^{4})\hskip 1.42262ptV_{A}V_{D}f\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\Bigg)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}|\not{\nabla}\psi||f_{1}(\psi)|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.53581pt\times||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\text{d}u^{\prime}

(9.199)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|

\displaystyle\hskip 8.53581pt\times\int_{0}^{1}||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.200)

Now we claim that the integral

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

(9.201)

can be controlled by means of the bootstrap assumptions on the top order norm of
f
and the lower order norms estimated earlier. The philosophy is exactly the same as before. Note that
||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}
is a function on spacetime and particular on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
. Therefore, we can apply the Sobolev embedding result on the sphere. Recall the Sobolev embedding for a function
\mathcal{G}
on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
:

\displaystyle||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}||\not{\nabla}\mathcal{G}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+|u|^{-\frac{1}{2}}||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(9.202)

Applying this to the function
\mathcal{G}=||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}
yields

\displaystyle||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\not{\nabla}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{D}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+|u|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg[\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{D}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\right)\Bigg]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\not{\nabla}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{D}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+a|u|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\mathcal{I}^{0}_{2}},

(9.203)

by using the second derivative estimates from the previous Proposition
9.3
. We further compute

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{D}f|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{\int_{P}V_{A}V_{D}f\not{\nabla}V_{A}V_{D}f}{\sqrt{\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{D}f|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}}+\not{\Gamma}|||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\lesssim||\not{\nabla}V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt|||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}.

(9.204)

Recalling the following relation between the derivatives,

\displaystyle e_{A}=

\displaystyle V_{(A)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{\not{\Gamma}^{C}_{AB}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}}{p^{3}}+{\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{A}}^{C}+\frac{{\chi_{A}}^{C}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+C)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert})V_{(4+A)}+\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}\chi_{AB}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}}{p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\right)(V_{(4)}+V_{(0)}),

(9.205)

we establish the schematic expression

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}V_{A}V_{E}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\sim||V_{D}V_{A}V_{E}f+|u|^{2}(\not{\Gamma}\not{p}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\chi p^{4})V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{E}f+|u|^{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{E}f

\displaystyle\hskip 204.85983pt+(\chi\not{p}+\underline{\eta})(V_{4}V_{A}V_{E}f+V_{0}V_{A}V_{E}f)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}.

(9.206)

Putting together all these basic computations, we obtain

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\left(\not{\nabla}\left(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{E}f|^{2}d\mathcal{P}_{x}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

(9.207)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg[||V_{D}V_{A}V_{E}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+a\Gamma||V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{E}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\Gamma||V_{4+D}V_{A}V_{E}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{4}V_{A}V_{E}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg].

(9.208)

Notice that this controls the top order terms in the Vlasov estimates. The vital point to note here that without uniform estimate on the first derivative
Vf
in
L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})
, this strategy fails. This, in turn, necessitates
L^{\infty}
control of the Weyl curvature. Therefore, at least two derivatives of the curvature on the null cone
H
(and/or
\underline{H}
) need to be controlled. Finally, the integral in (
9.201
) is estimated as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}||V_{A}V_{E}f||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{1}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{D}V_{A}V_{E}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{2}\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}||uV_{4+D}V_{A}V_{E}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}||uV_{4+D}V_{A}V_{E}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a\Gamma}{|u|^{4}}||V_{4}V_{A}V_{E}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|u|^{-\frac{1}{2}}||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg[\mathcal{V}+\frac{a^{2}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}+|u|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{V}\Bigg]\lesssim|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}.

(9.209)

Recalling back the estimates that we wanted to obtain,

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,u^{\prime}}}\Bigg(\int_{p}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\not{\nabla}\psi f_{1}(\psi)f_{2}(\not{p})f_{3}(p^{4})V_{A}V_{E}fdP\Bigg)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\int_{0}^{1}||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}||V_{A}V_{E}f||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})||u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}\psi||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})||u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.210)

First, note that several estimates repeat, and therefore we do not write the estimates for every term. Collecting all terms together yields

\displaystyle\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}})||_{L^{4}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}|||u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\Bigg(\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2+\frac{1}{2}}},\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2+\frac{1}{2}}}\Bigg)|u^{\prime}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}\Bigg(\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|},\frac{\Gamma}{|u|}\Bigg).

(9.211)

We now move on to estimating the terms containing two derivatives of Weyl curvature. This ensemble of terms is depicted in the following term in the transport estimates:

\displaystyle\sum_{L=0}^{6}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}fV_{A}V_{B}(C^{L}_{C})V_{L}f\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.212)

We compute
V_{A}V_{B}(C^{L}_{C})
for all
L=0,....,6
,

\displaystyle V_{A}V_{B}(C^{0}_{C})\sim\bigg[\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\alpha\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\beta\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\alpha\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}|^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt(p^{4}+\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p})+\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+|\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}|^{2}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}+\mathcal{E}_{l.o.t}\bigg]_{ABC},

(9.213)

where the
\mathcal{E}_{l.o.t}
contains terms that are linear in the first derivative. The top derivative terms of
V_{A}V_{B}(C^{0}_{C})
have the following schematic structure

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi\cdot f_{1}(\psi)\cdot f_{2}(\not{p})\cdot f_{3}(p^{4}),

(9.214)

for
\psi
denoting a Ricci coefficient or a Weyl curvature component. If
\psi
is a Ricci coefficient that belongs to the set
\{\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi,\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi},\eta,\underline{\eta},\omega=0,\underline{\omega}\}
, then we estimate
\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi
in
L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
,

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi f_{1}(\psi)f_{2}(\not{p})f_{3}(p^{4})V_{0}f\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\hskip 1.42262pt\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\times\Bigg[\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f|^{2}\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\text{d}S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg]^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{\underline{u}}||V_{0}f||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.215)

Now note that
\sup_{\underline{u}}|||u|V_{0}f||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}
is estimated in the previous step and we use bootstrap for
\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f|^{2}
. Therefore, the final estimates become

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi f_{1}(\psi)f_{2}(\not{p})f_{3}(p^{4})V_{0}f\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})||u^{\prime}|^{-1}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.216)

We now collect (without duplication) the terms that contain second derivatives of the Ricci coefficients. We have

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\psi_{g}||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|p^{4}||u^{\prime}|^{-1}

(9.217)

\displaystyle\lesssim a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}|u^{\prime}|^{-2}|u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{3}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{5}},

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\psi_{g}||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}\not{p}||u^{\prime}|^{-1}\lesssim\frac{a^{3}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{4}},

(9.218)

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}||u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}|u^{\prime}|^{-1}|u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{3}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{5}},

(9.219)

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi_{g}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}||u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}|u^{\prime}|^{-2}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{3}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{4}},

(9.220)

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\psi_{g}||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}||u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}|u^{\prime}|^{-2}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{5}},

(9.221)

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\psi_{g}||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}||u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}|u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{5}},

(9.222)

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|p^{4}||u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}|u^{\prime}|^{-2}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{4}}.

(9.223)

We now move on to estimating terms involving the Weyl curvature
\psi\neq\underline{\alpha}
:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi f_{1}(\psi)f_{2}(\not{p})f_{3}(p^{4})V_{0}fdP\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\int_{0}^{1}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}||V_{0}f||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\text{d}u^{\prime}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi||_{L^{2}(H_{u^{\prime}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\Bigg[\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}}}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f|^{2}\Bigg]^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{\underline{u}}||V_{0}f||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\psi||_{L^{2}(H_{u^{\prime}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})||u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(9.224)

Now estimate

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\not{\nabla}^{2}\alpha\not{p}V_{0}fdP\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\alpha||_{L^{2}(H_{u^{\prime}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}||u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}|u^{\prime}|^{-2}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{3}},

(9.225)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}^{2}\beta\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptV_{0}f\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\beta||_{L^{2}(H_{u^{\prime}})}\sup_{\underline{u}}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}||u^{\prime}|^{-1}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}|u^{\prime}|^{-3}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{5}}.

(9.226)

Now, suppose the curvature component
\psi
is
\underline{\alpha}
. The advantage of the term containing
\underline{\alpha}
is that it has the best decay among the weyl curvature components. Then by bootstrap and the first order estimate from proposition
9.2
,

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}\hskip 1.42262ptf_{1}(\psi)\hskip 1.42262ptf_{2}(\not{p})\hskip 1.42262ptf_{3}(p^{4})\hskip 1.42262ptV_{0}f\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})||u^{\prime}|^{-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\int_{0}^{1}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}|||u|V_{0}f||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}|u^{\prime}|^{-1}\text{d}u^{\prime}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a\sqrt{\mathcal{V}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|\Bigg(\int_{0}^{1}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\Bigg(\int_{0}^{1}||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}|u^{\prime}|^{-1}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a\sqrt{\mathcal{V}}\sup_{u}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{1}||V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{\prime}|^{10}\int_{0}^{1}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\text{d}u^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\Bigg(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|}{|u^{\prime}|^{12}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{\prime}|^{10}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|}{a^{-3}|u^{\prime}|^{12}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\Bigg(\sup_{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{\prime}|^{10}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|}{a^{-3}|u^{\prime}|^{12}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Now recall that
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha})=s_{2}(\underline{\alpha})+1=3
and therefore the estimates read

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{10}}{a^{3}}||\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{10}}{a^{3}}\frac{a^{6}}{|u^{\prime}|^{12}}||||\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||||a\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}=||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}\underline{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H})}

(9.227)

and therefore

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}\hskip 1.42262ptf_{1}(\psi)\hskip 1.42262ptf_{2}(\not{p})\hskip 1.42262ptf_{3}(p^{4})V_{0}f\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\Bigg(\sup_{\underline{u}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}\underline{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}})}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{\underline{u}}\Bigg(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\sup_{\underline{u}}||f_{1}(\psi)||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{2}(\not{p})|\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f_{3}(p^{4})|}{a^{-3}|u^{\prime}|^{12}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

(9.228)

Now recall the term that actually contains
\underline{\alpha}
so that we have an explicit form of the functions
f_{1},f_{2}
, and
f_{3}

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Bigg[\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}V_{A}V_{B}V_{C}f\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}\hskip 1.42262ptV_{0}f\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\theta^{1}\text{d}\theta^{2}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\text{d}u^{\prime}

(9.229)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\mathcal{V}\Bigg(\sup_{\underline{u}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}\underline{\alpha}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}})}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{\underline{u}}\Bigg(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\sup_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|\not{p}|}{a^{-3}|u^{\prime}|^{12}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{V}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma\Bigg(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{-1}}{a^{-3}|u^{\prime}|^{12}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\mathcal{V}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma\frac{a^{2+\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{6}}.

(9.230)

The remaining terms are estimated in an exactly similar fashion-they verify similar or better estimates. A collection of all the terms yields the desired estimate

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}_{3}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{I}^{0}_{3}.

(9.231)

This completes the proof for the third derivatives of the Vlasov field.

\square

9.4
Estimating the stress-energy tensor
T
from the estimates of
f

Once the norms
\mathfrak{V}_{1}-\mathfrak{V}_{3}
are estimated, we are left to estimate the norm of the Vlasov stress-energy tensor. For this, we need to use the expression for
e_{\mu}
in terms of the vector fields
V
. We will consider the action of derivatives belonging to the following set
\mathcal{D}:=\{\not{\nabla}_{4},a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla},|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}\}
. First consider
\mathcal{D}=|u|\nabla_{3}
which is same as
|u|e_{3}
when
f
is a function. First, recall the vital expressions

•

There holds

\displaystyle e_{A}=

\displaystyle V_{(A)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{\not{\Gamma}^{C}_{AB}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}}{p^{3}}+{\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{A}}^{C}+\frac{{\chi_{A}}^{C}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+C)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert})V_{(4+A)}+\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}\chi_{AB}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}}{p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\right)(V_{(4)}+V_{(0)}).

(9.232)

•

There holds

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert e_{3}=

\displaystyle\left(1+\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{\eta_{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{A}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(0)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{({\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{A}-e_{B}(b^{A}))\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}+\eta^{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)|u|V_{(4+A)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{\eta_{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{A}}{p^{3}}V_{(4)}+|u|\underline{\omega}(V_{(4)}+V_{(0)}).

(9.233)

•

There holds

\displaystyle e_{4}=V_{(3)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{{\chi_{B}}^{A}p^{B}}{p^{3}}+2\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}^{A}\right)V_{(4+A)}

(9.234)

Now, recall that we have the estimates for the
V
derivatives of
f
. We can use these identities to estimate the ordinary spacetime derivatives of the Vlasov stress-energy tensor. We do this systematically. For example, note that in light of the point-wise estimates on the Ricci coefficients,

\displaystyle||u|e_{3}f|\lesssim|V_{0}f|+\frac{\Gamma}{|u|}||u|V_{4+A}f|+\frac{\Gamma}{|u|}|V_{4}f|

(9.235)

since

\displaystyle 1+\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{\eta_{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{A}}{p^{3}}=1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1

(9.236)

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert^{3}\left(\frac{{\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}+\eta^{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{|u|}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}

(9.237)

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{\eta_{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{A}}{p^{3}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(9.238)

and the term
|\lvert u\rvert^{3}\left(\frac{{\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}+\eta^{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+A)}(f)|\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{|u|}||u|V_{4+A}f|\lesssim||u|V_{4+A}f|
. First, we provide the
L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
and
L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
estimates for the first derivatives of the vlasov field. Recall the following elementary lemma

Remark 13
(Action of Derivative on stress-energy tensor)

Suppose the Vlasov stress-energy tensor be
T_{\mu\nu}:=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f(x,p)p_{\mu}p_{\nu}\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}
. One can explicitly compute the action of the derivatives
\not{\nabla},\not{\nabla}_{4},
and
\not{\nabla}_{3}
on the stress tensor. For example, observe the following expression for
\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{T}_{3A}

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}

\displaystyle\not{T}_{3A}=4e_{3}\bigg(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}fp_{A}p^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg)-\Gamma^{C}_{3A}T_{3C}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T[e_{3}(f)]_{3A}+\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}T[f]_{3A}+[\underline{\chi}^{C}_{A}-e_{A}(b^{C})]T_{3C}+T[e_{3}(\log p^{4})f]_{3A}+4\bigg(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}fe_{3}(p_{A})p^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg).

(9.239)

where one uses the fact that
p_{A}=\not{g}_{AC}p^{C}
.
One can similarly evaluate the action of derivatives on other stress-energy tensor components.

Proposition 9.5
(First-order Estimates for the Vlasov Stress-Energy Tensor Components)

Let
(\mathcal{M},g)
be a four-dimensional Lorentzian manifold foliated by a double null foliation
\{S_{u,\underline{u}}\}_{(u,\underline{u})}
, with associated null coordinates
u,\underline{u}
, and let
e_{3},e_{4}
denote the standard incoming and outgoing normalized null vector fields satisfying
g(e_{3},e_{4})=-2
, and
\{e_{A}\}_{A=1,2}
an orthonormal frame tangent to
S_{u,\underline{u}}
. Let
f\colon\mathcal{P}\subset T^{*}\mathcal{M}\to\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}
denote the distribution function of a collisionless ensemble of particles governed by the Vlasov equation, and let
T_{\alpha\beta}
denote the associated stress-energy tensor defined by

T_{\alpha\beta}(x):=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f(x,p)\,p_{\alpha}p_{\beta}\,\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}},

where
p\in\mathcal{P}_{x}
lies on the mass shell
g^{\mu\nu}p_{\mu}p_{\nu}=0
, and the integration is taken with respect to the invariant volume measure on the future-directed null cone at
x
. Fix
a\gg 1
, a large parameter governing the hierarchy of estimates, and define the second-order differential operator set

\mathcal{D}:=\left\{|u|\not{\nabla}_{3},\,a^{1/2}\not{\nabla},\,\not{\nabla}_{4}\right\},

where
\not{\nabla}
denotes the induced Levi-Civita connection on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
, and the normalization of vector fields is consistent with the double-null framework.

Then, for each
u<0
,
\underline{u}\geq 0
, and for every pair of tensorial components

(\alpha,\beta)\in\{(4,4),(4,3),(4,A),(A,3),(A,B),\\
(3,3),\}

the following scale-invariant
L^{2}
estimates hold on the sphere
S_{u,\underline{u}}
:

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}T_{44}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}T_{43}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a},

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}T_{4A}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}},

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}T_{A3}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}},

(9.240)

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}T_{AB}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a},

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}T_{33}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}.

The implicit constants in the estimates are numerical. Moreover, we have the improved estimate

\displaystyle\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\left\|T_{44}[|u|e_{3}f]\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1

(9.241)

and also for the point-wise norm

\displaystyle\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\left\|T_{44}[|u|e_{3}f]\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1.

(9.242)

Proof.

The improved estimates are easy to see once the previous estimates are obtained, since

\displaystyle|u|e_{3}f=\left(1+\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{\eta_{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{A}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(0)}f+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{({\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{A}-e_{B}(b^{A}))\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}+\eta^{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)|u|V_{(4+A)}f+\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\frac{\eta_{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{A}}{p^{3}}V_{(4)}f+\underline{\omega}V_{(4)}f

(9.243)

and verification of the estimates
|V_{0}f|\lesssim a|u|^{-1},~||u|V_{4+A}f|\lesssim a,~|V_{(4)}f|\lesssim a
implies
||u|e_{3}f|\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}
since the coefficients exhibit

\displaystyle 1+\big\|\,|u|\,\eta\,\not{p}\,\big\|

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;1+\frac{a^{1/2}\,\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\;\lesssim\;1,

(9.244)

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{({\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{A}-e_{B}(b^{A}))\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}+\eta^{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)\,\big|V_{4+A}f\big|

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;\frac{1}{|u|}\,\big\|\,|u|\,V_{4+A}f\,\big\|\,

(9.245)

\displaystyle\big\|\,|u|\,\eta\,\not{p}\,\big\|

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;\frac{a^{1/2}\,\Gamma\,|u|}{|u|^{3}}\;\lesssim\;1,

(9.246)

\displaystyle|u|\,\big|\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\big|

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;|u|\,\Bigg(\big|\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\tfrac{2}{|u|}\big|+\frac{2}{|u|}\Bigg)\,\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{|u|}\;+\;1,

(9.247)

the extra
|u|^{-1}
decay factor appears.
The estimates for the first derivatives are straightforward. First, recall the improved first-order estimates for
\mathfrak{V}^{S}_{1}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{V}^{S}_{1}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{I}^{0}_{S},

(9.248)

where
\mathcal{I}^{0}_{S}
is the initial data for
\mathfrak{V}^{S}_{1}
. Now controlling the
L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
of the first derivatives is a straightforward consequence of the afore-mentioned expression relating the vector fields
e_{\mu}
to the
V
-vector fields. Here, we only present the estimates for one stress-energy component. The rest of the estimates follow in an exact similar fashion. We focus on the higher-order estimates that are non-trivial. For
\mathcal{D}=\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3},

\displaystyle T_{33}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f\,p_{3}p_{3}\,\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}\,dp^{2}\,dp^{3},

(9.249)

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}T_{33}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Big(\mathcal{D}f+\tfrac{1}{2}|u|\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,f\Big)\,p^{4}p^{4}\,\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}\,dp^{2}\,dp^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f\,p^{4}\,\mathcal{D}(p^{4})\,\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}\,dp^{2}\,dp^{3},

(9.250)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg(\underbrace{\big(1+|u|\,\eta\,\not{p}\big)\,V_{0}f+|u|^{3}\Big((\underline{\chi}-\not{\nabla}b+\not{\Gamma})\not{p}+\eta\,p^{4}\Big)\,V_{4+A}f+|u|\,\eta\,\not{p}\,V_{4}f}_{\text{transport contributions}}+\frac{1}{2}|u|\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\,f\Bigg)p^{4}p^{4}\,\text{d}\mathcal{P}_{x}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f\,p^{4}\,\mathcal{D}(p^{4})\,\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}\,dp^{2}\,dp^{3}.

(9.251)

Recalling that

\displaystyle e_{3}(p^{4})=\frac{\Bigg(2\underline{\chi}_{AB}-\not{g}_{BC}e_{A}(b^{C})-\not{g}_{AC}e_{B}(b^{C})\Bigg)p^{A}p^{B}}{2p^{3}},

(9.252)

as well as the estimates on
\Gamma^{A}_{3B}=\underline{\chi}^{A}_{B}-e_{B}(b^{A})
, we immediately obtain

\displaystyle|e_{3}p^{4}|\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}.

(9.253)

The idea is that the momentum variables
p^{\mu}
and the Ricci coefficients are bounded on the mass-shell in
L^{\infty}
, whenever estimates are available. For example,

\displaystyle 1+\big\|\,|u|\,\eta\,\not{p}\,\big\|

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;1+\frac{a^{1/2}\,\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\;\lesssim\;1,

(9.254)

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{({\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{A}-e_{B}(b^{A}))\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}+\eta^{A}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)\,\big|V_{4+A}f\big|

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;\frac{1}{|u|}\,\big\|\,|u|\,V_{4+A}f\,\big\|\,

(9.255)

\displaystyle\big\|\,|u|\,\eta\,\not{p}\,\big\|

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;\frac{a^{1/2}\,\Gamma\,|u|}{|u|^{3}}\;\lesssim\;1,

(9.256)

\displaystyle|u|\,\big|\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\big|

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;|u|\,\Bigg(\big|\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\tfrac{2}{|u|}\big|+\frac{2}{|u|}\Bigg)\,\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{|u|}\;+\;1.

(9.257)

Therefore, using the decay of momentum support,

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{D}T_{33}\big|

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;|u|^{-4}\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Big(\,|V_{0}f|+\big\|\,|u|V_{4+A}f\,\big\|+|V_{4}f|+|f|\Big)\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}\,dp^{2}\,dp^{3}

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;|u|^{-4}\Big(\,\|V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\big\|\,|u|V_{4+A}f\,\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\times\Bigg(\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}\,dp^{2}\,dp^{3}\Bigg)^{\!1/2}

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;|u|^{-5}\Big(\,\|V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\big\|\,|u|V_{4+A}f\,\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big),

(9.258)

where Hölder’s inequality is applied on the mass shell
\mathcal{P}_{x}
.
By Minkowski’s inequality, we further obtain

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{D}T_{33}\big\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;|u|^{-5}\Big(\,\|V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\big\|\,|u|V_{4+A}f\,\big\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big).

(9.259)

By the estimates on the first derivative of the Vlasov field, we have

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{D}T_{33}\big\|_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}.

(9.260)

Since
s_{2}(\mathcal{D}T_{33})=s_{2}(T_{33})=2
, it follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{D}T_{33}\big\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle=\frac{|u|^{4}}{a^{2}}\,\big\|\mathcal{D}T_{33}\big\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\;\lesssim\;\frac{1}{a^{2}}\cdot\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}\;\lesssim\;\frac{1}{a\,|u|}.

In particular, we automatically obtain

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\,\big\|\mathcal{D}T_{33}\big\|_{\mathcal{L}^{4}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;\frac{1}{a^{2}}.

(9.261)

We now proceed to consider the angular derivative

\mathcal{D}:=a^{1/2}\,\not{\nabla}.

We first recall that

\displaystyle\not{T}_{33}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f\,p_{3}p_{3}\,\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}\,dp^{2}\,dp^{3},

(9.262)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}\not{T}_{33}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\big(\mathcal{D}f+\not{\Gamma}\,f\big)\,p^{4}p^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}\,dp^{2}\,dp^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f\,p^{4}\,e_{A}(p^{4})\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}\,dp^{2}\,dp^{3}.

(9.263)

Next, we recall that

\displaystyle e_{A}

\displaystyle=V_{(A)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{\not{\Gamma}^{C}_{AB}\,p^{B}}{p^{3}}+{\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{A}}^{C}+\frac{{\chi_{A}}^{C}\,p^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+C)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{2}\left(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\right)V_{(4+A)}+\left(\frac{\chi_{AB}\,p^{B}}{2\,p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\right)\big(V_{(4)}+V_{(0)}\big).

(9.264)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{T}_{33}

\displaystyle\;\sim\;\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Big[V_{A}f+|u|^{2}\,(\not{\Gamma}\,\not{p}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}+p^{4}\chi)\,V_{4+A}f+|u|^{2}\,\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\,V_{4+A}f

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt+(\chi\,\not{p}+\underline{\eta})\,\big(V_{4}f+V_{0}f\big)+\not{\Gamma}\,f\Big]\,p^{4}p^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}\,dp^{2}\,dp^{3}.

(9.265)

By applying the pointwise estimates, the decay of the momentum support and Hölder’s inequality on the mass–shell
\mathcal{P}_{x}
, we find

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{T}_{33}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|^{5}}\Big(\|V_{A}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|\,|u|V_{4+A}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big).

(9.266)

Note that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{33}|

\displaystyle=a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\not{g}^{AB}\,\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{T}_{33}\,\not{\nabla}_{B}T]\not{T}_{33}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\cdot\frac{1}{|u|^{5}}\Big(\|V_{A}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|\,|u|V_{4+A}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big).

(9.267)

Hence,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{33}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{6}}\Big(\|V_{A}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|\,|u|V_{4+A}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big).

(9.268)

Using the Vlasov estimates from Proposition
9.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{D}\not{T}_{33}\|_{L^{4}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{6}}.

(9.269)

Since
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}T_{33})=\frac{5}{2}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{D}T_{33}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=\frac{a^{1}}{|u|^{6}a^{\frac{5}{2}}}\,|u|^{5}\lesssim\frac{1}{a|u|}.

(9.270)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\,\|\mathcal{D}T_{33}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}.

(9.271)

For
\mathcal{D}_{3}:=\not{\nabla}_{4}
, recall that

\displaystyle e_{4}=V_{(3)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{{\chi_{B}}^{A}p^{B}}{p^{3}}+2\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}^{A}\right)V_{(4+A)}

(9.272)

Moreover,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{33}

\displaystyle\sim\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\big(e_{4}f+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,f\big)\,p^{4}p^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}+\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}fe_{4}(p^{4}p^{4})\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}.

(9.273)

Thus,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{33}

\displaystyle\sim\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Big(V_{3}f+|u|^{2}(\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\not{p}+\underline{\eta})V_{4+A}f+\operatorname{tr}\chi\,f\Big)p^{4}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}}{p^{3}}\,dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}.

(9.274)

The pointwise bounds imply

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{33}|\lesssim|u|^{-5}\Big(\|V_{3}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|\,|u|V_{4+A}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big).

(9.275)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{33}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim|u|^{-5}\Big(\|V_{3}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|\,|u|V_{4+A}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}.

(9.276)

Since
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{33})=s_{2}(\not{T}_{33})=2
, we deduce

\displaystyle\|\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{T}_{33}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{|u|^{4}}{a^{2}}\cdot\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}\lesssim\frac{1}{a|u|},

(9.277)

or equivalently,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\,\|\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{33}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}.

(9.278)

Now, notice that the remaining estimates follow exactly in a similar fashion.

\square

Proposition 9.6
(Sharp High-Order Estimates for the Stress-Energy Tensor Components)

Let
(\mathcal{M},g)
be a four-dimensional Lorentzian manifold foliated by a double null foliation
\{S_{u,\underline{u}}\}_{(u,\underline{u})}
, with associated null coordinates
u,\underline{u}
, and let
e_{3},e_{4}
denote the standard incoming and outgoing normalized null vector fields satisfying
g(e_{3},e_{4})=-2
, and
\{e_{A}\}_{A=1,2}
an orthonormal frame tangent to
S_{u,\underline{u}}
. Let
f\colon\mathcal{P}\subset T^{*}\mathcal{M}\to\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}
denote the distribution function of a collisionless ensemble of particles governed by the Vlasov equation, and let
T_{\alpha\beta}
denote the associated stress-energy tensor defined by

T_{\alpha\beta}(x):=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f(x,p)\,p_{\alpha}p_{\beta}\,\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}},

where
p\in\mathcal{P}_{x}
lies on the mass shell (
g^{\mu\nu}p_{\mu}p_{\nu}=0
) and the integration is taken with respect to the afore-mentioned measure on the future-directed null cone at
x
. Fix
a\gg 1
, a large parameter governing the hierarchy of estimates, and define the second-order differential operator set

\mathcal{D}^{2}:=\left\{D_{1}D_{2}:D_{1},D_{2}\in\left\{|u|\not{\nabla}_{3},\,a^{1/2}\not{\nabla},\,\not{\nabla}_{4}\right\}\right\},

where
\not{\nabla}
denotes the induced Levi-Civita connection on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
, and the normalization of vector fields is consistent with the double-null framework. Then, for each
u<0
,
\underline{u}\geq 0
, and for every pair of tensorial components

(\alpha,\beta)\in\{(4,4),(4,3),(4,A),(A,3),(A,B),(3,3)\},

the following scale-invariant
L^{2}
-estimates hold on the sphere
S_{u,\underline{u}}
:

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{43}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a},

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{4A}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}},

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{A3}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}},

(9.279)

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{AB}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a},

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{33}\right\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}.

The implicit constants in the estimates are numerical. In addition, let

\mathcal{D}^{3}:=\left\{D_{1}D_{2}D_{3}:D_{1},D_{2},D_{3}\in\left\{|u|\not{\nabla}_{3},\,a^{1/2}\not{\nabla},\,\not{\nabla}_{4}\right\}\right\},

where
\not{\nabla}
denotes the induced Levi-Civita connection on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
, and the normalization of vector fields is consistent with the double-null framework. Then the following estimate holds for the third derivatives of every pair of tensorial components
(\alpha,\beta)\in\{(4,4),(4,3),(4,A),(A,3),(A,B),(3,3)\}
,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{44}\right\|_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{43}\right\|_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a},

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{4A}\right\|_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}},

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{A3}\right\|_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}},

(9.280)

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{AB}\right\|_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a},

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{33}\right\|_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}.

uniformly in
u\in[u,u_{\infty}]
. The constants involved in the estimates are numerical.

Proof.

The proof is a simple consequence of expressing the vector fields
(a^{\frac{1}{2}}e_{A},e_{4},|u|e_{3})
in terms of the vector field
V
s. In light of the identities
9.232
-
9.234
, we can successively express
\mathcal{D}^{2}
in terms of
V^{2}f
. For example, note the following semi-schematic expression for
\mathcal{D}=|u|\nabla_{3}

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{2}f\sim\;

\displaystyle\Big(|u|\,\not{p}\,\eta+|u|\,\not{p}\,\mathcal{D}\eta\Big)V_{(0)}f

\displaystyle+\left(1+\lvert u\rvert\,\frac{\eta_{A}\,p^{A}}{p^{3}}\right)\Bigg[\left(1+\lvert u\rvert\,\frac{\eta_{A}\,p^{A}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(0)}V_{(0)}f+\lvert u\rvert^{3}\left(\frac{({\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{\;A}-e_{A}(b^{B})\,p^{B}+\eta^{A}\,p^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+A)}V_{(0)}f

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt+\lvert u\rvert\,\frac{\eta_{A}\,p^{A}}{p^{3}}V_{(4)}V_{(0)}f\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\Big(|u|^{3}\,\not{p}\,\underline{\chi}+|u|^{3}\,\not{p}\,\mathcal{D}\underline{\chi}+p^{4}\,\mathcal{D}\eta+|u|\,\chi\,\not{p}\,\not{p}\Big)V_{(4+A)}f

\displaystyle+\lvert u\rvert^{3}\left(\frac{({\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{\;A}-e_{A}(b^{B}))\,p^{B}+\eta^{A}\,p^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)\Bigg[\left(1+\lvert u\rvert\,\frac{\eta_{A}\,p^{A}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(0)}V_{(4+A)}f

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt+\lvert u\rvert^{3}\left(\frac{({\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{\;A}-e_{A}(b^{B})\,p^{B}+\eta^{A}\,p^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+A)}V_{(4+A)}f+\lvert u\rvert\,\frac{\eta_{A}\,p^{A}}{p^{3}}V_{(4)}V_{(4+A)}f\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\Big(|u|\,\not{p}\,\eta+|u|\,\not{p}\,\mathcal{D}\eta\Big)V_{(4)}f

\displaystyle+\lvert u\rvert\,\frac{\eta_{A}\,p^{A}}{p^{3}}\Bigg[\left(1+\lvert u\rvert\,\frac{\eta_{A}\,p^{A}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(0)}V_{(4)}f+\lvert u\rvert^{3}\left(\frac{({\underline{\chi}_{B}}^{\;A}-e_{A}(b^{B})\,p^{B}+\eta^{A}\,p^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+A)}V_{(4)}f

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt+\lvert u\rvert\,\frac{\eta_{A}\,p^{A}}{p^{3}}V_{(4)}V_{(4)}f\Bigg],

(9.281)

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{3}f

\displaystyle\sim\mathcal{D}\big(\not{p}|u|\eta+\not{p}|u|\mathcal{D}\eta\big)V_{0}f

\displaystyle\quad+\big(\not{p}|u|\eta+\not{p}|u|\mathcal{D}\eta\big)\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{0}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+A}V_{0}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{0}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\eta\not{p})\mathcal{D}(|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{0}f

\displaystyle\quad+(1+|u|\eta\not{p})^{2}\big(\not{p}|u|\eta+\not{p}|u|\mathcal{D}\eta\big)\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{0}V_{0}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+A}V_{0}V_{0}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{0}V_{0}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{D}(|u|\eta\not{p})|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})V_{4+A}V_{0}f

\displaystyle\quad+(1+|u|\eta\not{p})\big(|u|^{3}\not{p}\underline{\chi}+|u|^{3}\not{p}\mathcal{D}\underline{\chi}+p^{4}\mathcal{D}\eta+|u|(\underline{\chi}-e(b))\not{p}\not{p}\big)V_{4+A}V_{0}f

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\eta\not{p})|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{4+A}V_{0}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+A}V_{4+A}V_{0}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{4+A}V_{0}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+(1+2|u|\underline{\eta}\not{p})\mathcal{D}(|u|\eta\not{p})V_{4}V_{0}f

\displaystyle\quad+|u|\eta\not{p}(1+|u|\eta\not{p})\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{4}V_{0}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+A}V_{4}V_{0}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{4}V_{0}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{D}\big(|u|^{3}\not{p}\underline{\chi}+|u|^{3}\not{p}\mathcal{D}\underline{\chi}+p^{4}\mathcal{D}\eta+|u|(\underline{\chi}-e(b))\not{p}\not{p}\big)V_{4+A}f

\displaystyle\quad+\big(|u|^{3}\not{p}\underline{\chi}+|u|^{3}\not{p}\mathcal{D}\underline{\chi}+p^{4}\mathcal{D}\eta+|u|(\underline{\chi}-e(b))\not{p}\not{p}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{4+A}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+A}V_{4+A}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{4+A}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{D}(|u|\eta\not{p})|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{4+A}f

\displaystyle\quad+(1+|u|\eta\not{p})\big(|u|^{3}\not{p}\underline{\chi}+|u|^{3}\not{p}\mathcal{D}\underline{\chi}+p^{4}\mathcal{D}\eta+|u|(\underline{\chi}-e(b))\not{p}\not{p}\big)V_{0}V_{4+A}f

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\eta\not{p})|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{0}V_{4+A}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+A}V_{0}V_{4+A}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{0}V_{4+A}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})\mathcal{D}\big(|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})\big)V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f

\displaystyle\quad+\big(|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})\big)^{2}\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+C}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{D}(|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p}))|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{4+A}f

\displaystyle\quad+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})\mathcal{D}(|u|\eta\not{p})V_{4}V_{4+A}f

\displaystyle\quad+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})|u|\eta\not{p}\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{4}V_{4+A}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+C}V_{4}V_{4+A}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{4}V_{4+A}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{D}\big(\not{p}|u|\eta+\not{p}|u|\mathcal{D}\eta\big)V_{4}f

\displaystyle\quad+\big(\not{p}|u|\eta+\not{p}|u|\mathcal{D}\eta\big)\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{4}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+C}V_{4}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{4}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+(1+2|u|\underline{\eta}\not{p})\mathcal{D}(|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{4}f

\displaystyle\quad+|u|\eta\not{p}(1+|u|\eta\not{p})\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{0}V_{4}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+C}V_{0}V_{4}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{0}V_{4}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{D}(|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p}))|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4+A}V_{4}f

\displaystyle\quad+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})\mathcal{D}(|u|\eta\not{p})V_{4+A}V_{4}f

\displaystyle\quad+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta\not{p})|u|\eta\not{p}\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{4+A}V_{4}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+C}V_{4+A}V_{4}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{4+A}V_{4}f\big]

\displaystyle\quad+|u|\eta\not{p}\mathcal{D}(|u|\eta\not{p})

\displaystyle\quad+|u|\eta\not{p}\big[(1+|u|\eta\not{p})V_{0}V_{4}V_{4}f+|u|^{3}(\underline{\chi}\not{p}+\eta p^{4})V_{4+C}V_{4}V_{4}f+|u|\eta\not{p}V_{4}V_{4}V_{4}f\big]

(9.282)

where one uses the fact

\displaystyle e_{3}(p^{4})=\frac{\Bigg(2\underline{\chi}_{AB}-\not{g}_{BC}e_{A}(b^{C})-\not{g}_{AC}e_{B}(b^{C})\Bigg)p^{A}p^{B}}{2p^{3}}.

(9.283)

First of all, note that
\Gamma^{C}_{3B}\not{g}_{AC}
appears again. Moreover, note an important point: wherever
V_{4+A}
appears, we absorb a factor of
|u|
with it. For example, we write
V_{K}V_{4+A}f=\frac{1}{|u|}V_{K}(|u|V_{4+A}f)+V_{K}(\frac{1}{|u|})|u|V_{4+A}f
. In light of the estimates on the Ricci coefficients including
\Gamma^{A}_{3B}=\underline{\chi}^{A}_{B}-e_{A}(b^{B})
, the coefficients of

\tilde{V}_{(1)}\tilde{V}_{(2)}f,\hskip 2.84526pt\tilde{V}_{(1)}\tilde{V}_{(2)}\tilde{V}_{(3)}f,

with
\tilde{V}_{i}\in\tilde{V}
for
i=1,2,3
(recall the definition of
\tilde{V}
in equation (
9.5
)), are uniformly bounded by
\lesssim 1
–this is vital, as we shall see soon. The coefficient of the lower derivatives verify better decay estimates, except for the zeroth order term. This will be important and a distinguishing feature of the large data problem. Now we estimate
||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{\alpha\beta}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
for all spacetime indices
\alpha,\beta
. First, recall that

\displaystyle s_{2}(T_{44})

\displaystyle=s_{2}(\not{\nabla}_{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi)=0,

(9.284)

\displaystyle s_{2}(T_{33})

\displaystyle=s_{2}(\not{\nabla}_{3}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})=1+1=2,

(9.285)

\displaystyle s_{2}(T_{A4})

\displaystyle=s_{2}(\not{\nabla}_{4}\eta)=\frac{1}{2},

(9.286)

\displaystyle s_{2}(T_{A3})

\displaystyle=s_{2}(\not{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\eta})=1+\frac{1}{2}=\frac{3}{2},

(9.287)

\displaystyle s_{2}(T_{43})

\displaystyle=s_{2}(\not{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\omega})=1,

(9.288)

\displaystyle s_{2}(\not{T}_{AB})

\displaystyle=s_{2}(T_{AB})=s_{2}(\not{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\hat{\chi}})=1,

(9.289)

by means of the signature conservation of the transport equations for the Ricci coefficients. we estimate
\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}
first:

\displaystyle|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}|

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;\int_{p}\Big[V_{0}V_{0}f+|u|\,V_{4+A}V_{0}f+V_{4}V_{0}f+V_{0}(|u|\,V_{4+A}f)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+|u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f+|u|^{-1}V_{4}(|u|\,V_{4+A}f)+V_{0}V_{4}f+|u|\,V_{4+A}V_{4}f

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+|u|^{-2}V_{4}V_{4}f+|u|^{-1}(V_{0},\,V_{4},\,|u|\,V_{4+A})f\Big]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\,\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{P}|f|\,\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\,\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;|u|^{-1}\Big[\|V_{0}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\||u|\,V_{4+A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{4}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\|V_{0}(|u|\,V_{4+A}f)\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\|V_{4}(|u|\,V_{4+A}f)\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{0}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\||u|\,V_{4+A}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\|V_{4}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|u|^{-1}\|(V_{0},\,V_{4},\,|u|\,V_{4+A})f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big]+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}},

(9.290)

where we have used Hölder’s inequality on mass-shell with the measure
\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}
, the estimate on
f
i.e.,
\sup_{\mathcal{P}|_{u=u_{\infty}}}f=\sup_{\mathcal{P}}f\lesssim a

and the decay estimate on the momentum support. Therefore the
L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
norm of the spacetime entity
|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}|
is estimated as

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim|u|^{-1}\Big[\|V_{0}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\||u|\,V_{4+A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{4}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{0}(|u|\,V_{4+A}f)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{4}(|u|\,V_{4+A}f)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{0}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\||u|\,V_{4+A}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{4}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Big]+\frac{a}{|u|},

(9.291)

where we have used the uniform first-order estimate from Proposition
9.2
, the second derivative estimates from Proposition
9.3
, and the third derivative estimates from Proposition
9.4
. We thus arrive at

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}.

(9.292)

In the scale-invariant norm, noting
s_{2}(\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44})=s_{2}(T_{44})=0
, we have

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}~or~\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

(9.293)

The most important point to note here is that this estimate is sharp when
\mathcal{D}=|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}
. This is due to the fact that when
\mathcal{D}=|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}
hits
T_{44}=\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}
, one generates the following terms

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}T_{44}\sim\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f\hskip 1.42262pt|u|\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+\dots\hskip 1.42262pt,

(9.294)

and subsequently

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}\sim\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}(|u|\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+\dots\hskip 1.42262pt,

(9.295)

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{44}\sim\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}f|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}\Bigg(|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}(|u|\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\Bigg)\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+\dots\hskip 1.42262pt,

(9.296)

and by the estimates on
|\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|}|\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}
, zeroth order estimate on
f

\sup_{\mathcal{P}|_{u=u_{\infty}}}f=\sup_{\mathcal{P}}f=a
, and the decay of momentum support (
3.12
)-(
3.13
)we get

\displaystyle|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}|\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}+\text{better decay},~|\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{44}|\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}+\text{better decay}.

(9.297)

This, in the
\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
–norm, yields

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|},~||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{44}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}.

(9.298)

This is the worst possible case since, if one of
\mathcal{D}
is
a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}
or
\nabla_{4}
, then that would produce additional decay. The largeness of
f
is reflected in this estimate due to its sharpness. It can not be improved further while keeping the norm of
f
fixed. Moving on, we wish to control the following norms

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}T_{44}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})},||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}|u|\nabla_{3})T_{44}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})},||(\not{\nabla}_{4})^{2}T_{44}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{3}T_{44}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})},\dots\hskip 1.42262pt.

(9.299)

For this, we compute
e_{A}e_{B}
in terms of
V_{\mu}V_{\nu}
. First recall

\displaystyle e_{A}=

\displaystyle V_{(A)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{\not{\Gamma}^{C}_{AB}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}}{p^{3}}+{\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{A}}^{C}+\frac{{\chi_{A}}^{C}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+C)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert})V_{(4+A)}+\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}\chi_{AB}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}}{p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\right)(V_{(4)}+V_{(0)})

(9.300)

and so

\displaystyle e_{A}e_{D}f=\Bigg[V_{(A)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{\not{\Gamma}^{C}_{AB}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}}{p^{3}}+{\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{A}}^{C}+\frac{{\chi_{A}}^{C}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+C)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert})V_{(4+A)}+\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}\chi_{AB}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{B}}{p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{A}\right)(V_{(4)}+V_{(0)})\Bigg]\Bigg[V_{(D)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{\not{\Gamma}^{E}_{DF}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{F}}{p^{3}}+{\underline{\hat{\chi}}_{D}}^{E}+\frac{{\chi_{D}}^{E}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{4}}{p^{3}}\right)V_{(4+E)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert})V_{(4+D)}+\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}\chi_{DF}\hskip 1.42262ptp^{F}}{p^{3}}-\underline{\eta}_{D}\right)(V_{(4)}+V_{(0)})\Bigg]f.

(9.301)

We thus infer that the leading order term reads (the remaining lower order terms appear with higher decay and are so irrelevant in the analysis):

\displaystyle e_{A}e_{D}f\sim V_{A}V_{D}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{A}(|u|V_{4+D}f)+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}(V_{A}V_{4}f+V_{A}V_{0}f)+l.o.t.

(9.302)

We will encounter terms of the type
\not{\Gamma}\not{\nabla}T_{44}
while applying
\not{\nabla}
twice. This yields

\displaystyle\big|\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{44}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Bigg|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{A}V_{D}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{A}\big(|u|V_{4+D}f\big)+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\big(V_{A}V_{4}f+V_{A}V_{0}f\big)\Bigg]p^{3}p^{3}\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}\,dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg|+|\not{\Gamma}|\,\big|\not{\nabla}T_{44}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim|u|^{-1}\Bigg[\big\|V_{A}V_{D}f\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}\big\|V_{A}\,|u|V_{4+D}f\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\big\|V_{A}V_{4}f\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\big\|V_{A}V_{0}f\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+|u|^{-1}\Bigg(\big\|Vf\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\big\||u|V_{4+A}f\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\big\|V_{4}f\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\big\|V_{(0)}f\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg).

(9.303)

Here, we have used the fact that
|\not{\Gamma}-\overline{\not{\Gamma}}|\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}
and
|\overline{\not{\Gamma}}|\lesssim 1
(note that these are component-wise norms rather than tensor norm) and first order estimate of
Vf
from Proposition
9.2
.
This leads to the norm estimate

\displaystyle\left\|\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{44}\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle:=\left\|\sqrt{\not{g}^{AC}\not{g}^{BD}\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\nabla}_{B}T_{44}\,\not{\nabla}_{C}\not{\nabla}_{D}T_{44}}\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim|u|^{-3}\Bigg[\left\|V_{A}V_{D}f\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}\left\|V_{A}\,|u|V_{4+D}f\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\left\|V_{A}V_{4}f\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}\left\|V_{A}V_{0}f\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+|u|^{-3}\Bigg(\left\|Vf\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\left\|\,|u|V_{4+A}f\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\left\|V_{4}f\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\left\|V_{(0)}f\right\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}.

(9.304)

\square

Recall that
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{44})=1
and therefore

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{44}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}||\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{44}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|},

(9.305)

which implies

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}T_{44}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}~or~\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

(9.306)

Similarly, one obtains
