\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{3}T_{44}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim|u|^{-4}+\frac{a}{|u|^{4}},

(9.307)

hence

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{3}T_{44}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{4}}+\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{|u|^{4}}.

(9.308)

Noting
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{3}T_{44})=\frac{3}{2}
, we immediately obtain

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{3}T_{44}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}=\frac{|u|^{3}}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Bigg(\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{4}}+\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{|u|^{4}}\Bigg)\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|}+\frac{a}{|u|}~or~\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{44}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim 1.

(9.309)

Now for the
\not{\nabla}_{4}
derivatives, compute

\displaystyle e_{4}e_{4}f=\Bigg[V_{(3)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{{\chi_{B}}^{A}p^{B}}{p^{3}}+2\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}^{A}\right)V_{(4+A)}\Bigg]\Bigg[V_{(3)}+\lvert u\rvert^{2}\left(\frac{{\chi_{C}}^{D}p^{C}}{p^{3}}+2\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}^{C}\right)V_{(4+D)}\Bigg]f

\displaystyle=V_{3}V_{3}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}|u|V_{4+A}f+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f+l.o.t

(9.310)

and note that the lower order terms
l.o.t
involve terms with one derivative of
f
and the coefficients enjoy higher decay since they contain one derivative of the Ricci coefficients. Therefore, we do not repeat these terms; rather, we pay attention to the top-order terms. Explicit computations yield

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}\not{T}_{44}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Bigg|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{3}V_{3}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}\big(|u|V_{4+A}f\big)+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\big(|u|V_{4+A}\big)\big(|u|V_{4+D}\big)f\Bigg]p^{3}p^{3}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\,dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\big(|f\nabla_{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi|+|f\operatorname{tr}\chi\operatorname{tr}\chi|\big)\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\,dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+\text{l.o.t.}

\displaystyle\lesssim|u|^{-1}\Bigg[\|V_{3}V_{3}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}\|V_{3}(|u|V_{4+A}f)\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\|(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg],

(9.311)

and so the following holds

\displaystyle\|\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{44}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim|u|^{-1}\Bigg[\|V_{3}V_{3}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}\|V_{3}(|u|V_{4+A}f)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\|(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}.

(9.312)

Consequently,

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{44}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}~or~\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{44}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1

(9.313)

since
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{44})=0
. Next, we move on to estimating the remaining stress-energy tensor components.

T_{A3}
:
First consider
\mathcal{D}=|u|\nabla_{3}
. We compute

\displaystyle|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{A3}|\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{0}V_{0}f+|u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f+V_{4}V_{0}f+V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)+|u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f

\displaystyle\quad+|u|^{-1}V_{4}(|u|V_{4+A}f)+V_{0}V_{4}f+|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f+|u|^{-2}V_{4}V_{4}f\Bigg]|p^{4}p_{A}|\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f|\,|p^{4}p_{A}|\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+\text{l.o.t.}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle|u|^{-3}\Bigg[\|V_{0}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\||u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{4}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{4}(|u|V_{4+A}f)\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{0}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\||u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{4}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{a}{|u|^{4}},

(9.314)

where the lower order terms
l.o.t
involve first derivatives as well as terms with higher decay structure produced as a result of
e_{3}
acting on
p^{4}
. Using the definition of the
L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
norm, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{A3}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\;|u|^{-4}\Bigg[\|V_{0}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\||u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{4}V_{0}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\|V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{0}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\||u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|V_{4}V_{4}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}

(9.315)

and by the estimates on Vlasov

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{A3}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}

(9.316)

and similarly, for the third derivatives

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{A3}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}

(9.317)

or in scale invariant norm noting
s_{2}(\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{A3})=s_{2}(T_{A3})=\frac{3}{2}

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{A3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}|u|}+\frac{|u|^{3}}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}|u|}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|},

(9.318)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{A3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}|u|}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|},

(9.319)

or

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{A3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}\lesssim 1,~\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{A3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

(9.320)

Next, consider

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{E3}|=

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{A}V_{D}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{A}(|u|V_{4+D}f)+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}(V_{A}V_{4}f+V_{A}V_{0}f\Bigg]p_{E}p^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+\not{\Gamma}\not{\Gamma}T_{E3}|+l.o.t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-3}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{A}|u|V_{4+D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]

(9.321)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}.

(9.322)

Passing to the
L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})-
norm, we obtain

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{E3}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}:=

\displaystyle||\sqrt{\not{g}^{AC}\not{g}^{BD}\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\nabla}_{B}T_{E3}\not{\nabla}_{C}\not{\nabla}_{D}T_{F3}\not{g}^{EF}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-6}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{A}|u|V_{4+D}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{6}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{6}},

(9.323)

which, after noting that
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{E3})=1+1+\frac{1}{2}=\frac{5}{2}
yields

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}T_{E3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|},\hskip 8.53581pt\text{or}\hskip 8.53581pt\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{E3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

(9.324)

Similarly, for the third derivatives, the same calculations yield

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{E3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

(9.325)

Moving on to
\not{\nabla}_{4}^{2}T_{E3}
,

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{E3}|=

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{3}V_{3}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}|u|V_{4+A}f+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f\Bigg]p_{E}p^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+l.o.t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-3}\Bigg[||V_{3}V_{3}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}||\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}||(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg],

(9.326)

hence

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{E3}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=

\displaystyle||\sqrt{\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{E3}\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{F3}\not{g}^{EF}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

(9.327)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-4}\Bigg[||V_{3}V_{3}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}||(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}.

Noting that
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{E3})=1+\frac{1}{2}=\frac{3}{2}
, the scale-invariant version becomes

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{E3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

(9.328)

In an exactly similar fashion, one obtains

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\nabla^{3}_{4}T_{E3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

(9.329)

Similarly, one obtains
\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{E3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim 1
and
\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{E3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1
for all
\mathcal{D}
.

For
T_{4A}
:

\displaystyle|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{4A}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{0}V_{0}f+|u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f+V_{4}V_{0}f+V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)+|u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|^{-1}V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f+V_{0}V_{4}f+|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f+|u|^{-2}V_{4}V_{4}f\Bigg]\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f|\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+l.o.t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-1}\Bigg[||V_{0}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|||u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt+|||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

(9.330)

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt+|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}.

Passing to the
L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
norms yields

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-2}\Bigg[||V_{0}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|||u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}.

(9.331)

For the commutator
(\mathcal{D},\not{\nabla}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{2}\not{T}_{4A}
, we have

\displaystyle||(\mathcal{D},\not{\nabla}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim|u|^{-2}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}},

(9.332)

hence by the estimates on Vlasov from Proposition
9.3
, we have

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}},~||(\mathcal{D},\not{\nabla}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}

(9.333)

or, in scale invariant norms, noting
s_{2}(\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{4A})=s_{2}(T_{4A})=\frac{1}{2}
,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1,~\frac{|u|}{a}||(\mathcal{D},\not{\nabla}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

(9.334)

For
\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{4E}
, we have

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{4E}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{A}V_{D}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{A}(|u|V_{4+D}f)+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}(V_{A}V_{4}f+V_{A}V_{0}f\Bigg]p_{E}p^{3}\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}|+|\not{\Gamma}\not{\Gamma}T_{4E}|+l.o.t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-1}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{A}|u|V_{4+D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}.

This implies

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{4E}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=

\displaystyle||\sqrt{\not{g}^{AC}\not{g}^{BD}\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\nabla}_{B}T_{4E}\not{\nabla}_{C}\not{\nabla}_{D}T_{4F}\not{g}^{EF}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-4}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{A}|u|V_{4+D}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

(9.335)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}

(9.336)

and similarly

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{3}T_{4E}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim|u|^{-5}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}.

(9.337)

Since
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{j}T_{4E})=1+\frac{j}{2}=\frac{3}{2}
,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}T_{4E}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1,

(9.338)

and similarly

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{3}T_{4E}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

(9.339)

The rest of the derivatives
\mathcal{D}
are handled similarly.

For
T_{AB}
:
First, consider
\mathcal{D}=|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}
and observe

\displaystyle|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{AB}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{0}V_{0}f+|u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f+V_{4}V_{0}f+V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)+|u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|^{-1}V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f+V_{0}V_{4}f+|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f+|u|^{-2}V_{4}V_{4}f\Bigg]p_{A}p_{B}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|fp_{A}p_{B}|\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+l.o.t.

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-1}\Bigg[||V_{0}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|||u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{AB}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-3}\Bigg[||V_{0}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|||u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}.

(9.340)

Similarly, for the three derivatives

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{AB}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim|u|^{-3}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}

(9.341)

and by the estimates on Vlasov from proposition
9.3

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{AB}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{3}},~||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{AB}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}

(9.342)

or in scale invariant norms, noting
s_{2}(\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{AB})=s_{2}(T_{AB})=1
,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{AB}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})},\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{AB}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1.

(9.343)

Next we consider
\mathcal{D}=a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}
. Note

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{EF}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{A}V_{D}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{A}(|u|V_{4+D}f)+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}(V_{A}V_{4}f+V_{A}V_{0}f\Bigg]p_{E}p_{F}\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg|+|\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\Gamma}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{T}_{EF}|+l.o.t.

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-1}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{A}|u|V_{4+D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}},

(9.344)

hence

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{EF}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}:=||\sqrt{\not{g}^{AC}\not{g}^{BD}\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\nabla}_{B}T_{HE}\not{\nabla}_{C}\not{\nabla}_{D}T_{IF}\not{g}^{HI}\not{g}^{EF}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-5}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{A}|u|V_{4+D}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{5}},

(9.345)

and similarly

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{3}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-6}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a}{|u|^{6}}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{6}}.

(9.346)

and taking into account the signature of
T_{4E}
we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{EF}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1

(9.347)

and

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{EF}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1.

(9.348)

Next, we consider
\mathcal{D}=\not{\nabla}_{4}
. We compute

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{EF}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{3}V_{3}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}|u|V_{4+A}f+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f\Bigg]p_{E}p_{F}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f|\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+l.o.t.

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-1}\Bigg[||V_{3}V_{3}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}||\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}||(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}},

(9.349)

since
|\operatorname{tr}\chi|\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{|u|}\lesssim 1
, so

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{EF}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=||\sqrt{\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{IE}\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{HF}\not{g}^{IH}\not{g}^{EF}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-3}\Bigg[||V_{3}V_{3}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}||(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}.

(9.350)

Similarly

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{3}_{4}T_{AB}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim|u|^{-3}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}

(9.351)

Consequently, noting
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{EF})=1=s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{3}_{4}T_{EF})
, we obtain the scale-invariant estimates

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{EF}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1,\hskip 8.53581pt\frac{|u|}{a}||\not{\nabla}^{3}_{4}T_{EF}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1.

(9.352)

For
T_{43}
, as with all previous components,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{43}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{0}V_{0}f+|u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f+V_{4}V_{0}f+V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)+|u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|^{-1}V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f+V_{0}V_{4}f+|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f+|u|^{-2}V_{4}V_{4}f\Bigg]p^{4}p^{3}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|f|p^{4}|p^{3}|\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+l.o.t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-3}\Bigg[||V_{0}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|||u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{4}},

(9.353)

and

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{43}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-3}\Bigg[||V_{0}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|||u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{3}},

(9.354)

and similarly,

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim|u|^{-3}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}

(9.355)

and by the estimates on Vlasov from the proposition
9.3

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{43}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{4}},~||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{4}},

(9.356)

or in scale invariant norm noting
s_{2}(\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{43})=s_{2}(T_{43})=1,~s_{2}(\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{43})=s_{2}(T_{43})=1

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{43}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|},~||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|}~or~

(9.357)

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{43}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1,~\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1.

(9.358)

Next we consider
\mathcal{D}:=a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}
and compute

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{43}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{A}V_{D}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{A}(|u|V_{4+D}f)+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}(V_{A}V_{4}f+V_{A}V_{0}f\Bigg]p^{4}p^{3}\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}|+|\not{\Gamma}\not{\nabla}T_{43}|+l.o.t.

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-3}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{A}|u|V_{4+D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}

(9.359)

and recalling

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{43}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=||\sqrt{\not{g}^{AC}\not{g}^{BD}\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\nabla}_{B}T_{43}\not{\nabla}_{C}\not{\nabla}_{D}T_{43}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-5}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{A}|u|V_{4+D}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}

(9.360)

and similarly

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{43}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-5}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}.

(9.361)

Taking into account that
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{43})=1+1=2,~s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{3}T_{43})=\frac{3}{2}+1=\frac{5}{2}
,

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{43}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a|u|}\implies||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}T_{43}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|},

(9.362)

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{3}T_{43}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}|u|}\implies||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{3}T_{43}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|},

(9.363)

or

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{43}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1,~\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{43}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1.

(9.364)

Now we consider
\mathcal{D}=\not{\nabla}_{4}
and evaluate

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{43}|=\Bigg|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{3}V_{3}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}|u|V_{4+A}f+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f\Bigg]p^{4}p^{3}\frac{\sqrt{\det\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg|+l.o.t.

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-3}\Bigg[||V_{3}V_{3}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{3}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}||(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]

(9.365)

and so

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{43}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-3}\Bigg[||V_{3}V_{3}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}||(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}.

(9.366)

For the third derivatives,

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{43}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim|u|^{-3}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

(9.367)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}

(9.368)

where we have used the Vlasov estimates from proposition
9.3
.
Consequently noting
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{43})=1=s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{3}_{4}T_{43})

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{EF}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|},~||\not{\nabla}^{3}_{4}T_{EF}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|}~or~

(9.369)

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{EF}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1,~\frac{|u|}{a}||\not{\nabla}^{3}_{4}T_{EF}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\lesssim 1.

(9.370)

T_{33}
:
Recall that
T_{33}
has the maximum decay rate among the stress-energy tensor components due to the appearance of
p^{4}p^{4}
. First consider
\mathcal{D}:=|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}
and evaluate

\displaystyle|\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{33}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{p}\Bigg[V_{0}V_{0}f+|u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f+V_{4}V_{0}f+V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)+|u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|^{-1}V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f+V_{0}V_{4}f+|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f+|u|^{-2}V_{4}V_{4}f\Bigg]p^{4}p^{4}\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}|fp^{4}p^{4}|\sqrt{\det\not{g}}\frac{dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}+l.o.t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-5}\Bigg[||V_{0}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|||u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{6}},

(9.371)

hence, in the
L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})-
norm,

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{33}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-5}\Bigg[||V_{0}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+|||u|V_{4+A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|||u|^{2}V_{4+A}V_{4+B}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle||V_{4}|u|V_{4+A}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{0}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|V_{4+A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+||V_{4}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]+\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}.

(9.372)

Similarly, for three derivatives

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim|u|^{-5}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}.

(9.373)

By the estimates on Vlasov from Proposition
9.3

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{33}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{5}},~||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{5}},

(9.374)

or in scale invariant norms, noting
s_{2}(\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{33})=s_{2}(T_{33})=2=s_{2}(\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{33})
,

\displaystyle||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a|u|},~||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a|u|}~\text{or}~

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}\lesssim 1,~\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(9.375)

Now consider
\mathcal{D}=a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}
and evaluate

\displaystyle|\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{33}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{A}V_{D}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{A}(|u|V_{4+D}f)+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}(V_{A}V_{4}f+V_{A}V_{0}f\Bigg]p^{4}p^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}|+|\not{\Gamma}\not{\nabla}T_{33}|+l.o.t.

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-5}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{A}|u|V_{4+D}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]

(9.376)

and recalling

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{33}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=

\displaystyle||\sqrt{\not{g}^{AC}\not{g}^{BD}\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\nabla}_{B}T_{33}\not{\nabla}_{C}\not{\nabla}_{D}T_{33}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-7}\Bigg[||V_{A}V_{D}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}||V_{A}|u|V_{4+D}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{4}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|^{2}}||V_{A}V_{0}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{7}}.

(9.377)

Similarly,

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim|u|^{-8}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{8}}

(9.378)

and so, noting
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{33})=1+2=3,s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{3}T_{33})=\frac{3}{2}+2=\frac{7}{2}
,

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}|u|}\implies||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{2}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a|u|},

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{5}{2}}|u|}\implies||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a|u|},

or equivalently,

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{2}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}\lesssim 1,~\frac{|u|}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(9.379)

Finally, consider
\mathcal{D}=\not{\nabla}_{4}
and compute

\displaystyle|\nabla^{2}_{4}T_{33}|=

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{\mathcal{P}_{x}}\Bigg[V_{3}V_{3}f+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}|u|V_{4+A}f+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f\Bigg]p^{4}p^{4}\frac{\sqrt{\not{g}}dp^{1}dp^{2}dp^{3}}{p^{3}}\Bigg|+l.o.t.

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-5}\Bigg[||V_{3}V_{3}f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}||\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}||(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg],

(9.380)

from where it follows that

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{33}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-5}\Bigg[||V_{3}V_{3}f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u|}V_{3}(|u|V_{4+A}f)||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}||(|u|V_{4+A})(|u|V_{4+D})f||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\Bigg]\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{5}}.

(9.381)

Similarly, for three derivatives,

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}_{4}^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}(H_{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle|u|^{-5}||\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=3}||(|u|V_{0})^{i_{1}}(|u|V_{4+A})^{i_{2}}(V_{4})^{i_{3}}(V_{A})^{i_{4}}(V_{3})^{i_{5}}f||_{L^{2}(H_{u})L^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{x})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{5}},

(9.382)

where the derivative estimates for the Vlasov fields from Proposition
9.3
are utilized.
Consequently, noting
s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{33})=2=s_{2}(\not{\nabla}^{3}_{4}T_{33})

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a|u|},~||\nabla_{4}^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a|u|}~or~

\displaystyle\frac{|u|}{a}||\not{\nabla}^{2}_{4}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}\lesssim 1,~\frac{|u|}{a}||\not{\nabla}_{4}^{3}T_{33}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(9.383)

Remark 14

Note that given the
L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
estimates on the first and second derivatives of the stress-energy tensor components,
||\mathcal{D}T_{\mu\nu}||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
is controlled automatically by means of the scale-invariant Sobolev estimates. Notice that we do not have the same privilege for the Weyl curvature components since their second derivatives are controlled on the null hypersurfaces and therefore co-dimension one trace inequalities are indispensable.

10
Energy Estimates for the Weyl curvature: Completion of the proof of the semi-global existence theorem

In this final section, we complete the energy estimates for Weyl curvature components in terms of the initial data and therefore close the boot-strap. There are several ways to execute the energy estimates. Since Einstein’s gravity is a variational theory, one expects the existence of a stress-energy tensor. However, due to the equivalence principle (or existence of geodesic normal coordinates), one can not simply find a stress-energy tensor for gravity. For the purpose of obtaining estimates, the Bel-Robinson tensor suffices. However, here we will adopt a direct integration by parts approach.
First, recall the following generalized Grönwall type proposition

Proposition 10.1

Let
f(x,y),\hskip 1.42262ptg(x,y)
be positive functions defined on a rectangle

U:=\begin{Bmatrix}0\leq x\leq x_{0},\hskip 1.42262pt0\leq y\leq y_{0}\end{Bmatrix}.

Suppose there exist constants
J,c_{1},c_{2}
such that

f(x,y)+g(x,y)\lesssim J+c_{1}\int_{0}^{x}f(x^{\prime},y)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}x^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{y}g(x,y^{\prime})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}y^{\prime},

for all
(x,y)\in U
. Then there holds

\forall(x,y)\in U:\hskip 14.22636ptf(x,y)+g(x,y)\lesssim J\mathrm{e}^{c_{1}x+c_{2}y}.

Proposition 10.2

For a Bianchi pair
(\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2})\in\left\{(\alpha,\widetilde{\beta}),(\widetilde{\beta},(\rho,\sigma)),((\rho,\sigma),\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}),(\widetilde{\underline{\beta}},\underline{\alpha})\right\}
satisfying

\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}+\left(\frac{i+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1})\right)\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2}=P_{i},

\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2}-\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}=Q_{i},

the following holds true:

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})}}||\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}||\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,u^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1},P_{i}\rangle}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2},Q_{i}\rangle}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

Proof.

For the proof of this scale-invariant energy estimates, see
[
4
]
.

\square

Proposition 10.3

Let the hypotheses of Theorem
1.2
be in force, and suppose in addition that the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
) hold on the spacetime domain under consideration. Let

\mathcal{D}\in\big\{\,|u|\,\not{\nabla}_{3},\,a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla},\,\not{\nabla}_{4}\,\big\}

be one of the admissible commutation operators defined in Section
3.5
, and let
i
be an integer satisfying
0\leq i\leq 2
. Then the following
scale-invariant
L^{2}
energy estimate
holds:

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\;+\;\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\;\;\lesssim\;\;\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\;+\;\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\;+\;\frac{C}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.

(10.1)

In particular, for the case
\mathcal{D}=\mathrm{Id}
(i.e. without commutation), (
10.1
) reads

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\lVert{\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\;+\;\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\lVert{\widetilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\;\;\lesssim\;\;\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\lVert{\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\;+\;\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\lVert{\widetilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\;+\;\frac{C}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.

(10.2)

Here:

•

\lVert{\cdot}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}
and
\lVert{\cdot}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}
denote the scale-invariant
L^{2}
flux norms along the outgoing and incoming null hypersurfaces
H_{u}
and
\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}
, respectively.

•

\mathcal{D}^{i}
denotes the
i
-fold composition of
\mathcal{D}
with itself.

Proof.

The vital point to note here is that the bulk terms in estimating the energy involve a spacetime integral, and therefore the stress-energy terms involving the Vlasov source are always controllable on
H
. With this fundamental notion, we proceed to estimate the energy by means of Bianchi-pair integration. The energy estimates for
\not{\nabla}
are easy to prove. We consider the top derivatives.
First, recall the equations

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{\nabla}^{i}\alpha+\frac{i+1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{\nabla}^{i}\alpha-\hat{\mathcal{D}}\not{\nabla}^{i}\tilde{\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\beta^{R}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho,\sigma,\beta^{R})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}T_{4A}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{AB}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}T_{A3}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha:=P_{i}.

(10.3)

Similarly, we obtain for
\widetilde{\beta}

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{\nabla}^{i}\tilde{\beta}-\prescript{*}{}{\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\not{\nabla}^{i}\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\widetilde{\beta},\alpha)+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}T_{4A}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}T_{4A}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}T_{4A}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{4A}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\beta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\beta}:=Q_{i}

(10.4)

Now we apply the energy lemma.
By Hölder’s inequality, one has

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\leq&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{9}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{j}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\cdot\sup_{u^{\prime}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\alpha\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\end{split}

(10.5)

Let us focus on the sum in the above line. For the first three terms, there holds

\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{j}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma\cdot R}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}.

For the fourth term, there holds

\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{4}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Gamma^{2}+\Gamma R.

For the fifth and sixth terms, there holds

\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{5}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{6}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Gamma^{3}+\Gamma^{2}R.

The seventh term is estimated as follows:

\displaystyle a^{\frac{3}{2}}R\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{i}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{i}\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{AB}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{\frac{3}{2}}R\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{a|u^{\prime}|}\hskip 1.42262pta\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\hskip 1.42262ptR\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1,

(10.6)

where we have used the top derivative Vlasov estimate from proposition
9.6
. Next, for the lower order terms in the following is estimated by
L^{4}(S)-L^{4}(S)
type estimates and using the co-dimension-1 trace inequality or the Sobolev embedding

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}a^{\frac{3}{2}}R\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{i}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\nabla_{4}T_{AB}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u^{\prime}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}R\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1

(10.7)

Next we estimate

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}a^{\frac{3}{2}}R\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{i}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}T_{4A}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u^{\prime}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{\frac{3}{2}}R\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u^{\prime}|}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{aR\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1

(10.8)

and for the ninth term

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}a^{\frac{3}{2}}R\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{i}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}T_{A3}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u^{\prime}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}

(10.9)

we control in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})-\mathcal{L}^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
and use the scale-invariant codimension-1 trace inequality

\displaystyle||\varphi||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim||\varphi||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}+||\not{\nabla}_{4}\varphi||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(H)}\left(|||\varphi||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(H)}+||a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\varphi||^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{sc}(H)}\right)

(10.10)

to control
\not{\nabla}\alpha
in
L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})

\displaystyle||\not{\nabla}\alpha||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim||\not{\nabla}\alpha||_{L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}+\mathcal{R}

(10.11)

and so

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}a^{\frac{3}{2}}R\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{i}{2}}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}T_{A3}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u^{\prime}})}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim a^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{R}\mathcal{V}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{R}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(10.12)

Collecting all the terms, we obtain

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\Gamma^{3}+\Gamma^{2}R+\Gamma R+1.

(10.13)

Similarly, for the analogous term involving
\tilde{\beta}
, there holds

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot a^{\frac{i}{2}}\tilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\leq&\sum_{j=1}^{7}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{j}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\cdot\sup_{u^{\prime}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\tilde{\beta}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
&\lesssim a^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{R}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{7}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{j}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.\end{split}

(10.14)

Note the important point that the Vlasov field can only be controlled on
H_{u}
, so naturally, we control the term
Q_{i}
involving the Vlasov stress-energy terms along
H_{u}
. The first term is estimated as follows

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma(R+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(10.15)

The sixth term is estimated as

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{6}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma(R+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(10.16)

For the seventh term, we have

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{7}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{2}(R+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}

(10.17)

Now the source terms are controlled. The second term is controlled as

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}T_{4A}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\mathcal{V}(\mathcal{V}+\Gamma)}{a|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{V}(1+\Gamma)}{|u|^{2}}.

(10.18)

The third term is estimated as

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}T_{4A}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{a|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}.

(10.19)

The fourth term is estimated as

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}T_{4A}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}},

whereas finally the top order term (the fifth term)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\nabla^{i_{3}}\nabla_{4}T_{AB}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a\frac{\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{a|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{a\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{3}}\lesssim\frac{a\mathcal{V}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}.

(10.20)

Collecting all the terms together, we obtain

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\tilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\tilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
&+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\tilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\tilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.\end{split}

(10.21)

This proves the claim when
\mathcal{D}=a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}
. We actually need a commutation for
[\mathcal{D}^{k},\not{\nabla}]
appearing from the Hodge term in the Bianchi equation for
\mathcal{D}=|u|\nabla_{3},\nabla_{4}
. First, we obtain the relevant equations for the pair
(\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha,\mathcal{D}^{i}\beta)
for
\max{i}=2
. To this end, we recall the following lemma.

Lemma 10.1

Assume a tensorfield
\phi
satisfies an equation of the form

\not{\nabla}_{3}\phi+a\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\phi=G_{0}.

there holds:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}^{k+\ell}\phi+a\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{k+\ell}\phi=G_{i},

where

\displaystyle G_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\big(\eta,\underline{\eta},1\big)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}G_{0}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{\nabla}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,T_{34})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\beta},T_{3A})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\phi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{T}_{33}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi.

(10.22)

Here,
\psi_{g}\in\{\eta,\underline{\eta},1\}.

Now, the important point here is that for the Bianchi system,
G_{0}
actually contains the Hodge term, which is principal.
Let us recall the equations for
\alpha
and
\beta
:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\alpha+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha=\not{\nabla}\widehat{\otimes}\beta+P,

(10.23)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\beta=\not{\mbox{div }}\alpha+Q.

(10.24)

Here,
G_{0}
is
\not{\nabla}\widehat{\otimes}\beta+P
. Now note the following commutation formula. Define, for an arbitrary
k
,

a^{k}:=[\mathcal{D},\not{\nabla}]\mathcal{D}^{k-1}\Phi_{2},

b^{k}:=\mathcal{D}[\mathcal{D}^{k-1},\not{\nabla}]\Phi_{2}.

We compute

\displaystyle[\mathcal{D}^{k},\not{\nabla}]\Phi_{2}=

\displaystyle[\mathcal{D},\not{\nabla}]\mathcal{D}^{k-1}\Phi_{2}+\mathcal{D}[\mathcal{D}^{k-1},\not{\nabla}]\Phi_{2}=a^{k}+b^{k}=a^{k}+\mathcal{D}(a^{k-1}+b^{k-1})=\dots

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{l}a^{k-l}=\sum_{l=1}^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k-l}a^{l}.

(10.25)

Therefore, the goal is to evaluate
a^{j}
for an arbitrary
j
. First, for
\mathcal{D}=|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}
,

\displaystyle a^{j}=

\displaystyle[\mathcal{D},\not{\nabla}]\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}=|u|(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}+|u|\underline{\beta}\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{j}\Phi_{2}+|u|(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\eta\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}

(10.26)

and for
\mathcal{D}=\not{\nabla}_{4}
,

\displaystyle a^{j}=[\mathcal{D},\not{\nabla}]\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}=(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}+\Bigg((\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}+\beta+\not{T}_{4}\Bigg)\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}.

(10.27)

Hence, explicitly, the commutation reads for
\mathcal{D}:=|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}
,

\displaystyle[\mathcal{D}^{k},\not{\nabla}]\Phi_{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k-j}\Bigg(|u|(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}+|u|\underline{\beta}\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{j}\Phi_{2}+|u|(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|u|(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\eta\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k-j}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{k}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k-j}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\underline{\beta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}+j-1}\Phi_{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-j}\mathcal{D}^{j_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}+j}\Phi_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{k}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k-j}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+j-1}\Phi_{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k-j}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\eta\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+j-1}\Phi_{2},

(10.28)

while for
\mathcal{D}=\not{\nabla}_{4}
,

\displaystyle[\mathcal{D}^{k},\not{\nabla}]\Phi_{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-j}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{j-1}\Phi_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{k}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k-j}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}+j-1}\Phi_{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-j}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\beta\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}+j-1}\Phi_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{k}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-j}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\not{T}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}+j-1}\Phi_{2}.

(10.29)

Therefore, recalling Proposition

3.18
, the commuted equation for
\alpha
reads

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha=\nabla\widehat{\otimes}\mathcal{D}^{i}\beta+P^{1}_{i},

where
P^{1}_{i}
reads

\displaystyle P^{1}_{i}=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\big(\eta,\underline{\eta},1\big)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\nabla\beta+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\big(\eta,\underline{\eta},1\big)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}P

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{\nabla}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,T_{34})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\beta},T_{3}A)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{T}_{33}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\beta

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\underline{\beta},T_{A3})\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\beta

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\beta

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\beta

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\eta\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\beta,

(10.30)

for
\mathcal{D}=|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}

and

\displaystyle P^{1}_{i}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\big(\eta,\underline{\eta},1\big)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\nabla\beta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mbox{div}\mkern-19.0mu/\,\,\,\,\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\chi\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\rho,\not{T}_{34})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\beta},\not{T}_{3})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}+1}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\not{T}_{33}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\not{\nabla}\beta

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\beta

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\beta

(10.31)

for
\mathcal{D}=\not{\nabla}_{4}
.
We now commute with
\not{\nabla}^{j}
such that
i+j=2

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha+(\frac{j+1}{2})\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha=\not{\nabla}\widehat{\otimes}\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\beta+P_{i,j},

where
P_{i,j}
reads

\displaystyle P_{i,j}:=\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j-1}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}P^{1}_{i}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j-1}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}+1}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j-1}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}\psi^{j_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}+1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j-1}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}\psi^{j_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}\psi^{j_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}(\rho,\sigma,\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}),

(10.32)

with
P_{i,j}=P^{1}_{i}
for
j=0
. Now we commute with the
\not{\nabla}_{4}
equation, which is much simpler. First, recall the proposition.
Assume a tensor-field
\phi
satisfies
\not{\nabla}_{4}\phi=F_{0}
. Then, if
\mathcal{D}\in\{\lvert u\rvert\not{\nabla}_{3},\not{\nabla}_{4},a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}\}
, there holds
\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{i}\phi:=F_{i},
where

\displaystyle F_{i}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}F_{0}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\phi

\displaystyle+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\phi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+\dots+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\phi.

(10.33)

We recall that here
\psi_{g}\in\{\eta,\underline{\eta},\lvert u\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\omega}\}
. We use it in the equation

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\widetilde{\beta}=\not{\mbox{div }}\alpha+Q,

to yield

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}=\not{\mbox{div }}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha+Q^{1}_{i},

(10.34)

where
Q^{1}_{i}
reads

\displaystyle Q^{1}_{i}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\lvert u\rvert\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}Q

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}+1}\widetilde{\beta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\operatorname{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\widetilde{\beta}

\displaystyle+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\beta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\beta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\beta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+\dots+i_{6}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lvert u\rvert\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{6}}\widetilde{\beta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\underline{\beta},T_{A3})\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\beta

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\eta\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha,

(10.35)

for
\mathcal{D}=|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}
and

\displaystyle Q^{1}_{i}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\not{\nabla}\alpha
