\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\underline{\eta}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\beta,\not{T}_{4})\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\alpha,

(10.36)

for
\mathcal{D}=\not{\nabla}_{4}
.
We now want to commute
\not{\nabla}^{j}
such that
i+j=2
, obtaining

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}=\not{\mbox{div }}\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha+Q_{i,j},

(10.37)

where
Q_{i,j}
reads

\displaystyle Q_{i,j}=\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}Q^{1}_{i}+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}\psi^{j_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}(\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta},\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha)+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}\psi^{j_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j-2}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}\psi^{j_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}.

(10.38)

Here, the terms containing
j-2
vanish when
j-2<0
.
Collecting the principal terms, we have the coupled Bianchi system

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha+(\frac{j+1}{2})\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha=\not{\nabla}\widehat{\otimes}\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\tilde{\beta}+P_{i,j},

(10.39)

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}=\not{\mbox{div }}\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha+Q_{i,j}.

(10.40)

The vital point to note here is that
s_{2}(\Psi)=s_{2}(\mathcal{D}\Psi)
and therefore we can apply the energy lemma for the fields
\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha
and
\mathcal{D}^{i}\tilde{\beta}
, namely

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\tilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})}}||\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}||\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\tilde{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,u^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha,P_{i,j}\rangle}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle\not{\nabla}^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta},Q_{i,j}\rangle}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(10.41)

By Hölder, one has

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{j}{2}}P_{i,j}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\leq&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\sum_{k}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{j}{2}}P_{i,j}^{k}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\cdot\sup_{u^{\prime}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\end{split}

(10.42)

We only control explicitly the borderline terms here, while the rest are of higher decay. Consider the following terms

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j-1}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}^{2}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}^{2}\Gamma\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{R}^{2}}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{a^{2}\Gamma\mathcal{R}^{2}}{|u|^{3}},

(10.43)

where we have used
L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})-L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})
and
L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})-L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})-L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})
estimates. Consider the next term

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Gamma a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{\Gamma|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Gamma\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma\mathcal{R}}{|u|}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|}.

(10.44)

The next borderline term is estimated as

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\Gamma\mathcal{R}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Gamma\frac{\Gamma|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{2}}.

(10.45)

The next terms are estimated as (recall that
i+j=2
):

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j\\
i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1\\
k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}=j_{3}\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}\underline{\eta}\,\not{\nabla}^{k_{3}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{\Gamma}{a}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\Gamma}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{3}},

(10.46)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j\\
i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1\\
k_{1}+k_{2}=j_{3}\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},1)^{i_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}\widetilde{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\mathcal{R}}{a}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\Gamma\frac{\mathcal{R}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}}{|u|}+\frac{a\Gamma\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{2}},

(10.47)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j\\
i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1\\
k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}=j_{3}\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\not{\nabla}^{k_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{k_{3}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\eta\not{\nabla}^{k_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}|u^{\prime}|\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{R}a^{-\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{R},

(10.48)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j\\
i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1\\
k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}=j_{3}\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\not{\nabla}^{k_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{k_{3}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}|u^{\prime}|\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}\Gamma}{a}\frac{\mathcal{R}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{R}}{|u|},

(10.49)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j\\
i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i\\
k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}=j_{3}\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},1)^{i_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{AB}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{\Gamma}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{\mathcal{V}}{|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{3}},

(10.50)

where we control the second derivative term
\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}(\eta,\underline{\eta})
in
L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
and
\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{AB}
in
L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
,which in turn is controllable by means of
||\sum_{i\leq 2}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{AB}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
by means of scale-invariant Sobolev embedding. The remaining terms enjoy similar or better estimates. Consequently, collecting all the terms, we have the following estimate for the
P_{i}
terms

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{j}{2}}P_{i,j}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\Gamma^{2}+\Gamma\mathcal{R}+1.

(10.51)

Now we control the remaining error term

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{j}{2}}Q_{i,j}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}\tilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\leq&\sum_{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{j}{2}}Q^{k}_{i,j}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\cdot\sup_{u^{\prime}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}\tilde{\beta}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}.\end{split}

(10.52)

Now we control the worst terms, and the remaining terms verify better or similar estimates.

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{j}{2}}Q^{k}_{i,j}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(10.53)

Again, we estimate term by term and provide the estimates for the terms with least decay

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j\\
i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1\\
k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}=j_{3}\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u|\not{\nabla}^{k_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{k_{3}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}|u^{\prime}|\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}\Gamma}{a}\mathcal{R}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma\mathcal{R}}{|u|},

(10.54)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}\psi^{j_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\psi_{g})\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}(\mathcal{D}^{i}\tilde{\beta},\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{R}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{R},

(10.55)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=j}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}\psi^{j_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{j_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}^{j_{4}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\widetilde{\beta}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma\frac{\mathcal{R}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{2}},

(10.56)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j\\
i_{1}+i_{2}=i\\
k_{1}+k_{2}=j_{3}\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}^{k_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\alpha||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\mathcal{R}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{2}},

(10.57)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j\\
i_{1}+i_{2}=i\\
k_{1}+k_{2}=j_{3}\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\not{\nabla}^{k_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\alpha||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{|u^{\prime}|}\mathcal{R}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{2}},

(10.58)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j\\
i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1\\
k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}+k_{4}=j_{3}\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}|u^{\prime}|\not{\nabla}^{k_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{k_{3}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\eta\nabla^{{k_{4}}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\alpha||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}|u^{\prime}|\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}\Gamma}{a}\Gamma a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\Gamma^{2}\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{2}},

(10.59)

while the worst matter term (the one that contains
T_{44}
that has the minimum decay among the stress-energy components) is controlled by

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{j}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=j\\
i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i\\
k_{1}+k_{2}=j_{3}\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{j_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{j_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},|u^{\prime}|\underline{\omega})^{i_{2}}\not{\nabla}^{k_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{4A},\not{\nabla}T_{44})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}|u^{\prime}|\Gamma\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{4}}.

(10.60)

Collecting, finally, all the terms, we estimate

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{j}{2}}Q_{i,j}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{j}\tilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\Gamma\mathcal{R}+1.

(10.61)

Therefore we obtain

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\tilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\tilde{\beta}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}},\end{split}

(10.62)

which completes the proof for
\mathcal{D}=|u|\not{\nabla}_{3}
and
\mathcal{D}=a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla}
(their joint appearance).

\square

Proposition 10.4

Let the hypotheses of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
) be in force on the spacetime region under consideration.
Consider an ordered pair of null curvature components

(\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2})\in\big\{\,(\widetilde{\beta},(\rho,\sigma)),\;((\rho,\sigma),\tilde{\beta}),\;(\tilde{\beta},\underline{\alpha})\,\big\}.

Let
\mathcal{D}\in\big\{\,|u|\,\not{\nabla}_{3},\,a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla},\,\not{\nabla}_{4}\,\big\}
denote one of the admissible commutation operators introduced in Section
3.5
, and let
i
be an integer satisfying
0\leq i\leq 2
.

Then the following
scale-invariant
L^{2}
energy inequality
holds:

\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\;+\;\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\;\;\leq\;\;\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\;+\;\lVert{\mathcal{D}^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\;+\;\frac{C}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.

(10.63)

In particular, for the uncommuted case
i=0
, (
10.63
) reads

\lVert{\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\;+\;\lVert{\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\;\;\leq\;\;\lVert{\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\;+\;\lVert{\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\;+\;\frac{C}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.

(10.64)

Here:

•

\lVert{\cdot}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}
and
\lVert{\cdot}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}
denote the scale-invariant
L^{2}
-flux norms along the outgoing and incoming null hypersurfaces
H_{u}
and
\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}
, respectively.

•

\mathcal{D}^{i}
denotes the
i
-fold composition of
\mathcal{D}
.

Proof.

The schematic equations for
\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2}
are:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\Psi_{1}+\left(\frac{1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1})\right)\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\Psi_{1}-\mathcal{D}\Psi_{2}=

\displaystyle(\psi,\hat{\chi})\Psi+(\not{\nabla}T_{43},\not{\nabla}T_{3B},\not{\nabla}T_{33},\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{A3},\not{\nabla}_{3}T_{43},\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{33},\not{\nabla}_{3}T_{A3})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\psi,\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})(T_{43},T_{A3},T_{33}),

(10.65)

\begin{split}\not{\nabla}_{4}\Psi_{2}-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}\Psi_{1}=(\psi,\underline{\hat{\chi}})(\Psi_{u},\alpha)+(\not{\nabla}T_{4A},\not{\nabla}_{3}T_{44},\not{\nabla}_{4}T_{43},\not{\nabla}T_{43},\not{\nabla}_{3}T_{4A},\not{\nabla}_{3}T_{AB},\not{\nabla}_{A}T_{3B})\\
+(\psi,\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})(T_{4A},T_{44},T_{43},T_{AB},T_{A3})\end{split}

(10.66)

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives, for
\Psi_{1}
, we have:

\begin{split}&\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}+\left(\frac{i+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1})\right)\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}-\mathcal{D}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2}\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{2}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{2}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(T_{43},T_{3B},T_{33})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{4}(T_{A3},T_{33})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{3}(T_{43},T_{A3})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(T_{4A},T_{43})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(T_{4A},T_{43})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}T_{3A}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}:=P_{i}.\end{split}

(10.67)

Analogously, for
\Psi_{2}
, we have

\begin{split}&\not{\nabla}_{4}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2}-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\Psi_{1},\alpha)\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(T_{4A},T_{3A},T_{43})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(T_{4A},T_{3A},T_{43})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{4}(T_{43})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{3}(T_{44},T_{4A},T_{AB})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{2}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\operatorname{tr}\chi)\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{2}\\
&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}T_{4A}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{2}:=Q_{i}.\end{split}

(10.68)

Making use of Proposition
10.2
once again, we arrive at

\begin{split}&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(10.69)

For the first spacetime integral in the above, we estimate

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\cdot\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.\end{split}

(10.70)

For the first term:

\begin{split}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(10.71)

For the rest of the terms, we estimate using the same philosophy as appropriate. There holds

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma(R+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(10.72)

Next we estimate

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3+1}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\Gamma[\eta,\underline{\eta}]\Gamma[\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}]\Gamma[\Psi_{1}]\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}(\tilde{\beta},\tilde{\underline{\beta}})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot 1\cdot(\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+1)\\
\lesssim&(\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+1)^{2}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(10.73)

Now we estimate the source terms (we only provide the worst estimates)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(T_{43},T_{33},T_{A3}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a\mathcal{V}}{a^{\frac{5}{2}}|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{a\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{a^{2}|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{2}}+\frac{\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{3}}\lesssim 1,

(10.74)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{4}(T_{A3},T_{33})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a\mathcal{V}}{a^{\frac{5}{2}}|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{a\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{a^{2}|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{2}}+\frac{\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{|u|^{3}}\lesssim 1,

(10.75)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{3}(T_{43},T_{A3})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}a\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a^{2}|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\frac{a\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a|u|}+\frac{\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1,

(10.76)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(T_{4A},T_{43})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}\Gamma}{a}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}|u^{\prime}|}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|}\lesssim 1,

(10.77)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}T_{3A}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\Gamma\mathcal{V}\mathcal{R}}{a^{2}|u^{\prime}|}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma\mathcal{V}\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{4}}\lesssim 1.

(10.78)

For the second and last one, a double application of Hölder’s inequality yields

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{2}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
\lesssim&\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{2}\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{2}\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\end{split}

(10.79)

Define
B:=\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}
. The most important point to note here is that the term
Q_{i}
contains the Vlasov source terms and it appears as the spacetime integral and therefore, we can control the Vlasov on
H
. We can then estimate
B
term by term as follows. The first term in
B
is estimated as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\psi_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i-1}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i-1}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1\\
i_{3}<i-2\end{subarray}}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1,

(10.80)

where in the first integral we estimate
\psi_{g}
in
L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
and
\not{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}
in the hypersurface norm
L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})
. In the second integral, we estimate
\not{\nabla}\psi_{g}
in
L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
and
\not{\nabla}^{i-1}\Psi_{1}
in
L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
and subsequent codimension-1 trace inequality on
H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})}
. The third and fourth integrals are straightforward to control. For the second term in
B
is estimated as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\Psi,\alpha)}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+1\right)^{2}\lesssim 1,

(10.81)

where we have used energy estimates on
\alpha
from proposition
10.3
. Now we estimate the source terms. Here we notice that
i+1=3
derivatives of Vlasov appear, which are controlled by means of the derivative estimates for Vlasov (proposition
9.6
)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(T_{4A},T_{3A},T_{43})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{2}\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a^{4}|u|^{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{2}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{a^{3}|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a|u|^{3}}+\frac{\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{|u|^{5}}\lesssim 1.

(10.82)

We estimate the next term (lower order derivatives on the vlasov terms)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi})\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(T_{4A},T_{3A},T_{43})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{2}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\Gamma|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{a^{2}}\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a^{3}|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{V}\Gamma}{a^{2}|u|}\lesssim 1.

(10.83)

The next

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{4}(T_{43})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{2}\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a^{4}|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{2}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{a^{3}|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a|u|^{3}}+\frac{\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{5}}\lesssim 1.

(10.84)

The next term is estimated as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\not{\nabla}_{3}(T_{44},T_{4A},T_{AB})}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{2}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}{a^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a^{2}|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{2}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}{a^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{a|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{V}}{a|u|}+\frac{\Gamma\mathcal{V}}{|u|^{3}}\lesssim 1,

(10.85)

and finally

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}T_{4A}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(10.86)

Here, we use
L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})-L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})-L^{4}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
type estimates together with the Sobolev embedding on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
to yield

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\not{\nabla}^{i_{1}}\psi^{i_{2}+1}_{g}\not{\nabla}^{i_{3}}T_{4A}\not{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{2}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{\Gamma\mathcal{V}\mathcal{R}}{a}\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\Gamma\mathcal{V}\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{5}}\lesssim 1.

(10.87)

The rest of the terms can also be bounded above by
1
, using the same approach. We finally arrive at an estimate of the form

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{\mathcal{L}^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&1+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\not{\nabla})^{i}\Psi_{2}\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(10.88)

From here, collecting all the terms together and using Gronwall’s inequality, we arrive at the desired result. The second energy estimate follows exactly in a similar fashion to the previous proposition.

\square

11
Dynamical Formation of a Trapped Surface

As is standard knowledge by now, the formation argument itself is a rather straightforward ODE argument. The core of it rests on obtaining estimates for certain inhomogeneities on the right-hand side of the Raychaudhuri equation, as we shall see below.

We begin with
\hat{\chi}
. Recall the null structure equation

\not{\nabla}_{3}\hat{\chi}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}+\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\hat{\chi}=\not{\nabla}\hat{\otimes}\eta-\operatorname{tr}\chi\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta+\hat{\not{T}}.

Contracting with
\hat{\chi}
once, we obtain

\frac{1}{2}\not{\nabla}_{3}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\underline{\omega}\hskip 1.42262pt\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}=\hat{\chi}\cdot\big(\not{\nabla}\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta+\hat{\not{T}}\big).

(11.1)

Using the fact that
\underline{\omega}=2\hskip 1.42262pt\Omega^{-1}\hskip 1.42262pt\not{\nabla}_{3}\Omega
, we can rewrite the above as

\not{\nabla}_{3}\big(\Omega^{4}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}\big)+\Omega^{4}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}=2\hskip 1.42262pt\Omega^{4}\hat{\chi}\cdot\big(\not{\nabla}\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta+\hat{\not{T}}\big).

(11.2)

Using the fact that
\not{\nabla}_{3}=\partial_{u}+b^{A}\partial_{\theta^{A}}
, we obtain

\frac{\partial}{\partial u}(\Omega^{4}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2})+\Omega^{4}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}=2\hskip 1.42262pt\Omega^{4}\hat{\chi}\cdot\big(\not{\nabla}\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta+\hat{\not{T}}\big)-b^{A}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta^{A}}(\Omega^{4}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}).

(11.3)

Substituting

\Omega^{4}\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}=\Omega^{4}\big(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\big)-(\Omega^{4}-1)\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}-\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert},

we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u}(\Omega^{4}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2})-\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\Omega^{4}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\hskip 1.42262pt2\hskip 1.42262pt\Omega^{4}\hat{\chi}\cdot\big(\not{\nabla}\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta+\hat{\not{T}}\big)-b^{A}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta^{A}}(\Omega^{4}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2})

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\Omega^{4}\big(\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\big)\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+(\Omega^{4}-1)\cdot\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\cdot\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}.

(11.4)

We therefore have the following equation for
\hat{\chi}
:

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\big(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{4}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}\big)=

\displaystyle 2\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{4}\hat{\chi}\cdot(\not{\nabla}\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\chi\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta+\hat{\not{T}}\big)-\lvert u\rvert^{2}b^{A}\partial_{\theta^{A}}(\Omega^{4}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2})

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\lvert u\rvert^{2}\big(\operatorname{tr}\chi+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\big)(\Omega^{4}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2})+\lvert u\rvert^{2}(\Omega^{4}-1)\cdot\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\cdot\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}.

(11.5)

Using the a priori estimates obtained in the previous sections, the integral from
u_{\infty}
to
u
of the right-hand side of
(
11.5
) is controllable as follows (see, also,
[
3
]
):

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\big\lvert\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\big(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{4}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}\big)\big\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}.

(11.6)

Recalling the bounds on
\lVert\Omega^{-1}-1\rVert
from Proposition
3.1
, together with the structure equations relating the expression
{\partial}_{u}\Omega=\not{\nabla}_{3}\Omega-b^{A}\partial_{\theta^{A}}\Omega
to
\underline{\omega},\eta,\underline{\eta}
, we obtain

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\big\lvert\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\big(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}\big)\big\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}.

(11.7)

Thus, there holds

\lvert u\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}(u,\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\geq\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}(u_{\infty},\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(11.8)

Integrating from 0 to 1 with respect to
\underline{u}
, we have

\int_{0}^{1}\lvert u\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}(u,\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\geq\int_{0}^{1}\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}(u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(11.9)

For
\not{T}_{44}
we have

\partial_{u}\not{T}_{44}=\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{T}_{44}-b^{A}e_{A}\not{T}_{44}.

(11.10)

As such, after using the estimates on the Vlasov matter obtained in Sections
9.2
-
9.4
, together with the co-dimension one trace inequality from Proposition
3.11
, we have

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\lvert\partial_{u}(\lvert u\rvert\not{T}_{44})\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\lvert\lvert u\rvert(\not{\nabla}_{3}\not{T}_{44}-b^{A}e_{A}(\not{T}_{44}))-\not{T}_{44}\rvert\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}-\frac{a}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert},

(11.11)

which translates to

\int_{0}^{1}\lvert u\rvert\not{T}_{44}(u,\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\geq\int_{0}^{1}\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert\not{T}_{44}(u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}-\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(11.12)

Putting (
11.9
) and (
11.12
) together, we arrive at

\int_{0}^{1}\big(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\lvert u\rvert\not{T}_{44}\big)(u,\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\geq\int_{0}^{1}\big(\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert\not{T}_{44}\big)(u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(11.13)

We thus have, for
u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-\frac{a}{4},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\big(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\lvert u\rvert\not{T}_{44}\big)(u,\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\geq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\big(\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert\not{T}_{44}\big)(u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}-\frac{2a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle a-\frac{2a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\geq a-\frac{8a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{a}\geq\frac{3a}{4}.

(11.14)

Plugging in

u=-\frac{a}{4},
we have

(a/4)^{2}\bigg[\int_{0}^{1}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\frac{4}{a}\not{T}_{44}\bigg]du^{{}^{\prime}}\geq\frac{3a}{4},

(11.15)

which translates to

\int_{0}^{1}\big(\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\not{T}_{44}\big)(-\frac{a}{4},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\geq\int_{0}^{1}\big(\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\frac{4}{a}\not{T}_{44}\big)(-\frac{a}{4},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\geq\frac{12}{a}.

(11.16)

We now consider the Raychaudhuri equation

\not{\nabla}_{4}\operatorname{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\chi)^{2}=-\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}-\not{T}_{44}.

Keeping in mind that
e_{4}=\frac{1}{\Omega^{2}}\partial_{\underline{u}},
we have

\frac{\partial}{\partial\underline{u}}\operatorname{tr}\chi=\Omega^{2}\big(-\frac{1}{2}(\operatorname{tr}\chi)^{2}-\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}-\not{T}_{44}\big).

(11.17)

As such, we have

\displaystyle\lvert\operatorname{tr}\chi\rvert(-\frac{a}{4},1,\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\leq\lvert\operatorname{tr}\chi\rvert(-\frac{a}{4},0,\theta^{1},\theta^{2})-\int_{0}^{1}\Omega^{2}\big(\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\not{T}_{44}\big)(-\frac{a}{4},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8}{a}-\frac{12}{a}-\int_{0}^{1}(\Omega^{2}-1)\big(\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\not{g}}^{2}+\not{T}_{44}\big)(-\frac{a}{4},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq\frac{8}{a}-\frac{10}{a}\leq\frac{-2}{a}<0.

(11.18)

Here we have used that on
\{\underline{u}=0\},
the metric is precisely Minkowski and hence

\operatorname{tr}\chi(-\frac{a}{4},0,\theta^{1},\theta^{2})=\frac{2}{\frac{a}{4}}=\frac{8}{a}.

For
\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}
, we have shown the bound

\lVert\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(11.19)

which implies

\operatorname{tr}\underline{\chi}(-\frac{a}{4},1,\theta^{1},\theta^{2})<0,\hskip 5.69054pt\text{for all}(\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\in\mathbb{S}^{2}.

(11.20)

It follows that
S_{-\frac{a}{4},1}
is a trapped surface. This completes the proof.
